### [32] [FPScan: An Automated Constraint-Based Analyzer for Floating-Point Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07492)
*Julien Bortolussi,Dorra Ben Khalifa,Pierre-Loïc Garoche*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FPScan是一个通过静态抽象解释推断量级并构造一阶约束、借助SMT求解器检测浮点程序中吸收和灾难性相消的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多程序员缺乏数值分析背景，容易在浮点运算中遇到吸收和灾难性相消等数值陷阱，现有工具很少显式检测这些关键异常。

Method: 1) 使用自定义静态分析（基于抽象解释）推断程序变量的数量级；2) 基于这些数量级构造一阶约束以建模误差传播与数值精度；3) 使用通用SMT求解器判断是否存在吸收或相消。

Result: 在FPBench上进行实验证明FPScan可检测出目标数值陷阱，并就健全性与分析时间与现有方法进行了比较，显示出可接受的检测能力与性能折衷。

Conclusion: FPScan能够形式化定义并检测吸收与灾难性相消，通过结合量级推断与SMT求解在FPBench上展示了有效性，并与现有工具在健全性与分析时间上进行了比较。

Abstract: Writing error-free floating-point programs is a challenging task, especially for programmers who lack a strong background in numerical analysis and rounding-error propagation. State-of-the-art techniques typically aim to bound such errors using static or dynamic analysis. However, only a few tools explicitly address critical floating-point pitfalls such as absorption and catastrophic cancellation. These anomalies represent situations in which rounding errors are significantly amplified, causing the semantics of the finite-precision computation to deviate substantially from the real-number semantics. In this article, we present FPScan, a novel tool to formally define and detect both catastrophic cancellation and absorption in floating-point programs. Our approach starts with a custom static analyzer based on abstract interpretation to infer the order of magnitude of all program variables. This magnitude information is then used to build a set of first-order constraints that model error propagation and numerical precision within the program. Finally, we employ an off-the-shelf SMT solver to determine whether the program exhibits any of these critical numerical pitfalls. Experiments were conducted on FPBench, a well-known benchmark suite of floating-point programs, to evaluate the effectiveness of our tool. We also present a comparison with state-of-the-art tools regarding soundness and analysis time.

</details>


### [33] ["We Permit the Use of AI, but [...]": The Landscape of AI Policies in Popular Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07542)
*Andre Hora,Romain Robbes,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开源项目普遍允许AI辅助代码贡献但附带条件，披露与责任条款参差不齐，存在多种反制策略，且政策迅速演化，论文提供了基线数据与标注语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI工具在代码贡献中的流行，开源社区需要新的治理工具以规范AI使用、保障代码质量并明确责任，作者旨在描绘这一新兴治理实践的现状并提供可供研究与维护者参考的标注语料。

Method: 收集281份AI贡献政策并按六个维度手工分类，同时对92份专门的AI政策文件进行时序追踪，以回答四个研究问题（AI使用许可、披露实践、对AI“垃圾产出”的反制措施、政策演化）。

Result: 发现主要结论包括：83.3%政策允许或鼓励使用AI；67.3%要求高水平人工参与；43.4%明确责任分配；48.8%要求披露，但披露形式与内容差异大；识别出针对PR、用户和自主代理的十种反制措施；约半数专门政策在创建后已有修订。

Conclusion: 开源社群正迅速采用AI贡献政策，绝大多数政策允许或鼓励使用AI，但普遍附带对人工参与、责任归属和披露的要求；政策类型多样且正在快速演进。

Abstract: Open source communities are converging on a new governance artifact: the AI contribution policy. These policies barely existed a few months ago and are now being written and adopted. We analyzed 281 AI contribution policies, and manually classified them along the six dimensions; to study how policies change, we also tracked 92 dedicated AI policy files over time. We answer four research questions on (1) AI usage allowance, (2) AI disclosure practices, (3) AI slop countermeasures, and (4) AI policy evolution. We find that, first, permission is the norm rather than the exception: 83.3% of policies permit or encourage AI in code contributions. But permission comes with conditions, as 67.3% require a high level of human involvement and 43.4% assign accountability. Second, AI disclosure is required by 48.8% of policies, most often in pull request descriptions and commit messages, but what must be disclosed varies widely. Third, we identify ten countermeasures against AI slop, targeting pull requests, users, and autonomous agents. Finally, policies are not static: half of the dedicated AI policy files have already been revised since creation. Our results give maintainers and researchers a baseline and a labeled corpus for studying the impact of AI policies.

</details>


### [34] [RefVerifier: Semi-Automated Reference Claim Verification for Scientific Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07652)
*Stefania Mocan,Florian Angermeir,Mark Kreitz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出RefVerifier，自动检索与定位引文证据以辅助审稿，展现了良好句子检测与证据定位性能，但开放获取解析率和端到端裁定准确率仍有提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着投稿量激增，审稿人时间受限，全面验证引文支持的陈述成本高昂；为避免未经证实的陈述与语义漂移在文献中传播，提出一个半自动化工具以降低审稿时的证据核查负担。

Method: 管线包括：1) 提取带引用的句子；2) 校验参考文献元数据与学术数据库匹配；3) 解析引用以检索开放获取全文PDF；4) 在检索到的文档中定位与句子相关的证据段落；5) 基于定位结果生成裁定及自然语言解释。

Result: RefVerifier是一种面向学术同行评审的半自动化“引文受限”参考验证原型，能够从手稿中提取带引文句子、校验书目元数据、将引用解析到开放获取PDF、定位证据段落并生成带自然语言解释的裁定。主要实验结果：断言检测F1=0.990；开放获取解析率57.6%；摘要证据定位命中率98%，全文定位命中率68%；端到端裁定准确率71%。

Conclusion: RefVerifier显示出在自动化证据检索与定位方面的可行性，可作为审稿者的工具辅佐。但需要改进引用解析覆盖率、进一步验证裁定可靠性并提供更详尽的错误分析与可复现资源。

Abstract: As software engineering research submission counts surge, peer reviewers face severe time constraints, making systematic verification of citation-supported claims prohibitively expensive. Consequently, unsubstantiated claims and semantic drift can propagate undetected across scientific literature. Existing approaches such as fact-checking and retrieval-augmented generation tools operate on open-domain web data or evaluate claims in isolation without processing complete manuscripts. To address this gap, we present RefVerifier, a semi-automated, citation-bounded reference verification prototype designed to support in academic peer review. RefVerifier extracts citation-bearing sentences from manuscripts, checks bibliography metadata against scholarly databases, resolves references to full-text open-access PDFs, localizes relevant evidence passages, and generates verdicts with natural language explanations. Evaluating RefVerifier on public benchmarks shows claim detection at an F1 score of 0.990, open-access resolution of 57.6% of references, and evidence localization with a hit rate of 98% on abstracts and 68% on complete cited papers. In an end-to-end test with eight manuscripts, RefVerifier achieves a verdict accuracy of 71%. By automating document retrieval and evidence localization while preserving reviewer oversight, RefVerifier provides first indicators for the feasibility of semi-automated integrity checks in scholarly publishing.

</details>


### [35] [CodeTD: Topology of Attention Detects Hallucinations in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07779)
*Daria Voronkova,Ilya Trofimov,Anton Dmitriev,Eduard Tulchinskii,Evgeny Burnaev,Serguei Barannikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用注意力图的拓扑特征进行预执行代码正确性评估的方法 CodeTD，实验证明在多基准、多语言、多模型设置下优于现有基线且具备迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 代码助手的普及，需要在执行前自动评估生成代码的正确性以防止幻觉导致错误或安全漏洞。该工作旨在通过分析 Code LLM 的注意力图谱的拓扑特征，量化提示与生成代码之间的不匹配，从而进行预执行的正确性评估。

Method: 对 Code LLM 的注意力矩阵/图进行拓扑数据分析（如持久同调等），提取描述注意力结构的拓扑特征，并基于这些特征训练判别器以预测生成代码是否正确或存在重大错误。通过在多个基准与模型上验证该判别器，评估其性能与迁移性。

Result: 提出 CodeTD：基于拓扑数据分析（TDA）对注意力图进行表征的首个预执行代码正确性评估方法。在 HumanEval、MBPP、BigCodeBench、MultiPL-E 等基准、5 种编程语言和 10 个最高 34B 参数的模型上进行实验，结果显示 CodeTD 超越近期基线方法，并具有跨基准的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 拓扑特征的注意力图表征能有效量化提示与生成之间的不匹配，从而对 Code LLM 的输出做出可靠的预执行正确性判断；该方法在多种数据集与模型上表现良好，表明 TDA 在代码评估任务上的潜力。

Abstract: As AI-code assistant tools become widespread, automatic assessment of the correctness of generated code becomes a significant challenge. Code LLMs are prone to hallucinations, which may lead to code that does not solve the required problem, or even to code with severe security vulnerabilities. In this paper, we introduce CodeTD -- the first approach to pre-execution assessment of code correctness based on topological data analysis (TDA) of Code LLMs' attention maps. Our method quantifies prompt-generation mismatch using topological patterns of attention maps. We carry out experiments with common benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, BigCodeBench, MultiPL-E), 5 programming languages and 10 Code LLMs of size up to 34B parameters. The experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms recent baselines. Moreover, CodeTD is transferable between coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [36] [You can contribute if you... An Empirical Framework of AI Contribution Policies in OSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07919)
*Gregorio Robles,Daniel M. German*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实证分析开源项目关于AI辅助贡献的政策，发现这些政策旨在保护维护者注意力、维护问责、维持有意义的审查互动、处理法律与质量问题，并保留新手学习路径；作者据此提出了“AI贡献治理框架”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI降低了代码与文档生产成本，扩大了参与机会，但也扰动了维护者评估贡献者努力、能力与问责的传统方式，促使项目制定政策以规范AI介入的贡献。

Method: 对开源项目的AI相关贡献政策进行文献/文档式的实证分析，剖析政策背后的理由、规则与期望，总结出常见关注点与治理机制，并整合为AI贡献治理框架。

Result: 识别出政策试图解决的五类问题：保护维护者注意力、维护问责制、维持审查互动的意义、应对法律与质量顾虑、保留新手学习路径；并据此提出一个组织这些关注点与治理手段的框架，帮助社区制定政策并为研究提供共同词汇。

Conclusion: AI正在改变开源贡献的生产方式，项目为了应对注意力稀缺、能力与责任评估困难、法律与质量风险以及新手学习受损等问题，制定了多样化的AI贡献政策；提出的治理框架为社区制定政策与研究者建立分析范式提供了工具。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is reshaping open source software (OSS) contribution by lowering the cost of producing code, documentation, issue reports, and review interactions. This creates opportunities for broader participation, but also disrupts how maintainers assess contributor effort, competence, and accountability. In response, OSS projects are beginning to regulate AI-mediated contribution through contribution guidelines and other project documentation. This paper presents an empirical study of these emerging policies. We analyze project policies on AI-mediated contributions by evaluating their underlying rationales, rules, and expectations. Our analysis shows that these policies seek to protect scarce maintainer attention, preserve accountability, sustain meaningful review interactions, address legal and quality concerns, and maintain pathways for newcomer learning. Based on these findings, we introduce the AI Contribution Governance Framework, which organizes recurring concerns and governance mechanisms across projects. The framework helps OSS communities develop AI contribution policies and provides researchers with a vocabulary for studying how AI is changing collaborative software production.

</details>


### [37] [Service Health Engineering for Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08020)
*Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 引入“服务健康工程”，以端到端用户旅程为中心，结合传统可靠性实践与人机协同的 AI 报告体系，揭示静默失败并改进运维决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分布式系统的服务健康评估多基于组件仪表盘，忽视端到端用户结果，导致静默失败与未完成的异步工作难以被发现与修复。

Method: 提出服务健康工程作为学科，结合服务承诺、SLI/SLO、看门狗、事件度量、弹性测试和周度服务健康评审，并演示了一个由人类审阅、AI 辅助的报告架构，用文档审批工作流作为运行示例。

Result: 通过将遥测、工作流完成率、依赖行为、运维准备度与恢复验证连接，能够更准确地衡量服务是否满足用户承诺，并用 AI 辅助组装证据以提高效率，同时保留人类决策权以防止自动化误判。

Conclusion: 将可靠性实践围绕用户旅程是否按承诺完成进行整合，可以发现静默失败和滞留的异步工作，从而提升服务健康的可操作性。

Abstract: Distributed systems support many critical business workflows, but service health is often judged through component dashboards rather than through end-to-end user outcomes. This article presents service health engineering as a practical reliability discipline that connects telemetry, workflow completion, dependency behavior, operational readiness, and recovery validation. Using a document approval workflow as a running example, it describes how service promises, service-level indicators and objectives, watchdogs, incident measures, resiliency testing, and weekly service-health reviews can reveal silent failures and stranded asynchronous work. It also presents a human-reviewed, AI-assisted reporting architecture for assembling service-health evidence without making AI an autonomous decision-maker. The approach brings established reliability practices together around whether user journeys complete as promised.

</details>


### [38] [Agent ATO: Visualizing Agent Interaction Timelines from Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08301)
*Takuto Kawamoto,Yoshiki Hig,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Agent ATO 将控制台日志解析成按操作类型标注的交互时间线，帮助开发者可视化并比较 AI 编码代理的行为轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码代理逐渐进入开发流程，但仅通过最终代码难以判定代理决策与过程；控制台日志记录了丰富的交互与 token 使用，需将其转为可检视的轨迹以便开发者检视与比较。

Method: 从控制台日志中解析代理命令与工具使用，按类型（搜索／读文件／编辑／执行等）分类交互事件，并以总体与若干有针对性的过滤时间线（文件发现、文件阅读、文件编辑、执行）呈现，同时保留上下文信息。

Result: 实现了 Agent ATO 工具并用两次修复任务的运行示例演示其如何帮助检查与比较代理动作；提出未来扩展到更多代理、任务与环境，并计划评估是否降低比较轨迹所需的人工成本。

Conclusion: Agent ATO 提供了一种从控制台日志中提取并可视化 AI 编码代理交互轨迹的方法，使开发者能够比仅看最终代码更直观地理解代理在任务中的行为。

Abstract: AI coding agents are becoming part of developers' workflows, but their behavior is difficult to understand from final code changes alone. During a task, agents interact with software repositories through sequences of actions such as searching for files, reading code, editing programs, and running tests or build commands. These interactions, together with token usage, are often recorded in console logs, but raw logs are difficult for developers to inspect. In this paper, we propose Agent ATO (Agentic Trajectory Observer), a tool for visualizing AI coding agent interaction timelines from console logs. Agent ATO extracts agent interactions, classifies them by command or tool type, and visualizes them as timelines. In addition to an all-interaction timeline, Agent ATO provides filtered timelines that emphasize file discovery, file reading, file editing, and execution while preserving surrounding context. We illustrate how Agent ATO may help developers inspect and compare agent actions using selected runs from two repair tasks. Future work will apply Agent ATO to more agents, tasks, and development environments, and will evaluate whether it reduces the effort needed to compare trajectories.

</details>


### [39] [AttnCompress: Dynamic Attention-Guided Trajectory Compression for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08318)
*Zhengran Zeng,Yixin Li,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AttnCompress: an attention-guided, structure-aware dynamic compression framework that preserves syntactic/semantic evidence in ASE trajectories, improving performance and efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long ASE agent trajectories cause context window and cost bottlenecks; prior static pruning and coarse-grained approaches fail to maintain semantic dependencies and syntactic details needed for software engineering tasks.

Method: Dynamic attention-guided compression combining (1) structure-aware segmentation using perplexity spikes, (2) relevance estimation via proxy attention weights to score historical blocks, and (3) a dynamic rolling window for re-evaluation and recall of past context.

Result: On SWE-Bench-Verified and Multi-SWE-Bench AttnCompress achieves a 53.17% pass rate, outperforms prior baselines, reduces token consumption by 21.6% and total costs by 33.6%.

Conclusion: AttnCompress effectively preserves critical task evidence while compressing agent interaction trajectories, improving task pass rates and reducing token usage and total cost; it's model-agnostic and generalizes across programming languages.

Abstract: The transition from human-centric assistance to Autonomous Software Engineering (ASE) agents has enabled the resolution of complex real-world SE tasks. However, the trial-and-error nature of these agents generates lengthy interaction trajectories, creating severe bottlenecks in terms of context window limits and cost. While context compression offers a potential remedy, prior approaches suffer from static pruning strategies and granularity mismatches, often failing to preserve the semantic dependencies and syntactic details crucial for SE tasks. To strictly preserve critical task evidence while reducing context length, we introduce AttnCompress, a dynamic attention-guided trajectory compression framework. Unlike existing approaches, AttnCompress bridges the gap between semantic integrity and dynamic adaptability through three key mechanisms: (1) structure-aware segmentation via perplexity (PPL) spikes to preserve the syntactic structure of code and logs; (2) relevance estimation using proxy attention weights to quantify the precise relevance of historical blocks to the agent's current reasoning; and (3) a dynamic rolling window to re-evaluate and recall historical context as the task evolves. Extensive evaluation on SWE-Bench-Verified and Multi-SWE-Bench demonstrates that AttnCompress achieves a pass rate of 53.17%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art baselines while reducing token consumption by 21.6% and total costs by 33.6%. The framework proves to be model-agnostic and generalizes effectively across diverse programming languages.

</details>


### [40] [Tool Retrievers Are Underestimated: Annotation Expansion Reveals True Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08327)
*Yanyu Zhu,Chenheng Zhang,Shaoshen Chen,Hoilam Pao,Yufei zhang,Jiajun Chai,Dongnian Wang,Zhaoyu Hu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Hai-Tao Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolEX自动扩展基准中的“正确”工具组合以包含功能等价的替代方案，揭示现有一对一标注系统性低估检索器并夸大部分微调收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界的工具库中，多个工具或工具组合可实现相同功能，导致查询到工具的映射是多对多的，但现有基准通常仅给出单一“正确”组合，从而将等效但不同的组合判为错误，低估检索器表现。

Method: 提出ToolEX框架，自动发现并标注与基准标注工具组合功能等价的替代组合；将该框架应用于现有基准（Tool-DE和SkillRet），并用扩展后的基准（ToolEQ）重新评估若干检索器与微调变体，以量化原始一对一标注对评估结果的低估与微调增益的虚假部分。

Result: 在Tool-DE上，ToolEX发现67.9%的子查询存在等价替代，平均将每个查询的有效组合数从1扩展到5.3；在扩展基准ToolEQ上重新评估显示检索器指标显著上升，说明一对一标注系统性低估检索器性能；此外报告30–47%的微调带来的性能提升是评估伪影。对SkillRet的同样处理表明问题不仅限于工具检索。

Conclusion: 一对一的基准标注在工具/技能检索任务中有系统性偏差，需用等价扩展、集合化评估指标和更严格的等价验证以获得更真实的检索器性能评估；ToolEX提供了可行的自动化扩展方法并证明了其在现有基准上的有效性。

Abstract: In open-world scenarios with massive and evolving tool repositories, tool-augmented large language models rely on a retriever to surface relevant tools for a given query. Because such repositories often contain many tools that implement the same functionality, a single query can often be resolved by several distinct but functionally equivalent tool combinations, making the natural query-to-tool mapping inherently one-to-many. However, existing tool retrieval benchmarks annotate each query with a single relevant tool combination, collapsing this one-to-many mapping into a rigid one-to-one annotation and causing valid retrieved tools to be misjudged as failures. To address this, we propose ToolEX (Tool Equivalent eXpansion), a framework that automatically discovers and annotates the tool combinations functionally equivalent to the labeled ones. Applied to the 7,360-query Tool-DE benchmark, ToolEX finds that 67.9% of sub-queries admit equivalent alternatives, expanding the singular ground truth to an average of 5.3 valid combinations per query. Using the expanded benchmark ToolEQ, we re-evaluate eight base retrievers and two fine-tuned variants; metrics on ToolEQ rise substantially over Tool-DE, showing that one-to-one annotation systematically underestimates retrievers and that 30--47% of the reported fine-tuning gain is an evaluation artifact rather than genuine improvement. Applying the same pipeline to skill retrieval on SkillRet further confirms that the one-to-one problem extends beyond tool retrieval.

</details>


### [41] [RepoNav: From Snippet Retrieval to File-Centered Repository Navigation for Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08355)
*Hongzheng Chai,Jiakun Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoNav把检索结果重组为文件中心的导航视图，通过提供候选符号和简要结构线索，帮助LLM代理更准确地在大代码库中定位目标函数，从而改进函数级检索与仓库级问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索工具返回的是一列表孤立代码片段，缺乏文件内结构信息，导致LLM代理难以在同一文件中区分目标函数与语义相近的候选者。

Method: 在检索后对片段进行文件聚合和结构化呈现，向代理展示每个文件的紧凑结构线索和候选目标符号，支持按需浏览文件结构并比较同文件下的相似符号；通过对比实验和消融研究评估其作用。

Result: 在LocBench及一个仓库级问答基准上，RepoNav在不同模型上均提升了函数级定位表现并缩小了文件到函数的性能差距；消融实验表明结构化组织是关键贡献。

Conclusion: RepoNav通过将检索到的代码片段按文件为中心组织成导航脚手架，显著提高了LLM代理在仓库级任务中的函数定位能力，主要增益来自于对证据的结构化组织而非单纯展示更多文件结构。

Abstract: Solving repository-level code tasks requires LLM-based agents to use code search tools to navigate large codebases and identify a small set of relevant files and functions. However, current retrieval tools typically return flat lists of isolated code snippets: such lists can surface relevant files, but provide insufficient structure for agents to distinguish the target function from semantically similar alternatives in the same file. We introduce RepoNav, a lightweight post-retrieval interface that reorganizes retrieved snippets into a file-centered navigation scaffold. By presenting compact structural cues and candidate targets, this scaffold guides on-demand file-structure browsing, helping agents compare sibling symbols before selecting a target function. Across diverse models on LocBench, RepoNav improves function-level localization and narrows the file-to-function gap. Controlled ablations demonstrate that these gains come from structured evidence organization rather than simply exposing additional file structure, and the approach also improves performance on a repository-level question-answering benchmark.

</details>


### [42] [Authority Is Not a String: A Capability-Scoped Harness for Prompt-Injection-Resistant Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08371)
*Dimitrios Stamatios Bouras,Yihan Dai,Sergey Mechtaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CapScope通过在代理外维护并校验细粒度能力来限制间接提示注入，显著降低了被注入执行的概率，同时对修复成功率影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码代理在沙箱内对资源拥有环境权限（ambient authority），使得把恶意指令放入仓库文件或工具输出的间接提示注入能诱导代理执行未被请求的操作。作者希望在不依赖模型理解或识别恶意文本的前提下，制约工具使用以防止此类注入。

Method: 在Pi编码代理上实现了一个harness级别的授权机制：在读取仓库或工具输出前，从可信输入推导任务级的权限上限（authority ceiling），为每个子代理在模型上下文外分配类型化的能力集，且在每次工具调用时校验该子代理是否拥有对应能力。

Result: 在一个包含5个Python任务、5种注入面、4种授权条件和每格3次试验（共300次运行）的修复工作流评估中，注入效果在ambient-authority和global-policy基线下分别在33–47/75次和75次（似乎指示基线更易受攻击）执行成功；而CapScope仅在3/75次执行成功。CapScope完成68/75次修复，基线完成68–72/75次，表明安全增强对任务完成率影响有限。

Conclusion: CapScope有效地限制了间接提示注入带来的滥用——在不要求模型识别恶意文本的前提下，通过在代理外部分配和校验能力，从而显著降低了注入成功率，同时对修复任务的完成率影响甚微。

Abstract: Coding agents use system-level tools to read files, execute commands, and modify source code. Within the agent's sandbox, these tools often carry ambient authority: naming a resource is sufficient to act on it. Indirect prompt injection exploits this authority by placing instructions in repository files or tool output that cause the agent to perform actions the user did not request. We propose CapScope, a harness-level authorization mechanism that restricts tool use without requiring the model to identify malicious text. Before repository contents or tool output are read, CapScope derives a task-wide authority ceiling from trusted input. It then assigns each agent a separate set of typed capabilities, stored outside the model's context. Every tool call is checked against the capabilities of the agent that issued it. Permissions assigned to one sub-agent are therefore not automatically available to another. An injection may cause an agent to request an action, but the request is blocked unless that agent already has the required capability. We implement CapScope on the Pi coding agent and evaluate it in a repair workflow where an orchestrator delegates subtasks to separate sub-agents. The evaluation covers five Python tasks, five injection surfaces, four authorization conditions, and three trials per cell (300 runs). The injected effect executes in 33-47/75 runs under the ambient-authority and global-policy baselines, compared with 3/75 under CapScope. CapScope completes 68/75 repairs, while the baselines complete 68-72/75.

</details>


### [43] [PLC-Bin2Src: Retrieving Corresponding Structured Text Source Files for PLC Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08563)
*Ang Jia,Yaxin Duan,He Jiang,Ming Fan,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PLC-Bin2Src：通过平台感知前端生成可比表示，后端融合CDFG、FCG与恢复符号相似度，实现PLC二进制到Structured Text源码的跨平台检索，效果优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLC二进制通常为闭源或仅有二进制分发，难以追溯其源代码，阻碍依赖管理和安全审计。二进制→源码匹配是软件组成分析的重要环节，但受跨平台编译差异、二进制与ST表示差距及单元粒度不一致的挑战。PLC-Bin2Src旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 采用平台感知的前端为不同PLC平台（CODESYS、GEB、OpenPLC v2/v3）构建可比中间表示；后端统一融合控制-数据流图(CDFG)、函数调用图(FCG)和恢复的符号相似度，按综合相似度对源码候选排序并返回Top-k。评测使用PLC-BEAD数据集，给出Recall@1/5和MRR作为指标。

Result: 在四个平台上对PLC-BEAD评测，Recall@1=95.89%，Recall@5=99.66%，MRR=0.9769。

Conclusion: PLC-Bin2Src能高效且鲁棒地将PLC二进制映射到相应的ST源文件，为依赖清点、漏洞评估和安全审计提供关键支撑。但需进一步验证对混淆/优化构建、更多平台及大规模库的泛化能力，并量化各相似度分量的贡献与运行时开销。

Abstract: Software reuse allows existing components and third-party libraries to be incorporated into new applications, but binary-only components can obscure their origins and implementations. Software composition analysis seeks to identify these reused components and trace their provenance, supporting dependency inventory, vulnerability assessment, and security auditing. For PLC applications, binary2source matching provides a core link in this analysis: given an opaque PLC binary artifact, retrieve its corresponding Structured Text (ST) source file from a collected source repository. However, this task is complicated by cross-platform compilation heterogeneity, the representation gap between PLC binaries and ST source code, and the granularity mismatch between recovered binary units and ST source files. This paper presents PLC-Bin2Src, a cross-platform binary2source matching framework for retrieving corresponding ST source files for binaries produced by CODESYS, GEB, OpenPLC v2, and OpenPLC v3. Platform-aware frontends construct comparable representations, and a shared backend equally combines control--data flow graph (CDFG), function call graph (FCG), and recovered-symbol similarities to rank source candidates. We evaluate PLC-Bin2Src on PLC-BEAD. Results show that PLC-Bin2Src achieves 95.89% Recall@1, 99.66% Recall@5, and an MRR of 0.9769 across four PLC platforms.

</details>


### [44] [The Unreliable Progress Bar: Can LLM Agents Reliably Report Task Progress Throughout Execution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08589)
*Boyang Wang,Yunhan Wang,Yalun Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统化测试显示：模型的任务进度报告是阶段依赖的、并存在可预测的失效模式，代理系统不能单凭模型自报状态决定流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代理框架依赖模型输出的任务状态信号来决定是否继续或终止任务，但尚无系统研究验证模型能否在任务全过程稳定、可靠地报告进度，亦不清楚失败出现的阶段与模式。

Method: 作者在公开基准 τ^2-bench 以及新构建的受控测试床 StageIF 上，对模型在任务生命周期中多个检查点的进度报告能力进行系统化评估，比较不同模型与不同阶段的准确率与失效模式。

Result: 发现报告可靠性随任务阶段而变：多数模型在任务开始或结束阶段表现较好，但在中途执行时准确率下降；新一代模型缓解了中途失真但在终点趋向保守。作者提出了覆盖任务全程的评估协议，并建议代理框架不应仅依赖模型的状态报告来控制任务流程。

Conclusion: 本文表明大语言模型在任务进度报告上存在阶段性可靠性差异；几乎所有已部署模型在某些阶段可靠、在另一些阶段不可靠，且不同模型在何处失效不一致。新一代模型改善了中途失准问题，但在任务完成临界点表现出保守倾向。

Abstract: Recent large language models can emit task-progress signals that agent frameworks use to decide whether a task should continue or stop, yet whether a model can reliably report its task progress at every stage of a task, and where and how its reports fail, has not been studied systematically. We evaluate this ability on the public benchmark $τ^2$-bench and on StageIF, a controlled testbed in which reporting checkpoints are placed across the task's lifecycle. Both settings require reports at multiple task stages. We find that reporting reliability depends on the stage a task has reached, and that almost every deployed model we test is reliable at some stages and unreliable at others. Where reporting breaks down is not the same everywhere. Most deployed models lose accuracy once work is under way and recover once the task is done. The newest generation closes that mid-task drop and instead grows conservative at the finish line. Our study exposes a capability gap in task-progress reporting and provides an evaluation protocol that spans the whole course of task execution for this ability on which agent operation depends. The findings indicate that agent frameworks should not control task flow on the strength of the model's state reports alone.

</details>


### [45] [Beyond Fixed Fault Models: Comparing LLM-Based and Rule-Based Fault Injection in OpenStack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08681)
*Giuseppe De Rosa,Pietro Liguori,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comparing two code LLMs to a rule-based injector on OpenStack shows similar activation/observable-failure rates but different operational profiles: LLMs cause more catastrophic failures in Nova while ProFIPy induces more silent/multi-component effects; LLMs broaden coverage but need controls, oracles, and reproducibility measures.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Improve cloud-system testing by exploring data-driven, context-sensitive fault generation (LLMs) as an alternative to manually encoded, rule-based software fault injection.

Method: Empirical comparison between two code LLMs (Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder) and a rule-based injector (ProFIPy) applied to OpenStack Nova and Cinder; measured activation rates, observable-failure rates, operational outcome categories (Catastrophic, Silent, Multi-component), failure manifestation styles, and propagation scope on shared injection targets.

Result: Activation and observable-failure rates are comparable across LLMs and rule-based injector on shared targets; however, LLM outputs produce more Catastrophic outcomes in Nova while ProFIPy yields more Silent and multi-component effects. LLMs differ in manifestation styles but show agreement on propagation scope. Overall, LLMs extend behavioral coverage without clear overall superiority.

Conclusion: LLM-based fault injection complements rule-based injectors by increasing behavioral coverage but does not universally outperform them; practical use requires controlled generation, validation, and reproducible provenance.

Abstract: Software Fault Injection (SFI) supports testing of cloud systems by introducing software defects and observing their manifestation. Rule-based injectors such as ProFIPy provide controlled and reproducible source-level mutations but require fault patterns to be encoded manually. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a data-driven alternative by generating context-dependent software faults. We compare two code LLMs, Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, with ProFIPy in OpenStack's Nova and Cinder services. On shared injection targets, activation and observable-failure rates are comparable, but operational profiles differ: LLM-generated faults produce more Catastrophic outcomes on Nova, whereas ProFIPy produces more Silent and Multi-component effects. The sampled LLM outputs also differ in how they manifest failure, while showing greater agreement in their propagation scope. These findings show that LLM-based fault injection extends the behavioral coverage of fixed fault models without establishing general superiority, and that practical adoption still requires controlled generation, runtime validation, system-level oracles, and reproducible experimental provenance.

</details>


### [46] [DJPlus: Generating minimal test suites for strong coverage criteria in graph models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08953)
*Yavuz Köroğlu,Mutlu Beyazıt,Onur Kılınççeker,Serge Demeyer,Franz Wotawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DJPlus 用优化方法减小模型驱动测试的测试套件，在保持图覆盖要求（尤其是 edge-pair）下显著减少冗余步骤并缩短执行时间；prime path 虽更强但存在可扩展性问题，评测基于四个实际系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动测试中图覆盖会导致测试用例过长、执行耗时，故希望通过优化缩减测试套件在保持覆盖要求下减少冗余步骤，加速测试在被测系统上的执行。

Method: 基于优化（未在摘要明确指出具体优化模型）来选择或合并测试步骤，约束是满足指定的图覆盖准则（edge-pair 等）；实现并对比了若干替代方法，在四个系统上评估冗余步骤数和测试执行时间。

Result: DJPlus 提出了一种基于优化的测试套件缩减方法，目标是在满足图覆盖需求（如 edge-pair 覆盖）的前提下生成更短、冗余更少的测试套件。作者实现了该方法并在四个真实系统上评估，发现与对比方法相比，DJPlus 生成的冗余测试步骤少 2 到 26 倍，从而降低测试执行时间；同时指出 prime path 覆盖在规模上存在问题，而 edge-pair 在这四个系统上是可行的。

Conclusion: 论文提出的优化驱动缩减方法在实验用例上有效地降低了冗余和执行时间，证明了 edge-pair 覆盖在工业系统中的可行性，但关于方法细节、可扩展性、通用性和统计显著性的证据仍需补充以增强说服力。

Abstract: Automated test generation from graph models is essential to model-based testing. In this type of testing, graph coverage ensures test suite strength but also results in long test cases that take time to execute on the system under test. We propose a novel optimization-driven method, DJPlus, which generates reduced test suites while satisfying given graph-based test requirements. We implement DJPlus and show the feasibility of edge-pair criterion, a stronger coverage criterion than vertex or edge criteria, on four realistic systems, while prime path criterion poses scalability issues. Our evaluation reveals that the alternative methods generate 2 to 26 times more redundant test steps than DJPlus and DJPlus decreases test execution times by reducing the number of test steps. These results show that DJPlus is a positive step towards tackling the challenges of model-based testing at an industrial scale.

</details>


### [47] [It Is Not My Code Anymore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09022)
*Augusto Camargo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文主张：在AI辅助编程中，作者身份并不足以决定责任，需把注意力放在服务结果的验收准则与生产过程的证据上，并提供用于评估产物与过程的概念性问题集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI参与代码生成，关于代码“谁写的”、谁“拥有”、谁对失效负责的传统范式被挑战，需在工程与治理层面上厘清责任与验收标准。

Method: 通过一个假设的入学失败示例与对相关文献的选择性阅读，提出概念性区分与用于评估AI辅助软件生成过程与产物的问题。

Result: 提出了：将验收准则与所需服务结果对齐、以组件检查作为产出满足服务结果的证据、并比较生成程序与直接执行任务系统在作者对象上的差异；并给出一系列用于评估AI辅助软件生产的判断性问题。

Conclusion: 识别生成缺陷表达式的“制作者”并不能单独决定审查者、发布决策者或运行者的职责；集体所有权亦需明确职责分配。

Abstract: AI-assisted programming raises distinct questions about who produces code, who feels ownership of it, and who is responsible when it fails. This research note examines these distinctions through a hypothetical enrollment failure and a selective reading of the literature. Identifying the producer of a defective expression does not, by itself, determine the duties of reviewers, release decision-makers, or service operators. Collective ownership likewise leaves those duties to be specified. The discussion then considers how quality engineering can evaluate both generated implementations and the processes that produce them. Acceptance criteria should be justified by the required service outcome, with component checks contributing evidence toward that outcome. This perspective also permits comparison with systems that perform a task without generating a separate program for it. Such substitution would change the object of authorship while leaving the service obligation intact. The note reports no new empirical results; it proposes distinctions and evaluation questions for AI-assisted software production.

</details>

### [48] [Beyond Right and Wrong: Evaluating Second-order Social Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05437)
*Sunny Rai,Jinyi Kuang,Reyhan Jamalova,Annie Lou,Cristina Bicchieri,Niyati Malhotra,Victor Hugo Orozco-Olvera,Ana Maria Munoz-Boudet,Lyle H Ungar,Sharath C Guntuku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper defines metanorm reasoning, releases NormReact (450 scenarios), and shows current LLMs are biased toward punitive responses and less aligned with human expectations when observers are socially distant.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing alignment work focuses on first-order norms (what is acceptable) but neglects second-order metanorms (who enforces norms and how); accurate metanorm reasoning is needed for social intelligence and safe deployment in norm-sensitive applications.

Method: Introduced a metanorm-evaluation framework with two dimensions (emotional appraisal and behavioral response); proposed classification tasks predicting self-regulation (violators) and other-regulation (observers); created NormReact, a 450-scenario, hand-annotated dataset across violator gender and observer social closeness; evaluated six LLMs.

Result: Across six evaluated LLMs, models overpredict negative sanctions where humans expect inaction; alignment with humans worsens with greater social distance; suggests AI may overrepresent punishment and underrepresent restraint in social regulation.

Conclusion: LLMs currently overpredict punitive responses to norm violations and show decreasing alignment with human judgments as social distance increases, risking a distorted portrayal of social regulation in applied systems.

Abstract: Previous AI alignment efforts have focused primarily on first-order social norms -- teaching models what is socially acceptable or unacceptable (e.g., `do not steal'). However, social intelligence depends not only on norm recognition, but also on anticipating who will enforce it and how (e.g., public shame or even imprisonment). These second-order expectations, known as metanorms, govern how people respond when social rules are broken. We introduce a novel framework for evaluating metanorm reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) along two dimensions: emotional appraisal and behavioral response, and propose new classification tasks, namely, predicting self-regulation in violators, and other-regulation in observers. We release a multi-perspective dataset, NormReact, of 450 norm violation scenarios, hand-annotated for emotions and behavioral responses across norm violators' gender and observers' social closeness. Current LLMs portray a harsher social world: across six models, they overpredict negative sanctions where humans would expect inaction, and alignment with human judgments deteriorates as social distance increases. These findings suggest that AI systems in norm-sensitive domains from conflict mediation to policy simulation, may risk producing a distorted picture of social regulation: one that over-represents punishment and under-represents the tolerance, restraint, and relational calibration that characterize actual norm enforcement in real world.

</details>


### [49] [CriticGen: Generation-Aware Evaluation as Actionable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05439)
*Huifang Du,Zecheng Zuo,Sen Wang,Chenghao Fan,Haofen Wang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CriticGen 用实例特定的动态rubric把评估变为可执行的改进指令，显著提高评估信度与答案改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型评估常是粗粒度并与生成脱钩，给出通用且无法直接用于改进的解释；需一种能产出可执行、实例相关反馈的评估机制，将评估转化为对答案的可用控制信号。

Method: 在每个样本上生成特定的评估维度与评分准则（归入主类如主观/客观/自导约束），将这些准则作为动态量表（rubric），联合产出分数、理由、可执行改进建议和经改进的答案，利用准则驱动的反馈循环实现针对性改写。

Result: 在多项指标上取得提升：rubric 质量提升（如相关性/覆盖率从3.33/4.03到3.97/4.24）；评分相关性最高（Pearson=0.9556，Spearman=0.9560）；基于准则的理由与可执行建议的F1从0.6369/0.5994提升到0.7554/0.7900；反馈驱动改进使73.17%答案得到提升，且保全率为93.28%。

Conclusion: CriticGen 将评估与生成耦合，提供细粒度、针对实例的可执行反馈，使模型能够根据动态评分准则给出诊断并改写答案，从而显著提升评估质量与回答改进效果。

Abstract: Current evaluation methods for large language models are coarse-grained and decoupled from generation, producing generic explanations that fail to provide actionable feedback for model improvement. We propose CriticGen, a fine-grained, generation-aware evaluation framework that turns evaluation into actionable control for answer improvement. CriticGen first generates sample-specific evaluation dimensions and scoring criteria under high-level categories such as subjective, objective, and self-derived constraints. These criteria then serve as a dynamic rubric for jointly producing a score, a reason, an executable refinement suggestion, and a refined answer. This rubric-conditioned refinement process enables models to diagnose flaws and perform targeted answer improvement. Experimental results show that fine-grained evaluation should be both instance-specific and actionable. CriticGen induces higher-quality rubrics, improving relevance/coverage from 3.33/4.03 to 3.97/4.24. CriticGen also achieves the best score correlations, with 0.9556 Pearson and 0.9560 Spearman, and raises the F1 of criterion-grounded reasons and executable suggestions from 0.6369/0.5994 to 0.7554/0.7900. Crucially, its feedback translates into reliable answer improvement, improving 73.17% of answers with a 93.28% non-degradation rate.

</details>


### [50] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MERIT 将记忆的价值从“会话回忆”迁移到“影响工具行为”的实际边际效用测量，按代价计账并通过漏检验证任务依赖性；结果显示检索记忆在更新事实场景不可靠，结构化写入更新更稳健，全回放并不经济。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评估（如 LoCoMo、LongMemEval）侧重对话历史问答，衡量记忆“能否被回忆”，但并不衡量记忆“是否改变会使用工具的代理的行为和结果”，也缺乏显式成本考量。MERIT 旨在填补这一空白，通过现实的工具化任务、成本计量与鲁棒性检验来衡量记忆的实际边际效用。

Method: 构建 MERIT 基准与执行框架，包含三类域的分期工具使用任务；每个域通过自动化“信息泄露检查”验证后期回合确实依赖早期回合事实；设置难度阶梯直到“更新事实”回忆；引入可控的记忆损毁；对每一次记忆操作进行完整的 token 与美元计量。执行大规模试验（23,440 次评分回合，$42.57 成本），包括两代试点与一个预注册的 3 模型×3 随机种子网格实验，评估多种记忆实现（向量检索、结构化事实库、LLM 摘要、混合、全回放等），并在最新模型上做点查验。

Result: MERIT 提供了一个面向工具型智能体的记忆基准，按代价计账评估记忆的边际效用。基于 23,440 个评分回合（成本 $42.57）的大规模实验显示：记忆显著提高对依赖早期事实任务的成功率（从 0.00 提升到 0.55–1.00）；对“更新事实”场景，基于向量检索的检索不稳定（模型间 0.30–0.95），且即便检索正确，智能体也仅有 55% 的时间据此行动；而“写入时更新”机制（结构化事实库与 LLM 摘要）在 0.70–1.00 区间表现更可靠，混合策略反而劣于纯事实库。更换记忆实现最多能改变任务成功率 60 个百分点，且全回放在花费-效用比上从未占优（最佳条件每美元带来 2.7–3.9 倍的边际效用）。基准、测试工具和所有追踪已公开。

Conclusion: 记忆的实现方式决定了工具型智能体在依赖历史事实任务上的边际效用：向量检索在“更新事实”场景中不稳定且不总能驱动代理采取正确动作；写入时更新（结构化事实库或 LLM 摘要）更可靠；混合并非总优；全回放成本过高且不可取。研究者和工程团队应将记忆评估纳入代价度量，选择与任务特性相匹配的记忆策略。

Abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [51] [AutoFyn Technical Report: Non-Parametric Expert Iteration for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05446)
*Adib Hasan,Daniel Schaffield,Akashnil Dutta,Tarik Adnan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFyn提出一种更新持久化外部状态而非模型权重的多轮专家迭代式代理框架，依赖任务验证器提供客观奖励并将其蒸馏回持久状态，从而在数学、数据与安全任务上显著提升模型表现并生成可核实成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不对模型本体微调或改变权重的条件下，寻求一种可持续、可审计且跨多轮改进模型行为的机制。动机包括降低微调成本与依赖、提高行为可解释性和可验证性、并通过任务级客观奖励实现持续策略改进。

Method: 每轮从一个“冻结”模型的全新会话开始，所有跨轮知识仅通过显式接口（持久化记忆文件、报告、仓库状态）重引入。轮内由编排器分配多个专门化子代理以并行探索与构建不同解法，随后由以任务为基准的验证器对候选成果进行客观打分（奖励）。将验证器产生的奖励以某种蒸馏或写回机制更新持久化状态，从而在下一轮影响代理的策略选择。报告中形式化了循环、持久化状态结构与验证接口实现细节。

Result: 在三个实用领域的实证展示中：对2026年国际数学奥林匹克的六道新题，所有“有提升空间”的模型在AutoFyn框架下得分高于其提供者自带的编码代理；在Spider 2.0 dbt基准上构建了排名第一的代理；在多个开源项目中生成了16条经维护者确认的漏洞通告。

Conclusion: AutoFyn是一种以Expert Iteration为灵感的框架，通过在不更新模型权重的前提下，利用可验证的任务奖励信号来更新持久化状态，以期在多轮交互中改进模型的有效策略。其实验结果表明，在数学竞赛题、数据科学与网络安全三个领域均能显著提升基线模型的实际表现，并产生了可核实的攻击面报告与基准排名改进。

Abstract: We introduce AutoFyn, an agent harness inspired by the Expert Iteration algorithm, adapting a frozen model across many rounds by updating persistent state from verified reward signals rather than model weights. Each round begins from a fresh model session, and durable information is reintroduced only through explicit interfaces such as persistent memory files, reports, and repository state. Within a round, an orchestrator explores, plans and builds many alternative approaches with specialized agents, while a task-grounded verifier verifies the work and supplies an objective reward for measuring progress. This reward is distilled back into the persistent state, which updates the effective policy for the next round. In this technical report, we formalize this loop and describe its persistent state and verification interfaces. We then demonstrate its use in three domains, namely olympiad mathematics, data science, and cybersecurity. On the six fresh problems of the 2026 International Mathematical Olympiad, every model with room to improve scores higher under AutoFyn than in its provider's own coding agent. AutoFyn also built the top-ranked agent on the Spider 2.0 dbt benchmark, and has produced $16$ maintainer-confirmed vulnerability advisories in Next.js, MetaMask, pnpm, Warp, LiteLLM, Langflow, and Open WebUI.

</details>


### [52] [Damage-Aware Bandit Pruning for Vision and Language Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05448)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将结构化单元选择表述为在固定评估预算下的损害感知多臂老虎机问题，并用成对损害+有界奖励配合 UCB/分数化-Beta Thompson 策略逐步构建掩码，实验证明在多数比较中优于预算贪心等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不重新训练或超出评估预算的情况下，从完整功能单元中选取能在最小性能损害下被关闭的集合，对语言与视觉 Transformer 的结构化后训练剪枝提出一个可行方法。

Method: 将注意力头和 MLP 通道组在校准批次上临时屏蔽，利用“成对损害”（被屏蔽损失减去同批次基线损失）来减小批次间方差；以平滑有界的奖励驱动 UCB 风格策略或分数化-Beta Thompson 抽样；以逐步添加单元的方式构建最终掩码，并在原始密集检查点上功能性地将选中单元置零以衡量有效的结构抑制效果。

Result: 在 WikiText-2、LAMBADA、Imagenette 等数据集及 GPT-2、OPT、Pythia、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2、ViT-B/16、DeiT-Tiny、Swin-Tiny 的实验中，带有五个随机种子的对比显示：相较于随机、基于大小、静态显著性和预算贪心选择，老虎机方法在多数语言模型比较中降低了性能降级。论文报告的统计证据包括：在论文突出的 28 次比较中有 23 次引导自助置信区间不包含零，11 次配对检验 p < 0.05；在全部 116 次数据集级检验上按 Benjamini–Hochberg 校正后有 6 次 q < 0.05；对 ViT-B/16 和 Swin-Tiny 的匹配评估表明其收益不能仅由更大的候选评估预算解释。

Conclusion: 基于损害感知的多臂老虎机方法在受限的候选评估预算下，可更稳健地选择完整功能单元用于 Transformer 的结构化后训练剪枝，通常比预算贪心法在语言模型的降级上更小。

Abstract: Structured post-training pruning of transformers requires selecting complete functional units whose suppression causes limited degradation. We formulate structured-unit selection for language and vision transformers as a damage-aware multi-armed bandit problem under a fixed candidate-evaluation budget. Attention heads and MLP channel groups are temporarily masked on calibration batches. Paired damage is the masked loss minus the base loss on the same batch, reducing batch-to-batch variation. A smooth bounded reward drives either a UCB-style policy or fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling, and the final mask is constructed sequentially by adding one unit at each step. The selected units are functionally zeroed in the original dense checkpoint; therefore, the reported parameter effects represent effective structural suppression rather than physical compression or measured speedup. Experiments on WikiText-2, LAMBADA, and Imagenette cover GPT-2, OPT, Pythia, Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, ViT-B/16, DeiT-Tiny, and Swin-Tiny, with comparisons against random, magnitude, static-saliency, and budgeted-greedy selection. Across five seeds, the bandit methods usually reduce degradation relative to budgeted greedy in the paired language-model comparisons. Of 28 comparisons highlighted in the paper, 23 bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero and 11 paired tests have p < 0.05; six have q < 0.05 after Benjamini-Hochberg correction across the full family of 116 dataset-wise tests. Matched-evaluation results for ViT-B/16 and Swin-Tiny indicate that their gains are not explained solely by a larger candidate-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [53] [Compiling VGDL into Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05459)
*Mohit Jiwatode,Bodo Rosenhahn,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将VGDL游戏符号化组件确定性地编译为动态结构因果模型，保证对底层机制的因果保真并支持反事实推理与因果RL应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习代理和大型语言模型在把握游戏因果机制时常受虚假的相关性或幻觉影响，缺乏将复杂游戏机制正式映射为因果模型的方法学。

Method: 通过把游戏的精灵动态、交互规则和终止条件等组件逐一翻译为明确的结构方程，定义每一游戏刻（tick）为从时刻t到t+1的因果跃迁；不依赖轨迹推断或大型语言模型的噪声输出，而是进行符号到因果模型的直接转换。

Result: 所得模型对因果路径透明、可进行反事实推理、支持因果强化学习训练与程序化内容验证，为符号化游戏描述与因果扎根的游戏AI之间建立了有原则的桥梁。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种确定性框架，将以视频游戏描述语言（VGDL）编写的游戏直接编译为动态结构因果模型（Dynamic SCMs），从而实现对游戏机制的绝对因果保真映射。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and large language models often struggle to accurately capture the causal mechanics of game environments. Standard reinforcement learning agents tend to rely on spurious correlations, while large language models are prone to hallucinating game rules. Although causal reinforcement learning improves interpretability, there is currently no formal methodology to map complex game mechanics directly into causal models. To address this, we propose a deterministic framework that compiles games specified in the Video Game Description Language into Dynamic Structural Causal Models. Rather than inferring causal structures from gameplay traces or noisy large language models' outputs, our methodology directly translates game components, including sprite dynamics, interaction rules, and termination conditions, into explicit structural equations. Each game tick represents a causal transition from state variables at time $t$ to $t+1$. By establishing this grounded mapping, the approach guarantees absolute causal fidelity to the ground-truth game mechanics. The resulting models offer transparent causal pathways that support counterfactual reasoning, causal reinforcement learning agent training, and procedural content validation. This framework provides a principled bridge between symbolic game descriptions and causally grounded game AI.

</details>


### [54] [ARC-Bench: Closed-Loop Replanning Masks Broken Action Ranking in Frozen JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05461)
*Zhengshu Zhang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARC-Bench provides a no-leak test showing JEPA-style frozen latent distances do not reliably rank actions; closed-loop replanning conceals these defects, so reported success rates overstate the suitability of frozen latent representations for action-ranking.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To audit the implicit assumption in reward-free latent planners that distances in a frozen latent space produce an action ranking consistent with true task cost—an assumption often untested but central to JEPA-WM style planning.

Method: Introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak fixed-candidate evaluation protocol that tests whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly. Apply audit to official JEPA-WM checkpoints across domains, run backbone replacements (DINOv2 vs V-JEPA 1/2 at ViT-L/ViT-G), run provenance and control experiments (undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, metric-circularity controls), and test effect of replanning frequency with diagnostics (PointMaze first-plan).

Result: Found that the assumption fails severely: top-scored candidates are frequently suboptimal, with structural inversions in manipulation and maze domains; failure persists across video-pretrained backbone replacements; controls rule out trivial artifacts; closed-loop replanning masks the defect, so frequent replanning rescues episodes that have severe first-plan failures, leading to overestimation of latent rankability in closed-loop success metrics.

Conclusion: Frozen JEPA-style latent world models do not reliably rank candidate actions by true cost; latent closeness is not action-rankable. ARC-Bench reveals severe, structural ranking inversions across navigation and manipulation checkpoints, and closed-loop replanning hides these failures.

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models plan by scoring candidate actions with distances in a frozen latent space: an action is preferred if its predicted future embedding lands closer to the goal embedding. This silently assumes that latent closeness is action-rankable, i.e., that ordering candidates by latent distance agrees with ordering them by true cost. We audit this assumption directly. We introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak, fixed-candidate protocol that measures whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly, and apply it to official released JEPA-WM checkpoints across navigation and manipulation-style control. The assumption fails, severely and structurally: on the official manipulation audits the top-scored candidate is almost always suboptimal, and the same inversion appears in the maze domains. A controlled visual-backbone extension shows that the defect persists when DINOv2 is replaced by video-pretrained V-JEPA 1 and V-JEPA 2 encoders at ViT-L/ViT-G scale. Provenance, undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, and metric-circularity controls rule out trivial explanations. We then explain why this defect has stayed invisible: closed-loop replanning masks it. When we reduce the planner's replanning frequency, success collapses in both a navigation and a manipulation domain, and the episodes rescued by frequent replanning are enriched for severe first-plan ranking failures in the PointMaze first-plan diagnostic. Closed-loop success rates therefore systematically overstate the rankability of frozen latent representations. ARC-Bench supplies the measurement, and the masking mechanism the explanation, for methods that adapt, amortize, or replan around latent-space planners without directly auditing released JEPA-WM action rankability.

</details>


### [55] [RAPID: Reliability-Aware Pair Importance Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05481)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAPID通过将“可靠性门控的目标”与“自适应采样提议”分离，并用逆提议校正保证估计无偏，实现了在受限关系预算下高效的关系蒸馏；实验证明可靠性门控对性能提升贡献明显，自适应提议保持在种子级波动范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全部对的全对比计算在小批量上有二次复杂度，均匀子采样在有限预算下低效。需要一种能在有限关系预算下优先评估既可靠又信息量高的关系的方法。

Method: 提出RAPID：把关系目标按可靠性门控（依赖校准后的教师熵）来强调教师中可靠的关系；用与学生-教师残差（Detached residuals）和校准教师熵结合的自适应采样提议选择待评估的关系，并通过精确的逆提议校正使损失和梯度估计在条件上无偏。将试验性（pilot）评估计入总预算以模拟实际受限预算情形。

Result: 在AG News（BERT→DistilBERT，关系预算256，3个随机种子）和SST-2（DistilBERT→DistilBERT，关系预算64，3个随机种子）上，可靠性门控的关系蒸馏取得最高平均学生准确率（AG News: 94.285±0.054%；SST-2: 88.800±0.532%）；RAPID位列第二（AG News: 94.241±0.025%；SST-2: 88.685±0.462%），均优于交叉熵基线（94.154±0.124%与87.271±0.162%）。

Conclusion: 将教师模型的代表性几何通过样本间关系匹配进行蒸馏时，区分“可靠性门控的关系目标”和“全支撑自适应对提议”可以在受限关系预算下更有效地选择和评估关系，从而在若干文本分类蒸馏设置中取得优异性能。

Abstract: Inter example relational distillation transfers a teacher's representation geometry by matching relations among examples within a mini batch. Computing all pairs has quadratic complexity in the batch size, whereas uniform subsampling may use a limited relation budget inefficiently. We introduce Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation, or RAPID, which separates a reliability gated relational target from a full support adaptive pair proposal. Reliability determines which teacher relations are emphasized, while calibrated teacher entropy and detached student-teacher residuals determine which relations are evaluated. Exact inverse proposal correction makes the loss and gradient estimators conditionally unbiased with respect to the gated mini batch target. We evaluate RAPID in two text classification settings: AG News with BERT-to-DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 256, and SST-2 with DistilBERT to DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 64. Reliability gated relational distillation achieves the highest observed mean student accuracy on both datasets: 94.285 plus or minus 0.054 percent on AG News and 88.800 plus or minus 0.532 percent on SST-2. RAPID ranks second, achieving 94.241 plus or minus 0.025 percent and 88.685 plus or minus 0.462 percent, respectively, compared with 94.154 plus or minus 0.124 percent and 87.271 plus or minus 0.162 percent for the cross entropy baseline. Pilot evaluations are counted toward the same total budget as the main relation evaluations. Across both settings, the gated target yields the highest mean accuracy, while the adaptive proposal remains within seed-level variation. These results support the modular view that target reliability and evaluation priority are separable design dimensions.

</details>


### [56] [PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN: Prior-Guided Joint Probabilistic Forecasting of Clinical Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05488)
*Weizhi Nie,Rihao Chang,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种带软器官先验与个体化关系的低秩Student-t轨迹模型，能较好地做联合概率预测并提供可检验的中间表征，但对最终事件检测的校准和判别力仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床恶化由耦合且部分可观测的多变量轨迹构成，而非单一诊断标签；需要能表达联合不确定性的轨迹优先（trajectory-first）概率预测方法以支持可解释的中间输出与下游任务。

Method: 提出PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN：结合缺失感知时序编码器、软器官系统先验、个体化关系建模、非线性Kolmogorov–Arnold信息传递机制以及低秩多元Student-t输出层，对多变量生理时间序列进行联合概率预测。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV派生队列（6,882名患者，54,694个窗口）上，24小时历史➜6小时预测任务：归一化MAE 0.37727±0.00029（次低），RMSE 0.52656±0.00034（最低）；相对确定性TimeKAN MAE下降0.52%；概率评估：边际NLL 0.66380，CRPS 0.27301；经验覆盖率（50/80/95%）=0.533/0.831/0.958；去除关系结构导致最大性能下降；提高协方差秩提升联合似然但对点估计影响小；轨迹导出风险评分AUROC=0.603，弱于GRU-D的0.650。

Conclusion: 联合轨迹预测提供了可审查的中间任务，但仅靠准确的生理学预测并不能保证事件检测器的校准；PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN 在点估计和联合似然方面表现优异（最低RMSE、次低归一化MAE），但在风险评分/AUROC 上落后于专用分类器。

Abstract: Clinical deterioration unfolds through coupled, partially observed trajectories, not a single diagnostic label. We introduce PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN, a trajectory-first framework for joint probabilistic forecasting of multivariate physiology. It combines missingness-aware temporal encoders, a soft organ-system prior, patient-specific relations, nonlinear Kolmogorov-Arnold messages, and a low-rank multivariate Student-t head. We evaluate 24-hour histories and six-hour forecasts on a frozen MIMIC-IV-derived cohort of 6,882 patients and 54,694 windows. Across five seeds and 13 models, PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN obtains the second-lowest normalized MAE (0.37727 +/- 0.00029) and the lowest RMSE (0.52656 +/- 0.00034). It reduces MAE by 0.52% relative to deterministic TimeKAN. For probabilistic forecasting, it reaches a marginal NLL of 0.66380 and a CRPS of 0.27301. Empirical coverage is 0.533, 0.831, and 0.958 for nominal 50%, 80%, and 95% intervals. Removing relational structure causes the largest ablation loss. Increasing covariance rank improves joint likelihood but has little effect on point accuracy. A trajectory-derived risk score remains weaker than a dedicated GRU-D classifier (AUROC 0.603 versus 0.650), which limits the present clinical claim. Joint trajectory forecasting therefore provides an inspectable intermediate task, but accurate physiology forecasts alone do not ensure a calibrated event detector.

</details>


### [57] [SciLitBench: Benchmark and Design Principles for LLM-Powered Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05505)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciLitBench 提供可复现的多阶段基准，证明 LLM 对高召回筛查有实用价值，但在证据完备型信息抽取上仍远不足以取代人工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 LLM 在证据综述中角色的评估多分阶段孤立考察，缺乏跨阶段的综合基准来衡量模型在实际系统评价流程中的可靠性与局限。

Method: 构建多阶段基准（标题与摘要筛查、全文筛查、基于 schema 的数据抽取），包含 42,981 条检索记录、1,012 篇全文与 888 篇纳入论文的标注；在 6 个模型家族的 22 个开放权重 LLM 上，使用 F2、准确率和 Jaccard 等指标进行系统评估，并对比有无显式标准与研究者理由的设定影响。

Result: 显式包含/排除标准使标题与摘要筛查 F2 提升 28.8%；研究者撰写的理由使全文筛查提升 15%；数据抽取表现差异大，从发表年份 0.97 的准确率退降到计算方法 0.37 的 Jaccard 重叠；最强模型仅能恢复约 30% 的评估证据与 25% 的研究限制。

Conclusion: SciLitBench 表明：开放权重大模型在高召回的筛查任务中可通过明确的包含/排除标准与研究者撰写的理由显著提升表现，但在领域化、细粒度的数据抽取上表现远低于人类专家，构成了“高召回筛查”与“证据完整提取”之间的实际边界。

Abstract: Systematic reviews require sustained human judgment across thousands of records, yet existing evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically examine review stages in isolation. We introduce SciLitBench, a multi-stage benchmark spanning title and abstract screening, full-text screening, and schema-guided data extraction, with 42,981 retrieved records, 1,012 full texts, and annotations for 888 included papers. Across 22 open-weight LLMs from six model families, explicit inclusion and exclusion criteria improve title and abstract screening $F_2$ by 28.8\%, while researcher-authored rationales improve full-text screening by 15\%. Data extraction reveals a different reliability regime: performance declines from 0.97 accuracy for publication year to 0.37 Jaccard overlap for computational approach, while the strongest models recover only 30\% of annotated evaluation evidence and 25\% of limitations. SciLitBench identifies a practical boundary between high-recall screening and evidence-complete extraction and provides a reproducible resource for evaluating LLM-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [58] [SCAFFOLD: Self-Improving Web Agents via Recursive Parametric Skill Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05511)
*Bowei He,Xiaokun Zhang,Meng Ding,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Scaffold: induce, hierarchically compose, compress (MDL), and distill skills to achieve large, stable self-improvement for visual web agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing web agents learn tasks in isolation and discard procedural knowledge; prior skill-augmented frameworks treat skills as a flat cache lacking principled compression and recursive composition, limiting reuse and scalability.

Method: (1) Extract successful trajectories and induce parametric skills under a multi-instance abstraction constraint; (2) arrange skills into a recursively composed hierarchy so higher-level skills call lower-level ones; (3) compact the skill library via minimum-description-length (MDL) optimization and behavioral equivalence checking; (4) periodically distill skill-augmented trajectories into base model weights. Evaluate on WebArena, VisualWebArena, and an Online-Mind2Web held-out split.

Result: On WebArena, VisualWebArena, and an Online-Mind2Web held-out split, Scaffold improves success rate by 11.1–17.2 absolute percentage points over the strongest skill-augmented baseline, exhibits monotonic gains across five self-improvement iterations, and avoids library collapse. Code released.

Conclusion: Scaffold enables visual web agents to self-improve by inducing parametric, executable skills, organizing them into a recursive hierarchy, compressing the library using MDL and behavioral equivalence checks, and distilling augmented trajectories back into model weights; this yields substantial and monotonic performance gains without library collapse.

Abstract: Web agents need to navigate visually rich, long-horizon interfaces that change across sites, yet most previous agents still learn each task in isolation and discard the procedural knowledge they accumulate. Recent skill-augmented frameworks take an important first step, but they treat the skill library as a flat or two-tier prompt-side cache and offer no principled mechanism for compressing redundancy or composing skills recursively. We introduce \textsc{Scaffold}, a self-improving framework for visual web agents that (i) induces parametric, executable skills from successful trajectories under a multi-instance abstraction constraint, (ii) maintains a recursively composed hierarchy in which higher-level skills invoke lower-level ones, (iii) compacts the library via a minimum-description-length (MDL) criterion and behavioral equivalence checking, and (iv) periodically distills skill-augmented trajectories back into model weights to internalize the abstractions. Across WebArena, VisualWebArena, and a held-out split of Online-Mind2Web, \textsc{Scaffold} improves success rate by $11.1$--$17.2$ absolute points over the strongest skill-augmented baseline and shows monotonic gains across five self-improvement iterations without library collapse. We release the code and documents in the Github \href{https://github.com/BokwaiHo/SCAFFOLD}{repository}.

</details>


### [59] [Reasoning-Aware Compression: Identifying and Protecting Vulnerable Reasoning Circuits for Energy-Efficient LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05512)
*Leonard Twagirayezu,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模块脆弱性剖面的推理感知量化框架，通过校准扰动扫描识别敏感电路并恢复为FP16，实现在多个推理任务上更优的能耗—性能折中，发现脆弱性任务/架构相关且INT4有时会增加总体能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是：统一量化可能破坏关键的推理回路，从而不但损害性能还可能因为延长推理链路而增加总体能耗；因此需要推理感知的压缩策略以获得更优的能耗—性能折中。

Method: 在五个推理基准（GSM8K、FOLIO、MATH-500、ProofWriter、MuSiQue）上，使用硬件级GPU能耗测量，针对模型中所有196–224个（层，投影）对在校准划分上做扰动扫描以评估每个模块在INT4下的脆弱性，然后将最敏感的电路恢复为FP16以生成不同的选择性压缩配置并比较能耗—性能折中。

Result: 三项主要发现：1) INT4量化有时会通过延长推理链路导致能耗上升（例如GSM8K上25%功耗下降反而整体能耗上升）；2) 模块对量化的脆弱性依任务和架构而异（数学推理中attention投影更关键，逻辑推理中对不同架构敏感模式不同）；3) 有选择的压缩能达到统一方法不可及的帕累托点（示例：R1-Qwen-7B在ProofWriter上，相比FP16 +12个百分点且能耗下降9.7%），并在五个基准的留出集上验证。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：通过对推理敏感电路进行有选择的保留FP16并对其余模块进行INT4量化，可以在保持或提升推理性能的同时降低能耗，且该选择性压缩能达到统一量化方法无法超过的帕累托最优点。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) impose substantial energy costs during deployment, yet current compression methods apply uniform quantization across all components, risking damage to critical reasoning circuits. We present a reasoning-aware compression framework that benchmarks quantization conditions across five reasoning benchmarks, GSM8K, FOLIO, MATH-500, ProofWriter, and MuSiQue, with hardware-level GPU energy measurement; profiles per-module INT4 vulnerability across all 196-224 (layer, projection) pairs via a perturbation sweep on a held-out calibration split, then selectively restores the most sensitive circuits to FP16. Three findings emerge. First, INT4 quantization can increase energy by extending reasoning chains; a 25% power reduction becomes a net energy increase on GSM8K. Second, vulnerability is task-dependent: attention projections are more critical for mathematical reasoning, and sensitivity patterns differ by architecture in logical inference. Third, selective compression achieves Pareto-optimal points inaccessible to uniform methods: R1-Qwen-7B Top-10% on ProofWriter gains +12 pp over FP16 at -9.7% energy, validated on held-out data across five reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [60] [When and What to Teach: Budget-Aware Online Adaptation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05513)
*Jianwei Zhang,Sihan Cao,Pengcheng Zheng,Ya Wen,Pei Ke,Kuien Liu,Shen Gao,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种预算感知的在线教学框架，通过判断何时询问教师与选择性保留回合，显著降低教师调用与训练成本同时维持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中只能使用轻量本地模型并通过在线教学从强教师进化，但教师调用与交互反馈代价高，传统轨迹级偏好优化会在不可解的轨迹和冗余执行回合上浪费预算，需要有预算感知的教学策略。

Method: 框架包含两部分：1) solvability-aware teacher gate——判断当前轨迹/情形是否可解，从而决定何时向更强的教师发起查询；2) score-guided turn selection——基于評分或置信度挑选并保留信息量高的交互回合，实现轨迹修剪以节约预算与训练量。

Result: 在MiniWoB与TimeWarp上进行大量实验，结果显示在保持Comparable first-pass success的同时，平均减少教师调用22.6%和学生训练计算52.1%；并公开了代码仓库。

Conclusion: 提出了Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming（SG-OTT），通过可解性感知的教师门控与基于分数的回合选择，在保持首轮成功率不降的前提下显著降低教师调用和学生训练计算。

Abstract: Web agents have achieved significant success in automating complex internet tasks but deploying them in real-world environments requires continuous online adaptation. Given that deploying powerful proprietary models remains commercially cost-prohibitive, practitioners must rely on lightweight local models that evolve post-deployment via online teaching from a stronger teacher. However, standard interactive feedback imposes prohibitive costs. We show that conventional trajectory-level preference optimization wastes budget on both unresolvable episodes and redundant execution turns. To resolve these inefficiencies, we propose \textbf{Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming}, a budget-aware framework that systematically orchestrates \textbf{when} and \textbf{what} to teach. Specifically, our framework integrates a solvability-aware teacher gate to dictate \textbf{when} to query the teacher model and a score-guided turn selection mechanism to decide \textbf{what} informative turns to retain. Extensive experiments on MiniWoB and TimeWarp demonstrate that our method achieves comparable first-pass success while reducing teacher calls by 22.6\% and student training compute by 52.1\% on average. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjw131f1fc/budgeted-online-teaching.

</details>


### [61] [The Failure Happens Before the Drift: The Social Dynamics of Values in LLM Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05514)
*Farah Atif,Sougata Saha,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 实验证明当前LLM代理能产出可信对话样本，但不能可靠地代表并维持多样化的人类价值观分布，限制其在社会科学替代被试中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM代理能否作为多文化、价值冲突情形下的人类被试替代品，能否忠实再现并在长期互动中保持指定的价值观配置。

Method: 基于世界价值观调查（WVS）构建的模拟框架，设计1,200个角色、15个话题、约4,000次对话，比较GPT-4o、Gemini-2.5-Flash与Gemma-4-E4B三种模型，评估价值忠实度、价值漂移与对话现实性，并做去人口统计信息的消融实验。

Result: 超过50%的角色从一开始就无法表达其指定的WVS配置，2–7%在重复对话后出现价值漂移；去除人口统计信息对部分模型有益但不能改变总体偏离趋势；模拟对话在风格一致性与语义多样性间与人类对话呈不同的权衡，表现为语义多变但风格重复的交流。

Conclusion: 当前主流大型语言模型代理可以生成看似合理的价值相关对话，但不能稳定地刻画并保持多样化的真实人类价值谱系，因而作为社会科学研究中的被试代理存在显著局限。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used as proxies for human participants in social science research, yet it remains unclear whether they can faithfully simulate diverse and conflicting human value systems. We present a World Values Survey (WVS)-grounded simulation framework where culturally diverse agents with different communication styles engage in longitudinal, value-laden discussions. Across approximately 4,000 conversations involving 1,200 personas, 15 topics, and three models (GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B), we evaluate value faithfulness, value drift, and conversational realism. We find that more than 50\% of personas fail to express their assigned WVS profiles from the outset, while 2-7\% drift after repeated conversations. Ablations removing demographic details improve faithfulness for some models but do not change the broader trend: simulated value distributions still systematically deviate from the assigned WVS profiles. Compared to human discussions, simulated dialogues show a different trade-off between stylistic consistency and semantic diversity, often producing content-wise varied but stylistically repetitive exchanges. These findings suggest that current LLM agents can generate plausible conversations, but remain limited proxies for representing and preserving diverse human value profiles over time.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond "AI Helps Humans": Decision-Targeted Evaluation Design for Human-Agent Teams in the Agentic Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05527)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对有限重放预算下判定人机工作流是否优于‘仅人’和‘仅AI’两个基线，TEAM-Design 给每个任务对每个基线分配独立重放概率，基于可预测性、比较难度与成本调整概率，并证明了理论性质与在若干真实/半合成数据上的经验效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协同系统的部署评估中，必须判断人机组合是否优于“仅人类”与“仅代理”两个替代方案。但实际部署中只有人机工作流的结果可见，恢复任一替代方案需要付出专家时间或算力。有限的重放预算下需要一个能智能分配重放资源以最有效判定‘人机优于两者’的设计规则。

Method: 将部署评价问题形式化为在固定重放预算下对每个任务和每个缺失基线分配重放概率的优化问题；导出一个基于可预测性、不确定性与比较难度及重放成本的两概率规则（TEAM-Design）；给出理论证明（最优性与错误率控制）；在多个真实临床设置、一个编码基准及合成/半合成实验上进行实证检验。

Result: 理论上证明 TEAM-Design 解决了带预算的重放设计并在随机抽样下控制错误宣称概率；实证上在六个临床重分析中没有发现任何人机工作流同时优于两种基线，在一个编码基准中发现一例；在合成与半合成实验中，TEAM-Design 在当两项比较难度显著不对称时表现最好，但在两项难度相当时可能不如方差驱动分配。

Conclusion: 提出的 TEAM-Design 为每个任务分别对“仅人类”和“仅代理”两种缺失基线分配重放概率，从而在有限重放预算下优化应测任务的选择。该规则在预测困难或比较难以确立时提高重放概率，在重放成本高时降低概率，并证明在预算约束下可解该设计问题及通过随机抽样仍控制错误宣称工作流优于两种基线的概率。

Abstract: Wherever a coding agent works under engineer supervision, or a clinical model assists a radiologist, the deployment question is whether to keep the human-AI workflow or replace it with the human alone or the agent alone. The human-AI workflow is worth keeping only if it beats both of those alternatives. Yet once it is deployed, neither alternative outcome is observed: recovering one means replaying the task under that alternative, and every replay costs expert time or compute. Under a fixed replay budget, the design question is therefore which tasks should be more likely to receive a human-only replay, and which an agent-only replay. Existing methods do not directly target this decision. Agent benchmarks do not choose which missing baseline to measure, variance-based sampling ignores which of the two comparisons is closer to failing, and Bayesian information methods focus on learning model parameters instead of making the deployment decision. We propose TEAM-Design, a rule that gives every task two replay probabilities, one per baseline. It raises a probability where the missing baseline outcome is hard to predict from what is already known about the task and where that comparison is harder to establish, and lowers it where replay is expensive. We prove that the rule solves this budgeted design problem, and that drawing the replays at random from recorded probabilities still controls the chance of wrongly declaring that the workflow beats both. We reanalyze 6 clinical settings, where no human-AI workflow beats both alternatives, and a coding benchmark, where one does, then evaluate TEAM-Design on synthetic designs and on a semi-synthetic design built from a real chest X-ray reader study. TEAM-Design works best when one of the two comparisons is clearly harder to settle than the other, and can do worse than variance-based allocation when the two are similarly difficult.

</details>


### [63] [EdgeMem: LLM-Free Agent Memory Construction and Retrieval via Evidence-Preserving Multi-Anchor Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05553)
*Zeyang Cui,Jiannong Cao,Zhiyuan Wen,Bo Yuan,Junlan Feng,Shengyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EdgeMem通过保存原始对话轮次并用内容/时间/情节多锚超图组织它们，实现无需生成式LLM的构建与检索流程，在长会话记忆检索与问答上同时提高效率与保真性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理记忆通常将历史压缩为摘要或由LLM生成的表示，重复生成增加成本并可能在未知未来查询需求时丢失关键信息。需要一种既高效又能忠实保留源交互细节的记忆机制。

Method: 通过轻量本地处理，将原始对话轮次作为节点，用多锚点超图（内容锚、时间锚、情节锚）建立连接和索引；检索直接返回原始证据片段，保留源信息，最终由LLM基于检索结果生成答案。构建与检索过程中不调用生成式LLM。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上取得强检索与基于记忆的问答性能。在LoCoMo上，EdgeMem在共享提示下实现严格评审分数最高（61.01 vs. 最接近的58.70），且其构建与检索阶段无需生成式LLM调用，降低了计算成本与不确定性。

Conclusion: EdgeMem主张保留原始交互轮次并通过内容、时间和情节线索组织它们，从而为代理记忆提供高保真且高效的基础。该方法避免使用生成式LLM进行记忆构建与检索，仅在最终回答时调用LLM，实验显示在多项长上下文评测上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Agent memory allows LLM agents to use earlier interactions when answering new queries. Existing methods often compress interaction histories into summaries or other LLM-generated representations. Repeated generation adds cost and can discard answer-bearing details before the system knows what a future query will require. We propose EdgeMem, an agent-memory method built around a simple principle: preserve original interaction turns and organize them through complementary content, temporal, and episodic cues. EdgeMem realizes this principle with a multi-anchor hypergraph constructed by lightweight local processing. Retrieval directly returns source evidence and reserves LLM use for final answer generation, combining structured access to multi-session histories with faithful retention of the original conversation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show strong retrieval and memory-grounded question answering; on LoCoMo, EdgeMem achieves the highest strict-judge score among seven reproduced systems under a shared prompt (61.01 versus 58.70), while construction and retrieval require no generative-LLM calls. Overall, EdgeMem shows that preserving and organizing source evidence provides an effective and efficient foundation for agent memory without generative memory management.

</details>


### [64] [Deep belief networks are exact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05572)
*Gleb Smirnov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文给出存在性证明：任意严格正的二元向量分布都能被有限参数的sigmoid信念网络精确表示，证明依赖于将概率共享近似通过Brouwer不动点定理提升为精确表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Sutskever和Hinton提出的关于sigmoid信念网络能否精确表示任意严格正分布的问题，澄清这类模型的表示能力。

Method: 在Sutskever和Hinton提出的概率共享（probability‑sharing）近似框架基础上，通过引入Brouwer不动点定理，将原来的近似表示升级为精确表示，从而得到存在性证明。

Result: 存在性结果：对任意严格正的离散分布，在有限参数的sigmoid信念网络下能被精确表示。证明使用了不动点存在性（Brouwer定理）来完成参数的构造性（存在性）论证。

Conclusion: 本文证明了对任意在\{-1,1\}^n上的严格正概率分布，都存在一个具有有限参数的sigmoid信念网络可以精确表示该分布。

Abstract: We prove that every strictly positive probability distribution on \(\{-1,1\}^n\) is represented exactly by a sigmoid belief network with finite parameters. This answers a question of Sutskever and Hinton. The proof upgrades their probability-sharing approximation to exact representation using Brouwer's fixed-point theorem.

</details>


### [65] [EnvCraft: Synthesizing Executable Environments in Agentic RL for Claw-like Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05576)
*Yirong Zeng,Shen You,Jinhang Feng,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Yutai Hou,Hao Cong,Yuxian Wang,Wu Ning,Wang Xu,Bibo Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过自动合成可执行沙箱环境与拓扑感知任务生成，EnvCraft 为 Agentic RL 提供可扩展训练数据，显著提高Qwen系列模型在Claw与工具使用任务上的性能并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成环境多为工具调用端点，不足以支持端到端、具状态性的长时任务训练；Agentic RL 需要更多真实感、可执行的环境样本以提升长期决策与工具交互能力。

Method: 设计了环境合成引擎以构建沙箱隔离工作空间，并结合拓扑感知的数据生成引擎合成连贯的任务轨迹；总体合成了139个交互环境与约2万条复杂任务，用于训练 Qwen3/3.5（8B–32B）规模模型并在Claw风格与通用工具使用基准上评估。

Result: 在多尺度Qwen模型上，方法带来最高+11.9%（Claw-style基准）与+8.0%（通用工具使用基准）的性能提升，并在推理时降低了token代价，表明合成可执行环境既提升效果又提高效率。

Conclusion: EnvCraft 提出通过自动合成可执行环境与拓扑感知的数据生成，以缓解 Agentic RL 缺乏交互式训练环境的问题，实验证明合成环境能为Claw-like智能体提供有益且可泛化的学习信号。

Abstract: The paradigm of LLMs has rapidly shifted from passive language interfaces to autonomous Claw-like agents that execute long-horizon tasks across stateful workspaces. While Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) provides a promising path to optimize these agents, its scaling is heavily bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of interactive training environments. Existing synthetic environments are strictly limited to tool-calling endpoints, rendering them insufficient for accommodating the end-to-end real-world demands of claw-like agents. To bridge this gap, we introduce EnvCraft, an automated framework for synthesizing executable environments and scalable training data. Specifically, EnvCraft employs an environment synthesis engine to build sandbox-isolated workspaces, alongside a topology-aware data generation engine to produce coherent task trajectories. Overall, we synthesize 139 interactive environments comprising approximately 20K complex tasks for Agentic RL training. Experiments on Qwen3/3.5 models (8B-32B) show that our method yields gains of up to +11.9% on Claw-style benchmarks and +8.0% on general tool-use benchmarks, with concurrent reductions in inference token cost. The results confirm that synthesized executable environments provide robust and generalizable learning signals for training.

</details>


### [66] [Planning and Scheduling Business Processes under Control-Flow Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05578)
*Michel Kunkler,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对具有不确定分支决策的业务流程活动调度问题，利用历史日志估计的概率信息，将问题建模为机会约束优化，并提出两种求解方式：分解的两阶段方法（先规划以最小化期望多余活动并满足成功率约束，再对规划的活动调度以最小化完工时间）和集成方法（将规划与调度合并）。实验表明：集成方法在完工时间上更优，但难以扩展；分解方法可扩展到大规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程中活动序列受运行时基于数据的决策影响，导致路径不确定。历史执行日志可提供决策概率，利用这些概率提前规划能提高调度效率、降低浪费并保证较高的成功完成概率。

Method: 将路径不确定性与成功率约束通过机会约束（chance-constrained）引入模型；给出两种数学表述：①分解式：规划阶段最小化期望的多余活动（在可行性概率约束下），调度阶段固定规划结果最小化makespan；②集成式：单一优化问题同时考虑可行性、期望多余活动与makespan。用两份真实数据集和一份合成数据集进行对比实验。

Result: 实验结果显示：集成式通常产生更短的makespan，但随着问题规模增长计算不可行；分解式牺牲部分最优性换取可扩展性，在大规模实例上能有效求解。

Conclusion: 集成式在解的质量（makespan）上优于分解式，但计算不可行时规模受限；分解式在可扩展性与实用性之间取得更好平衡，是大规模场景下的首选。

Abstract: Scheduling activities in business processes can improve efficiency (e.g., reduce makespan), but is challenging because the exact sequence of activities required to complete a case is often uncertain due to decisions based on data that emerges during execution. Nevertheless, probabilistic information regarding such decisions can often be estimated or derived from historical execution logs, and can help anticipate which execution paths are likely to lead to successful completion. Planning with particular execution paths affects feasibility, i.e., the probability of successful completion, and the expected number of superfluous activities that are planned but never executed. We frame the problem as a chance-constrained optimization problem and present two formulations: A decomposed approach with two stages, a planning stage that minimizes the expected number of superfluous activities subject to a feasibility constraint, and a scheduling stage that minimizes the makespan over the planned activities; and an integrated approach that combines planning and scheduling into a single formulation.
