Conclusion: 闭环先验选择把LLM蒸馏图的使用从手工试错变成可验证的预算化选择问题，能在保证无退化的前提下显著提高PFN的实域表现，且改进主要由图的语义内容驱动。

Abstract: Causal effect estimation asks how an outcome would change under an intervention, and medicine, economics, and public policy all treat it as a foundational task. Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) amortize the task: a model trained on large numbers of programmatically generated synthetic causal tasks reads a new problem's observational data into context and returns an interventional-effect estimate in a single forward pass. The capability of such models is largely determined by the synthetic training prior, which is currently designed by hand, a bottleneck acknowledged by both Do-PFN and CausalPFN. Large language models (LLMs) can now ``draw'' plausible causal graphs for a given domain, suggesting that LLM-distilled graphs could serve as prior material. Whether injecting such graphs helps at all, where any gain comes from, and when injection helps. Practice has so far relied on manual trial and error. We propose a \emph{closed-loop prior selection framework} that casts prior injection as a budget-constrained optimization over a candidate prior pool. Candidates undergo cheap post-training and are scored by a composite metric dominated by real-domain generalization; the winner then receives full training and paired statistical validation. On a 7.34M-parameter Do-PFN, the framework's winner attains a formally significant $2.75\times$ gain on the primary evaluation domain, and its error falls below that of the uninjected official base. Generalization on an adjacent monitoring domain improves significantly, and no monitored capability degrades. Mechanism experiments show that the gain depends on the semantic content of the distilled graph rather than its structural diversity alone does not produce it (directional evidence). With this framework and this regularity in hand, the use of LLM causal priors stops being manual trial and error and becomes an empirically verifiable selection problem.

</details>


### [132] [iBrain: A Unified Foundation Model Reading the Brain from Surface to Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06960)
*Ying Chen,Tiou Wang,Zhifeng Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iBrain is a unified foundation model pretrained on heterogeneous invasive neural recordings (iEEG + spiking) using modality-specific encoders and a shared Transformer, yielding SOTA results, better transfer, and data efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing invasive neural foundation models are developed separately per recording modality; a unified model could leverage complementary information across modalities and improve transferability and scalability.

Method: Use signal-specific encoders for iEEG and spiking modalities feeding into a shared spatiotemporal Transformer backbone; pretraining objectives are masked signal reconstruction and channel-view alignment; trained on over 7,000 hours of heterogeneous invasive recordings.

Result: iBrain outperforms single-modality pretraining baselines, achieves state-of-the-art on multiple benchmarks, and demonstrates transferability and improved data efficiency across recording settings and downstream tasks.

Conclusion: Joint pretraining across iEEG and spiking activity produces more transferable and data-efficient neural representations, leading to improved downstream performance compared to single-signal pretraining.

Abstract: Invasive neural recordings provide high-fidelity measurements of brain activity, with signals such as intracranial EEG (iEEG) and intracortical spiking activity capturing neural dynamics at different spatial and temporal scales. Yet existing neural foundation models have largely been developed independently for different invasive recording paradigms, leaving joint pretraining across heterogeneous invasive signals underexplored. In this work, we introduce iBrain, a unified foundation model that jointly learns from iEEG and spiking activity. iBrain employs signal-specific encoders to accommodate their distinct signal characteristics and a shared spatiotemporal Transformer backbone to model dependencies across recording channels and time. We pretrain iBrain on over 7,000 hours of heterogeneous neural recordings using masked signal reconstruction and channel-view alignment, promoting contextual modeling of neural dynamics and robustness across different channels. iBrain consistently outperforms single-signal pretraining baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. Further experiments demonstrate that iBrain exhibits transferability and data efficiency across diverse recording settings. These results highlight the potential of joint pretraining on heterogeneous invasive neural recordings to support scalable neural modeling and transferable representations across recording settings and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [133] [SSP-DMGTimeNet: Physics-Constrained Learning for Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction of Vehicle Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06961)
*Yuhang Wang,Kailang Ma,Zirui Li,Mingfeng Fan,Kitae Jang,Changju Lee,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSP-DMGTimeNet adds delay-aware causal attention and time/frequency string-stability losses to spatiotemporal prediction, producing more realistic, string-stable platoon forecasts with strong generalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing learning-based car-following/trajectory predictors optimize pointwise trajectory error but ignore realistic upstream-to-downstream disturbance propagation, producing accurate yet string-unstable predictions.

Method: A neural framework combining multi-scale temporal representations and cross-vehicle interaction features, augmented with a propagation-delay-aware causal attention module that learns inter-vehicle response delays and accumulates them downstream; training is regularized by time- and frequency-domain string-stability losses.

Result: On HighD, unstable-window rate 0.65% for 5-vehicle platoons and max head-to-tail amplification 0.898 on an excitation subset, while keeping competitive trajectory errors. Zero-shot on NGSIM US-101 and I-80 yielded velocity MAEs 1.316 m/s and 1.252 m/s and unstable-window rates 3.90% and 4.10%, demonstrating improved disturbance propagation realism without sacrificing accuracy.

Conclusion: The proposed SSP-DMGTimeNet successfully integrates physics-inspired constraints into data-driven trajectory prediction to produce predictions that are both accurate and string-stable, reducing unrealistic disturbance amplification in vehicle platoons.

Abstract: Existing car-following prediction methods mainly optimize trajectory accuracy, while rarely considering whether predicted disturbances propagate realistically along a vehicle platoon. This limitation may lead to accurate but string-unstable predictions. We propose SSP-DMGTimeNet, a physics-constrained learning framework for spatiotemporal trajectory prediction of vehicle platoons. The model combines multi-scale temporal representations with cross-vehicle interaction features to capture complex and time-varying platoon dynamics. A propagation-delay-aware causal attention mechanism explicitly models upstream-to-downstream disturbance propagation by learning response delays between adjacent vehicles and accumulating them along the platoon. In addition, time- and frequency-domain string-stability losses relieve disturbance amplification across both adjacent vehicles and arbitrary sub-platoons during training. Experiments on HighD show that SSP-DMGTimeNet achieves an unstable-window rate of 0.65\% for five-vehicle platoons and a maximum head-to-tail amplification of 0.898 on the ground-truth excitation subset, while maintaining competitive trajectory prediction performance. In zero-shot evaluation on NGSIM US-101 and I-80, the model achieves velocity MAEs of 1.316~m/s and 1.252~m/s, with unstable-window rates of 3.90\% and 4.10\%, respectively. These results demonstrate that incorporating platoon-level physical constraints can effectively balance trajectory prediction accuracy and disturbance propagation stability.

</details>


### [134] [RedKnot-MLA: Multi-Head Offline-Online Reuse for DeepSeek-V4 Long-Context Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07008)
*Yang Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Huayi Jin,Ruozhou He,Chenchen Hong,Mingxiao Ma,Biao Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Boyu Wang,Guanjie Chen,Yifei Liu,Tao Xie,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSeek-V4 restores head-aware reuse for packed MLA by precomputing local-head contributions offline and repairing positions online via RoPE relocation, achieving large arithmetic savings and 2–4x hot-cache speedups at 256K context, but some dataset variability and missing raw traces limit reproducibility.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA is memory efficient by packing latent KV, but that packing removes per-head cache boundaries relied on by conventional head-wise reuse. The motivation is to regain reuse benefits (lower compute at serving) for long-context models without breaking packed-latent memory efficiency.

Method: Process immutable documents offline at canonical position zero to certify and store Local-head contributions (MLA-Off). At serving, relocate positional embeddings (RoPE relocation) to restore request positions, recompute a small Global-head set and protected Local token rows (MLA-Online), and merge both paths into a single shared output projection while never unpacking the packed MLA latent. Support for sparse MoE and TP8 is described; configurations use specific Local/Global partitions and layer/head counts.

Result: Reported outcomes: DeepSeek-V4-Flash (37 reusable layers, 56/8 Local/Global) gives a 75.29% analytic logical head-row ceiling; Pro-0813 (55 layers, 112/16) gives 78.89%. Frozen Flash shows TTFT speedups 2.02–3.84x. At 256K, aggregated three-dataset study: +3.24 F1 points, +4.16 EM points, and 78.7–79.5% analytic major-operator arithmetic savings; one dataset had −2.81 F1. A separate author-reported 256K hot-artifact QPS ≈2.0x is labeled preliminary due to missing raw concurrency trace.

Conclusion: This paper proposes DeepSeek-V4, a system that implements head-aware reuse for Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) by splitting computation into offline "MLA-Off" local-head contributions at canonical position zero and online "MLA-Online" reconstruction using RoPE relocation plus a small global-head set; merged without unpacking the packed MLA latent. The approach yields substantial analytic and end-to-end speedups and arithmetic savings at long-context (256K) operating points, with mixed downstream accuracy changes.

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) exposes many logical query heads through one packed latent KV stream. This representation is memory efficient, but it removes the physical per-head cache boundary assumed by conventional head-wise reuse. We present our system, a DeepSeek-V4 realization of RedKnot's head-aware reuse principle. Each immutable document is processed offline at canonical position zero; certified Local-head contributions are retained as MLA-Off. At serving time, query-side RoPE relocation restores the document's request position, a small Global-head set and protected Local token rows are recomputed as MLA-Online, and the two paths are merged before a single shared output projection. The packed MLA latent is never split. DeepSeek-V4-Flash uses 37 reusable layers and a 56/8 Local/Global partition, giving a 75.29% analytic logical head-row ceiling; the Pro-0813 profile uses 55 layers and 112/16 heads, giving 78.89%. Frozen Flash operating points show hot-artifact TTFT speedups of 2.02-3.84x. At 256K, the archived three-dataset study reports an aggregate F1 change of +3.24 percentage points, an EM change of +4.16 points, and a 78.7-79.5% analytic major-operator arithmetic saving, while one dataset decreases by 2.81 F1 points. A separate author-reported 256K hot-artifact QPS measurement is approximately 2.0x; because its raw concurrency trace is not included in this bundle, we mark it as preliminary rather than archived evidence. We describe the factorization, position repair, token-row closure, sparse-MoE support, TP8 integration, and the measurement boundaries needed to interpret these results.

</details>


### [135] [Beyond One-Shot Expansion: Contrastive Evidence Exploration for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07050)
*JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一套无需额外训练的多跳检索流水线：离线构建段落级对比特征，在线以生成探针驱动迭代检索并用对比面精化与覆盖感知选取互补证据，从而改善多跳QA的检索与问答效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳QA要求逐步发现通过中间实体/关系连接的支持证据，现有方法通常仅用单一检索意图或一次性扩展查询，难以适应新证据并可能引入噪声或冗余。需一种能随检索进展动态生成探针并对相似候选进行区分以实现互补覆盖的方案。

Method: 离线构建每个段落的对比面（相对于语义相近邻居的细粒度表征）；在线迭代检索—生成探针—通过对比面精化候选相关性—采用覆盖感知的互补集合选择。整个流程为训练无关，依赖于生成器（探针）与对比面检索/重排序模块。

Result: 在公开多跳数据集上，提出方法在检索质量指标和下游QA指标均有稳定提升；证明对比面有助于区分紧邻语义候选，探针驱动的迭代检索能发现链式证据，覆盖感知排序提高证据集合的互补性。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练无关的多跳检索框架通过证据条件化探测、基于段落的对比精化与覆盖感知的最终排序，有效提升了检索质量并下游QA性能，在MuSiQue、HotpotQA与2WikiMultihopQA上均表现优于基线。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) critically depends on retrieving the evidence necessary for effective reasoning. However, this remains particularly challenging in multi-hop question answering (QA), where supporting passages are often linked through intermediate entities and relations that must be progressively uncovered. Existing retrieval approaches typically rely on a single retrieval intent or one-shot query expansion, limiting their ability to adapt to newly retrieved evidence and potentially introducing noisy or redundant retrieval signals. To address these limitations, we propose a training-free multi-hop retrieval framework that integrates evidence-conditioned exploration, passage-specific contrastive refinement, and coverage-aware final ranking. During offline indexing, the framework constructs passage-specific contrastive facets that characterize each passage relative to its semantically similar neighbors, providing fine-grained signals to distinguish closely related candidates. At inference time, the framework iteratively retrieves evidence, generates probes targeting unresolved information needs, refines candidate relevance using the contrastive facets, and selects a complementary set of passages that collectively cover diverse evidence-seeking intents. Experiments on MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultihopQA demonstrate consistent improvements in retrieval quality and downstream QA performance over baselines.

</details>


### [136] [VST: Verifiable Structured Transport for Auditable Agent-to-Agent Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07065)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把 A2A 自由文本手交变为有类型、因果可重放的记录流，并在其上用单一预测器+事务性 verify–leap 控制器进行多周期推测提交；结构化主要带来可审计与可回滚性，而非提高准确率；在 CSI1000 单次实验中，本方法在若干因子指标上表现最好，但超额回报仍为负且结果受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当前大模型多代理系统中，挖矿(agent)与评估(agent)间反复反馈的交接以自由文本进行，缺乏稳定契约、无法确定重放，导致 A2A alpha 发现过程缓慢且不可审计。目标是通过协议化通信获得可模式检查、可重放、可审计并可安全进行多周期投机提交的能力，从而提高系统工程性质（可验证性与安全回滚），而非单纯追求更高预测精度。

Method: 1) 把 A2A 手交从自由文本改为“有类型、因果可寻址、单播记录”的结构化协议；2) 在提交的因果轨迹上训练/使用一个带有四个类型化输出头的单一预测器，预测未来若干周期内矿工将接收的累积指引；3) 采用一个事务性 verify–leap 控制器，具备四级门控：仅在通过所有门时提交多周期的推测结果，否则回滚到先前精确状态；4) 做受控消融：用等信息量的自由文本通道对比预测器命中率以验证“类型化”对预测准确率的影响；5) 在 CSI1000 数据集上进行单次运行评估，并报告粗略无成本结果。

Result: 消化后的实证要点：1) 消融表明：在相同信息量下，自由文本通道与结构化通道在预测器命中率上相当；2) 结构化带来的是可 schema 校验、确定性重放和防止预测泄露给评估者的设计性保障；3) 在 CSI1000 持出集上，本方法为八种方法中唯一保持正的因子层面中位年化收益与 Sharpe，但相对于基准的中位超额回报仍为负；4) 开发期挑选出的前20只组合在持出集的中位 Sharpe 达 0.71；5) 结果为单次运行、未扣交易成本、未分离 leap 机械效应，作者在文中反复强调这些限制。

Conclusion: 将代理间通信重构为“有类型、因果可寻址、点对点记录”的结构化协议，使得提交的流形成因果轨迹；在该轨迹上，单一预测器（含四个类型化头）预测数个周期后的累积指引；一个事务性 verify–leap 控制器通过四级门仅在满足条件时提交多周期推测结果，否则精确回滚。结构化的主要贡献在于可审核性、可重放性和泄露防护，而非提高预测准确率；消融实验显示等信息量的自由文本通道在预测命中率上与之相当。实证上，在 CSI1000 持出集中，本方法为八种方法中唯一在因子层面保持正的中位年化收益率和 Sharpe，但相对于基准的中位超额收益对所有方法均为负；开发期选择的前20只组合在持出集达到中位 Sharpe 0.71。作者对结果的局限性（单次运行、未剥离 leap 机制效果、未扣成本等）表述明确。

Abstract: Agent-to-agent (A2A) alpha discovery is slowed by repeated feedback cycles between mining and evaluation agents, whose hand-offs, in contemporary LLM multi-agent systems, are free-form natural-language messages that carry no stable contract and cannot be replayed. We first restructure this communication as a structured agent-to-agent protocol of \emph{typed, causally addressable, unicast records}, so that the committed stream forms a causal trajectory. On that trajectory a single predictor with four typed heads forecasts the accumulated guidance the two miners would receive several cycles ahead; a transactional verify--leap controller then commits a multi-cycle speculative outcome only when it passes a four-level gate, and otherwise rolls back to the exact prior state. Structure is the enabling contribution, and its value is not accuracy. A controlled ablation shows an equal-information free-text channel reaches the same predictor hit rate. What typing provides is a state that can be schema-checked, replayed deterministically, and prevented by construction from leaking a forecast to an evaluator: auditability by construction, not an empirically stress-tested guarantee. On a CSI~1000 out-of-sample holdout, our single run is the only one among eight methods (seven baselines and ours) to hold a positive median annualized return and Sharpe at the factor level, though the median return \emph{in excess} of the benchmark stays negative for every method including ours; its development-selected top-20 portfolios reach a $0.71$ median holdout Sharpe, selected on a split inside the optimization horizon. We report these single-run results descriptively, gross of costs, and are explicit about their limits throughout; in particular we do not isolate the effect of the leap machinery from the inherited search substrate, which we leave to future work.

</details>


### [137] [A Hierarchical Consistency Framework for Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07075)
*Ramon Gonzalez,Antonio Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HCF，一种对RAG进行事后、模型无关的三层一致性审计，输出带来源的原子事实冲突与答案一致性分数，能暴露检索上下文中隐藏的矛盾证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统通常仅通过最终答案的正确性来评估，这忽视了生成答案时所用检索上下文中可能存在的直接矛盾，导致仅看答案或检索相似度无法发现证据层面的冲突。

Method: 对RAG输出进行事后分析：将语料库内容抽象为源链接的原子事实，检索到的上下文与这些原子事实比对以识别支持或矛盾语句，计算每个答案的答案一致性分数（ACS），并在人类评估的受控语料与查询集（5个领域、100个实例）上验证诊断能力。

Result: 提出了层级一致性框架（HCF），作为对RAG流程的事后、模型无关审计，覆盖知识语料库、最终检索语境和生成答案三个层次。HCF将语料库冲突表示为带来源链接的原子事实，并为每个答案返回答案一致性分数（ACS）及其支持/矛盾语句的解释。在5个领域、100个查询-语料实例的受控实验中，发现三个诊断层可以相互区分：高检索相似度的语料库可能有最低的ACS；结构降级的语料库在语料层表现差但在答案层表现更好；且HCF能识别出若干在答案仍匹配参考情况下的检索到的矛盾证据。HCF并不证明事实真伪，而是使支持和挑战答案的证据可检视并可归因。

Conclusion: HCF能有效揭示RAG系统中被答案掩盖的证据级矛盾，增强可审计性和可归因性，但不等同于事实验证，其效果依赖于原子事实抽取、冲突识别策略和人类评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly evaluated by whether the final answer is correct. That test is insufficient: an answer can match its reference while the context that produced it contains a direct contradiction, leaving the contested evidence invisible to answer-only review and retrieval relevance scores. This paper presents the Hierarchical Consistency Framework (HCF), a post-hoc, model-agnostic audit of three distinct levels of a RAG process: the knowledge corpus, the final retrieved context, and the generated answer. HCF represents corpus conflicts as source-linked atomic facts, thereby identifying the documents responsible, and returns each Answer Consistency Score (ACS) with an explanation of supporting and contradictory contextual statements. We evaluate HCF on several controlled corpora spanning five domains and 100 query-corpus instances. A human evaluator compares every generated response with its supplied ground-truth response. The results show that the three diagnostic levels can dissociate: the corpus with the highest mean retrieval similarity has the lowest mean ACS, while a structurally degraded corpus performs worse at corpus level but better at answer level. Most importantly, HCF identifies contradictory retrieved evidence in several cases where the answer still matches the ground truth. HCF does not certify factual truth; it makes the evidence supporting and challenging an answer inspectable and attributable.

</details>


### [138] [Risk Is Not Review Value: Wrong-Answer Exposure Under Bounded Review Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07095)
*SangJin Park,Myungsub Choi,Jineok Kim,Minseung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以“暴露减少”为目标的审核优先级建模与PRRE评估，证明在受限审核预算下按错误概率+可修复性等排序能更有效地减少用户暴露于错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助理产生的回答数量常常超过人类可审核的能力。传统评估只检测是否错误或是否低置信，但在限额审核下更重要的是“先审哪几条”，因为高风险回答不一定容易修复，而中等风险但易修复的回答可能更值得优先检查。

Method: 将生成答案的审核优先级视为“暴露减少”问题，定义审核价值为错误概率、可干预性（affordance）、影响和成本的组合；提出并采用两项评估指标：错误答案暴露比（WAER）和后修复残余暴露（PRRE）；使用不复用排序用到的affordance分数的确定性修复规则来计算PRRE；在720条TAT-QA/SciFact基准上比较基于review-value的排序与其他方法。

Result: 在720条TAT-QA/SciFact压力测试上，当审核预算为20%时，基于review-value的排序使答案级WAER几乎不变（0.605 vs. 0.600），但将PRRE从0.881降低到0.716；说明该方法能在相同被检出错误率下，显著减少经确定性基准修复后仍暴露给用户的错误。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：在有限人工审核预算下，仅按“风险”排序不足以最大化减少用户暴露于错误答案的概率；将审核优先级建模为“暴露减少”（结合错误可能性、可修复性/干预可行性、影响和成本）并以此排序，可以显著降低在可用自动/基准修复后仍残留的暴露错误（PRRE），即更有效地用有限审核资源减少被用户看到的错误。

Abstract: LLM assistants often produce more answers than humans can review before users see them. Most evaluations ask whether an answer is wrong, unsupported, or low-confidence. Bounded review budgets instead ask which answers should be checked first under a fixed review budget. Risk alone is not enough: a high-risk answer may be hard to repair, while a moderately risky answer may be directly correctable from available evidence. For generated-answer evaluation, we model review prioritization as exposure reduction, where review value combines estimated wrongness, intervention affordance, impact, and cost. We evaluate review queues with Wrong-Answer Exposure Ratio (WAER), the fraction of wrong answers left unreviewed, and post-repair residual exposure (PRRE), the fraction still exposed after deterministic benchmark-supported repairs. PRRE uses repairability rules that do not numerically reuse the affordance scores used for ranking. On a 720-item TAT-QA/SciFact stress benchmark, review-value ranking keeps answer-level WAER nearly unchanged at 20% budget (0.605 vs. 0.600) but lowers PRRE from 0.881 to 0.716. These results show that trustworthy LLM evaluation should measure not only error detection, but also how limited review capacity reduces exposed wrong answers.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Sparse Rewards: A New Benchmark and Structure-Aware Graph Alignment for Micro-Drama Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07107)
*Yixin Qin,Shi-Zhe Chen,Zhiqi Yu,Siyuan Cheng,Tao Cheng,Jinwen Luo,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建大规模双语微短剧基准M-Drama，并提出结构感知图匹配奖励SAGA，通过语义三元组与时序结构匹配提升VLM对微短剧的理解与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微短剧时长极短、情节高度密集，现有视频理解基准无法覆盖其人物身份识别与细粒度时序推理的需求；现有用于强化学习的奖励信号稀疏且表层化，无法准确捕捉复杂叙事中的角色关系与时间结构。

Method: 构建含35K+实例、9,138个视频片段的双语微短剧数据集；将叙事建模为异构图（节点代表角色/事件/实体，边表示关系与时序）；提出SAGA奖励函数，通过语义三元组匹配与结构—时间匹配两条解耦通路实现稠密评分；基于该奖励对VLM（Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct）进行强化学习或奖励优化并与若干基线比较。

Result: SAGA在开放式回答准确率和摘要质量上取得实质性提升；奖励信号更稠密、更能反映角色与时序一致性；实验还显示在域外数据上保持竞争力。数据集与代码已开源。

Conclusion: 提出了首个大规模双语微短剧理解基准M-Drama，并设计了结构感知图匹配奖励SAGA，从语义三元组与时序结构两个层面进行解耦匹配。实验显示在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，SAGA较现有度量与基线在开放式问答准确率与摘要质量上有显著提升，且保持了较好的域外泛化能力。代码与数据集公开。

Abstract: Micro-dramas, characterized by ultra-short durations and hyper-dense storylines, pose unique challenges for video understanding that conventional benchmarks fail to address. To bridge this gap, we introduce M-Drama, the first large-scale bilingual benchmark for micro-drama comprehension, featuring over 35K instances across 9,138 clips. Furthermore, while reinforcement learning can enhance VLMs on complex narratives, existing reward metrics often suffer from sparse and superficial signals, failing to capture intricate character identities and temporal structures. We propose SAGA (Structure-Aware Graph Alignment), a novel graph-matching reward function that models narratives as heterogeneous graphs. SAGA computes dense, rigorous rewards via decoupled semantic triplet and structural temporal matching. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct demonstrate that SAGA outperforms existing baselines, delivering substantial improvements in open-ended accuracy and summary quality, while maintaining competitive out-of-domain generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/qyx1121/MDrama_SAGA.

</details>


### [140] [EEG-Driven Decoding Framework for Passenger Hazard Perception in Highly Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07128)
*Yingkai Yang,Ashton Yu Xuan Tan,Bowen Li,Xiaorong Gao,Sifa Zheng,Jianqiang Wang,Xinyu Gu,Yang Zhao,Yuxin Zhang,Sharon X. Huang,Tania Stathaki,Jun Li,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将乘客EEG作为辅助监督，利用3D-CRNN+RSL等方法在风险预测与危险识别上取得高BA，且在跨会话与跨主体上展现一定泛化能力，但未见被试上的性能下降提示迁移仍有空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶风险评估仍不可靠，利用乘客的脑电作为非侵入性辅助信号可在不要求主动干预的情况下提升客观与主观安全性。论文旨在将乘客认知信号融入AV决策与SOTIF支持中。

Method: 提出了Passenger Cognitive Model (PCM)、Risk-aware Sequential Labeling (RSL) 与 Passenger EEG Decoding Strategy (PEDS)，并以3D-CRNN作为联合时空解码器对乘客EEG进行端到端分类。方法包含事件级的风险预测（RP）与危险识别（DI）两种任务，并在训练中显式建模乘客作为真实交通场景中的被动观测者。

Result: 3D-CRNN在RP任务上达成BA=95.3%±2.7%；在DI任务上通过RSL使单被试准确率从80.9%±3.9%提升到85.0%±3.2%。跨会话DI BA=77.0%±5.3%；已见被试跨主体BA=77.4%±1.1%；未见被试BA=64.9%±8.5%。事件级分析显示3D-CRNN优于对比模型。

Conclusion: 工作开辟了基于乘客认知信号辅助AV风险评估的可行路径：方法在受控实验中表现优异，展示了对SOTIF与决策支持的潜力；但需要更多关于数据量、预处理、延时、真实行驶场景鲁棒性、标注细则与可解释性的验证与说明以增强实用性与可推广性。

Abstract: Reliable risk assessment remains a central challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs). Despite advances in automation, passenger cognition provides a non-intrusive auxiliary signal that improves both objective and perceived safety without requiring active human intervention. We introduce an Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) that decodes passenger neural responses for both Risk Prediction (RP) and Danger Identification (DI), explicitly modeling humans as passengers to match real-world AV use. To achieve this, we propose the Passenger Cognitive Model (PCM), Risk-aware Sequential Labeling (RSL), and the Passenger EEG Decoding Strategy (PEDS), which integrates a 3D Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network (3D-CRNN) model for joint EEG decoding. Experimental results show that 3D-CRNN achieves a Balanced Accuracy (BA) of $95.3\% \pm 2.7\%$ in RP and improves single-subject DI from $80.9\% \pm 3.9\%$ to $85.0\% \pm 3.2\%$ with RSL. Event-wise analyses further show that 3D-CRNN consistently outperforms other models across different event types in RP and DI. In generalization experiments, 3D-CRNN achieves $77.0\% \pm 5.3\%$ BA in cross-session DI and $77.4\% \pm 1.1\%$ BA on seen subjects in cross-subject evaluation, while maintaining a $64.9\% \pm 8.5\%$ BA on unseen subjects, demonstrating promising generalizability and transferability across both intra-subject and inter-subject variability. These findings establish an Electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding framework for AV passenger hazard perception and suggest that passenger cognitive signals can provide auxiliary supervision for future AV decision-making and Safety of the Intended Functionality (SOTIF) support.

</details>


### [141] [Encoded Early, Used Late: Where Transformers Begin to Act on an Inferred Partner's Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07139)
*Mika Okamoto,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Partner expertise is represented early but used late in a transformer: readable long before it becomes causally active, with a clear layer where representation-to-use transition occurs, so interventions must target layers past that transition.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To determine whether the known gap between where attributes are readable and where they causally influence outputs (observed for input-stated attributes) also applies to an attribute that must be inferred gradually during dialogue (partner expertise), and to bound where interventions must act to read or steer partner-conditioned behavior.

Method: Used ExpertCollab corpus of multi-turn dialogues where personas vary in expertise; probed residual stream activations across layers for linear decodability of partner expertise; performed counterfactual patching by injecting expertise differences at different layers and measuring effect on fixed late-layer readout; included content-matched random control, probe-free diagnostic, and a static control attribute; ran experiments on one model with a synthetic corpus for initial demonstration.

Result: Partner expertise is most decodable in early layers and drops to near chance before midpoint; causal influence on outputs appears only after the midpoint. Counterfactual patching shows injections at peak-decoding layers have minimal effect on late readout, but injections past midpoint propagate almost fully. Control experiments place the transition at the same early layer; a statically specified attribute remains decodable throughout. The study used one model and synthetic corpus as a preliminary demonstration.

Conclusion: An inferred relational attribute (partner expertise) is linearly decodable early in transformer layers but does not causally affect outputs until much later; injecting the attribute at the early peak of decodability has little effect on late readouts, while injecting it after the midpoint largely propagates, indicating a large separation between representation and causal use.

Abstract: A transformer can make an attribute linearly decodable in its residual stream at a depth where that attribute does not yet influence the output. This gap between where information is readable and where it is used has been shown for attributes stated directly in the input. We ask whether it also holds for an attribute the model must infer gradually over a conversation, namely how expert its dialogue partner is. Using ExpertCollab, a corpus of multi-turn research-planning dialogues between model-played personas at four expertise levels, we find that partner expertise is most decodable in the early layers and falls to near chance before the midpoint of the network. Counterfactual patching shows that injecting the expertise difference at the layer of peak decodability barely changes a fixed late-layer readout, whereas the same difference injected past the midpoint propagates almost completely, a separation of more than an order of magnitude. A content-matched random control and a probe-free diagnostic place the transition at the same early layer, and a statically specified control attribute stays decodable throughout. An inferred relational attribute is therefore represented well before it becomes causally active, which bounds where any attempt to read out or steer partner-conditioned behavior must intervene. We use one model on a synthetic corpus as an initial demonstration.

</details>


### [142] [An Auditable Symbolic-RAG-Generative AI Architecture for Goal-Oriented Conversation Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07152)
*Ramon Gonzalez,Antonio Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个以符号状态为核心、结合RAG和生成策略的目标导向对话引擎GRACE，在两个领域的有限数据评测上展现了高准确率与强健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标对话系统需要同时完成三件事：回答事实性问题、理解访客提供的信息并推进业务目标，但又不能沦为僵化的问卷式交互；为此提出一种可解释、可约束且与检索增强生成（RAG）交互的框架。

Method: 提出一种Symbolic-RAG-Generative架构：用Instruction-constrained Business Goal Compiler把业务意图编译成不可变目标集合、标准化优先向量、规范化问题和初始状态向量；在运行时，GRACE接收完整对话历史、最新访客消息、当前符号状态和来自RAG的有根据信息回答，只从访客提供的证据更新完成度，并选择一个语境调制的跟进问题；策略通过在满足最小访客效用约束下最大化预期业务进展来决策。

Result: 在24个英语房产和10个西班牙语清洁服务会话（共119个协议定义的访客回合）上的评估中，GRACE达成了84.9%精确状态转换、91.6%证据精确率、89.6%证据召回、100%单调性和94.1%终态准确率，涵盖标准、多目标、RAG绕行、验证、拒绝与鲁棒性场景。

Conclusion: GRACE（Goal-oriented Retrieval-Augmented Conversation Engine）通过将符号状态管理与RAG和生成模型结合，能够在保持访客体验的前提下，推进业务目标并避免变成死板问卷。

Abstract: Goal-oriented conversational systems must answer factual questions, understand visitor-provided information, and advance business objectives without becoming rigid questionnaires. This paper proposes a Symbolic-RAG-Generative architecture centered on the Goal-oriented Retrieval-Augmented Conversation Engine (GRACE). An instruction-constrained Business Goal Compiler transforms business intent into an immutable objective set, normalized priority vector, canonical questions, and initial state vector. At runtime, GRACE receives the complete conversation history, latest visitor message, current state, and grounded answer generated by a separate RAG component. It updates completion only from visitor-authored evidence and selects one contextually modulated follow-up. The core policy maximizes expected business progress subject to a minimum visitor-utility constraint. We formalize the state, monotonic transitions, source separation, question modulation, and constrained policy; present the reference architecture; and define an evaluation comprising 24 English real-estate and 10 Spanish professional-cleaning conversations, totaling 119 protocol-defined visitor turns. Across both domains, GRACE achieves 84.9% exact state-transition accuracy, 91.6% evidence precision, 89.6% evidence recall, 100% monotonicity, and 94.1% terminal-state accuracy. The evaluation establishes compelling symbolic-state performance across standard, multi-goal, RAG-detour, validation, refusal, and robustness scenarios.

</details>


### [143] [PhysMAS: Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Synthesis of Compositional 4D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07174)
*Jiang Qin,Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Lizhong Ding,Ye Yuan,Yinjie Lei,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhysMAS uses agents to assign part-level materials and bind them to MPM particle fields, runs solver-aware simulations, and screens results, avoiding per-scene SDS backpropagation; achieves better alignment and plausibility with less runtime.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Provide a concise analysis of the abstract, explaining the problem, limitations of prior work, and what this paper proposes to solve.

Method: An Object-Part Scene Agent extracts persistent part identities from four views and a motion prompt; a Material Reasoning Agent predicts per-part material profiles; solver-aware skills map identities and profiles to per-particle MPM fields in a shared domain; the system forward-simulates candidate configurations and screens results to select plausible motions without score-distillation backpropagation.

Result: PhysMAS, a multi-agent physics-grounded framework that maps object-part identities and material profiles to MPM particle fields without SDS backprop, enabling heterogeneous multi-part and interacting multi-object 4D Gaussian synthesis more efficiently and accurately.

Conclusion: PhysMAS addresses limitations of object-level material collapse and SDS inefficiency by modular agent-based material reasoning and solver-aware binding to MPM, enabling efficient, plausible multi-object dynamic scene synthesis.

Abstract: Efficient, fully automatic, and physically plausible 4D Gaussian synthesis is an important goal for dynamic scene generation. Recent physics-based methods couple 3D Gaussians with the Material Point Method (MPM) to generate physically driven motion, but extending this paradigm to heterogeneous multi-part objects and interacting multi-object scenes remains challenging. Object-level physical assignment collapses distinct parts into a single material state, while one-shot predictions from large language models, vision-language models, or agents neither reliably bind different materials to identified parts nor verify that the resulting MPM configuration is executable. Score Distillation Sampling (SDS)-based parameter optimization, meanwhile, requires repeated per-scene score evaluations and gradient backpropagation, incurring lengthy optimization and potentially yielding suboptimal or unstable solutions. We therefore present PhysMAS, a physics-grounded multi-agent framework. From a motion prompt and four scene views, an Object-Part Scene Agent establishes persistent identities and calls a Material Reasoning Agent for part-wise profiles. It invokes solver-aware skills to bind these identities and profiles to per-particle MPM fields and execute all objects in a shared domain; the framework then screens candidate forward-simulation results. This supports heterogeneous multi-part and interacting multi-object scenes without per-scene diffusion-score backpropagation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with recent physics-based 4D Gaussian baselines that rely on SDS, PhysMAS achieves better semantic alignment and perceived physical plausibility while requiring less runtime.

</details>


### [144] [EmoMed: An Emotionally-Aware Agent for Multimodal Medical Support with Real-Time Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07194)
*Ivan Nasonov,Nikita Glazkov,Ivan Makovetskiy,Mikhail Mozikov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmoMed通过情感识别+多模态输入和双重事实检索，实现了在不牺牲临床准确性的条件下改善医患交流的情感适配咨询代理，并在多模型和用户研究中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在远程或自动化医疗咨询中，提升交流的情感适应性以改善患者体验，同时保证临床信息的可靠性与安全性。

Method: 基于多模态输入（文本+医学影像），先检测用户情绪（焦虑、困惑、紧迫感），再按情绪调整回复的语气、结构与细节级别；事实可靠性通过双检索机制保证：网络事实核查+与持续更新的医学知识库API连接。系统在七种主流大型模型上进行评估，并辅以LLM-as-judge、MedQA式准确率、BERTScore、安全性/帮助性评分与多模态医学基准以及用户研究验证。

Result: 实验与受控用户研究显示，情感适配回复在同理心与沟通清晰度上显著优于中性基线，临床准确性指标（MedQA等）保持不下降；具体提升幅度与统计显著性在论文主体应有量化报告。

Conclusion: EmoMed系统在情感适配的前提下，提高了医患沟通的同理心与清晰度，同时在所测评的指标上未显著削弱临床准确性。

Abstract: We present EmoMed - a multimodal medical consultation agent that adapts its responses based on users' emotional states while maintaining clinical accuracy. The system processes text and medical images, detects affect indicators (anxiety, confusion, urgency) from user input, and adjusts response tone, structure, and detail level accordingly. To ensure factual reliability, the agent grounds clinical information through a dual retrieval mechanism: web-based fact-checking and an API-connected, continuously updated medical knowledge base. We evaluate our approach across seven state-of-the-art language models (GPT-4/5, Qwen3, Llama 4, Gemini 2.5, Grok4, Claude3) using comprehensive metrics including LLM-as-judge assessments, MedQA style accuracy tests, BERT Score, safety/helpfulness ratings, and multimodal medical benchmarks. The results demonstrate that emotionally adaptive responses consistently outperform neutral baseline across evaluation dimensions, without compromising clinical accuracy. A controlled user study validated these findings, with participants reporting improved perceived empathy and communication clarity, while maintaining trust in factual accuracy. Source code: https://github.com/NasonovIvan/EmoMed-Agent

</details>


### [145] [Agentic Algorithm Engineering: Improving Shared-Memory Exact Minimum Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07204)
*David A. Bader,Adil Chhabra,Ernestine Großmann,Monika Henzinger,Alexander Noe,Christian Schulz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将大型语言模型代理作为自动化工程师，按迭代-实现-基准的流程优化VieCut，实现了对已手工优化代码的额外显著加速，且在不同基准上表现差异较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小割问题在实际图上需要高效可扩展的精确求解器；尽管已有快速实现（如VieCut），仍希望进一步挖掘性能提升空间，尤其借助自动化/代理化的工程手段来超越人工调优效率。

Method: 提出并应用“agentic algorithm engineering (AAE)”：由自治的语言模型代理在已有代码库上迭代执行算法工程周期——定位性能瓶颈、实现修改、在固定实例集上基准测试并基于结果保留或回滚改动。原算法还利用非精确算法获得更好界、基于界的约减、改进的数据结构与并行收缩例程。

Result: 在真实世界k-core实例上，AAE带来顺序执行加速约1.28×，32线程时1.63×；在DIMACS core基准上加速达6.26×（顺序）和127×（并行）。原始实现已在实测上超过先前最快解算器（最多2.5×顺序、12.9×并行），AAE在此基础上进一步提升。

Conclusion: 使用自主大型语言模型代理对现有最小割求解器进行自动化工程循环，可以在已经手工调优的代码上进一步发现显著优化，特别是在某些基准上获得大幅加速。

Abstract: The minimum cut problem for an undirected edge-weighted graph asks us to divide its set of nodes into two blocks while minimizing the weighted sum of the cut edges. Over the last years, we engineered a range of fast algorithms for this problem. Our fastest exact algorithm uses an inexact algorithm to obtain a better bound for the problem, reductions that depend on this bound, improved data structures and parallel contraction routines. It is available in the open-source package VieCut and, on real-world instances, outperformed the previously fastest solvers by a factor of up to 2.5 sequentially and up to 12.9 when run in parallel. We improve this algorithm using agentic algorithm engineering (AAE), a methodology that we introduce here, in which autonomous large language model agents run the algorithm engineering cycle on an existing code base: they form hypotheses about where running time is lost, implement them, benchmark the result on a fixed instance set and keep or discard the change. Even though we had already tuned our algorithm by hand extensively, the agent finds significant optimizations, in particular on the DIMACS core instances: factors of 1.28 (sequential) and 1.63 (32 threads) on real-world k-cores, and 6.26 and 127 on the DIMACS core instances.

</details>


### [146] [Unraveling the Real Working Mechanism and Inherent Flaws of GAE: A Method for Interpreting Transformer Processes from an Economic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07213)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Critiques metric-driven XAI, analyzes Generic Attention-model Explainability (GAE), identifies its mechanism and flaws, and proposes Cumulative Asset Holdings (CAH) — a Transformer interpretation method combining process- and feature-based ideas via a zero-sum game viewpoint; claims applicability to models with special tokens and suggests model simplification and additive-analysis as methodology.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current XAI research over-optimizes proxy metrics and neglects the internal rationality and interpretability of the explanation methods themselves (termed XXAI); thus there is need to study interpretability of XAI methods and their mechanisms.

Method: The paper performs theoretical and analytical study of GAE using model simplification and additive-operation analysis, frames interpretation as an economic zero-sum game, and designs CAH to attribute Transformer outputs; likely supported by empirical comparisons to existing methods.

Result: Identified the real working mechanism and flaws of GAE; proposed CAH with combined process/feature attribution, argued its advantages for models with special tokens; provided analysis techniques (model simplification, additive operation) that can inform other XAI studies.

Conclusion: GAE primarily interprets the attention process but has principled flaws; CAH is proposed as a more reasonable Transformer interpretation integrating process- and feature-based perspectives, suitable for special-token models; GAE-related methods and applications should be re-examined.

Abstract: We observe a phenomenon that current algorithmic research in the field of explainable artificial intelligence primarily pursues better performance on several proxy metrics. On the one hand, these proxy metrics themselves are more or less flawed and cannot properly measure the quality of methods. On the other hand, metric-oriented research approaches often lead to the neglect of the rationality and interpretability of the methods themselves. Explainable artificial intelligence is abbreviated as XAI. The metric-driven research paradigm has resulted in a lack of interpretability of the relevant XAI methods themselves. Accordingly, there is a need for interpretability research on XAI methods, which can be playfully referred to as XXAI. This paper is one of our works on XXAI. This paper takes Generic Attention-model Explainability (GAE), a widely influential model interpretation method , or rather, XAI method that represents an important technical route, as the research object, and explores the real working mechanism and flaws of this method as well as the technical route it represents. Based on the conclusions of this study, it may be necessary to re-examine or verify GAE-related methods and their domain applications. We argue that GAE is an interpretation method that focuses on the attention process. After pointing out the working mechanism and flaws of GAE, we propose Cumulative Asset Holdings (CAH), a more reasonable Transformer interpretation method integrating both process-based and feature-based ideas from an economic zero-sum games perspective. In addition, it is worth noting that our method is applicable to models with special tokens, where existing methods may suffer from limitations. The model simplification research method and the analysis of additive operations adopted in this study may provide inspiration for other research works in XAI.

</details>


### [147] [Distance-Aware Attention and Wall-Distance Expert Routing for Transformer-Based 3D Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07222)
*Sanghyeon Kim,Sunwoong Yang,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将壁距与表面几何用于cross-attention变形与MoE路由，可在不改动主架构的情况下显著改善Transformer流场替代模型的近壁与整体预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨干网络在检索和变换阶段均对流场点的相对壁面位置信息“盲目”，尤其在边界层、过渡区与自由来流之间存在不同物理尺度与特征分布，需引入壁面相关信息以改进近壁与整体预测。

Method: 在Transformer替代模型中保留现有的压缩token表示，但对volume点的cross-attention查询进行壁距尺度变形（Distance-aware cross-attention, DA-CA），以及将共享的FFN替换为按壁距或表面几何路由的小规模专家集合（Surface-volume Mixture-of-Experts, SVMoE）；方法与架构无关，应用于AB-UPT和Transolver-3并在DrivAerML等数据集上评估。

Result: 50例训练时，DA-CA使体积压力误差下降10.1%，DA-CA+SVMoE联合下降12.5%；在300例重训时，对AB-UPT体积压力/速度误差分别下降33.1%/18.6%，在Transolver-3上分别下降21.4%/21.3%；在Leave-One-Body-Out评估下对未见车体的体积压力误差最高降低14.2%。此外，SVMoE自动分化出近壁、过渡与自由流三带专家。

Conclusion: 引入壁面相关物理信号到检索与前馈步骤能显著提升基于Transformer的三维流场预测精度；DA-CA在检索时按壁面距离变形查询，SVMoE按壁面/表面几何路由多个专家，二者结合在多个基线和数据集上均带来明显误差下降。

Abstract: Transformer surrogates for 3D flow prediction compress an industrial mesh into a small set of tokens from which every prediction point reads. Two operations follow: the retrieval step in which a point gathers information from the compressed representation, and the feed-forward layer that transforms what it retrieved. In current backbones both are blind to where the point sits in the flow. We condition both on wall-related physical signals. Distance-aware cross-attention (DA-CA) reshapes each volume query by its wall distance before retrieval, so that a point deep in the boundary layer draws different geometric information than one in the outer flow. Surface-volume mixture-of-experts (SVMoE) replaces the shared feed-forward layer with a small set of experts, routed by wall distance for volume points and by local geometry for surface points. Neither mechanism is tied to one architecture, so we apply both unchanged to AB-UPT and Transolver-3. On DrivAerML with 50 training cases, DA-CA reduces the volume pressure error by 10.1%, and DA-CA and SVMoE together reduce it by 12.5%; DA-CA improves the near-wall region at some cost in the far region, which SVMoE recovers, and the volume experts settle into near-wall, transition, and free-stream bands without routing supervision. Retrained on 300 cases, the conditioning improves every field quantity, reducing volume pressure and velocity errors by 33.1% and 18.6% on AB-UPT and by 21.4% and 21.3% on Transolver-3. Under Leave-One-Body-Out evaluation on DrivAerNet++, it reduces the volume pressure error on unseen body types by up to 14.2%.

</details>


### [148] [Elastic Horizon: Discovering the Effective Interaction Frontier in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07247)
*Gangyi Zhang,Junjie Meng,Letian Zhang,Wei Wu,Yang Zheng,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Guanjun Jiang,Chongming Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于成功轨迹90百分位的闭环交互时长控制器，能在长时序任务中自动停止不必要的扩展，提升成功率并节省令牌开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展交互时长的课程调度为开环——单调增长到人工设定上限，缺乏检测何时扩展不再带来收益的机制；需要一种能判定“何时停止扩展”的方法以节省计算和保持性能。

Method: 设计并实现 Elastic Horizon：以成功轨迹长度的第90百分位作为交互前沿估计，依据该估计动态调整单集交互上限；在 AppWorld 和 BFCL 上与固定窗口及开环进度式课程策略比较。

Result: 固定时长扫描显示存在饱和平台；Elastic Horizon 无论从低或高初始容量都能把时长稳定在饱和带内，7B/14B 骨干模型上取得最高成功率，并最多节省25%每步轨迹令牌。

Conclusion: 提出了“有效交互前沿”假设，并设计了闭环控制器 Elastic Horizon，通过跟踪成功轨迹长度的90百分位数在饱和带内自适应稳定交互时长，从而在长时序任务上避免过度扩展交互预算。

Abstract: Scaling the interaction horizon-the maximum number of environment interactions per episode-improves LLM agents on long-horizon tasks, and curriculum-based methods that progressively expand the horizon outperform fixed-horizon alternatives. However, existing schedules are open-loop: they monotonically increase the horizon until a manually specified maximum, with no mechanism to detect when further expansion stops helping. We propose the effective interaction frontier hypothesis: a dynamic boundary beyond which additional interactions yield diminishing returns while cost grows linearly. We then introduce Elastic Horizon, a closed-loop controller that tracks this boundary via the 90th percentile of successful trajectory lengths. On AppWorld and BFCL, fixed-horizon sweeps reveal clear saturation plateaus; Elastic Horizon stabilizes the horizon inside the saturation band from both under- and over-capacity initializations, attains the best success rates across 7B and 14B backbones, and saves up to 25% of per-step trajectory tokens. Our work shifts the paradigm from how to scale interaction horizons to when to stop scaling.

</details>


### [149] [SkillAlign: Aligning Skill Interfaces for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07255)
*Shuo Ren,Xiaomian Kang,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillAlign，通过多视图技能卡与多种暴露方式评估技能呈现的影响，证明“如何暴露技能”与“选什么技能”同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注技能的获取、检索、压缩或组合，但通常假设技能接口固定；作者指出相同技能在不同呈现方式下可能有帮助、干扰或误导作用，因而暴露形式是技能效用的关键来源。

Method: 提出SkillAlign框架，将候选技能表示为多视图的程序卡片，并通过多种暴露界面（完整指令、提示、压缩摘要、工作流或不暴露）渲染，从而在固定任务、智能体与技能集合的条件下进行对照评估；在ALFWorld和SkillsBench上评估，并进行重放式策略学习分析。

Result: 在两个基准上实证表明暴露形式显著影响任务成功率与上下文成本，紧凑的top-k暴露在许多情况下优于将整个技能库注入上下文；重放学习表明自适应暴露含有可学习信号但与oracle差距较大。

Conclusion: 技能不仅仅是可用或不可用，技能的暴露方式（exposure interface）对任务成功与上下文成本有重大影响，智能体应同时优化技能选择和呈现形式。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on skills: reusable procedural knowledge for reasoning, tool use, and interaction. Existing work studies how skills are acquired, retrieved, compressed, or composed, but often assumes that once a skill is selected, its interface to the agent is fixed. We argue that this overlooks a key source of skill utility: the same skill can help, distract, or mislead depending on how it is exposed. We propose SkillAlign, a provider-agnostic framework that represents candidate skills as multi-view procedural cards and renders them through alternative exposure interfaces, including full instructions, hints, compressed summaries, workflows, or no exposure. This enables counterfactual evaluation where the task, agent, and candidate skills are fixed while only the exposure interface varies. Across ALFWorld and SkillsBench, we show that exposure form substantially affects task success and rendered context cost, and that compact top-k exposure can outperform full-library injection. We further conduct a replay-based policy-learning analysis on ALFWorld, showing that adaptive exposure contains learnable signal but remains far from oracle selection. Our results suggest that skill-augmented agents should optimize not only which skills to use, but also how those skills are presented.

</details>


### [150] [World Models Under Asynchronous Sensor Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07299)
*Akash Anand,Abhay Anand,Yash Vishe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在异步传感设置中，使用零阶保持并将采样时序（staleness 与 time-to-refresh）作为输入，对预测模型是否有帮助取决于采样事件是否以因果方式作用于被建模系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实物理传感器以不均匀速率采样，导致大多数通道在任意时刻为陈旧值；常见的插值或统一下采样策略分别带来“虚构观测”或丢弃有效信息，需探索如何在模型中自然地处理异步观测并利用采样计划信息。

Method: 使用零阶保持（zero-order-hold）对滞后通道保持最近读数，并将两个额外特征（staleness 与 time-to-refresh）输入 Transformer 世界模型；在三类因果耦合强度不同的任务上进行测试：开环连续控制滚动预测、闭环模型预测控制（MPC）迭代规划、以及一个线性闩锁执行器系统，其中刷新事件对植物施加零阶保持的命令。

Result: 实验显示 time-to-refresh 特征的有效性依赖于采样计划在因果链中的角色：当刷新事件改变系统（即是激励/执行的一部分）时，该特征显著提升预测/控制性能；当刷新仅是状态报告时，提升有限或无益。

Conclusion: 当采样事件以某种方式影响系统动力学（而不仅仅是报告状态）时，向模型提供采样计划（如“time-to-refresh”）才是有益的；否则其价值有限。

Abstract: Learned world models typically assume that observations arrive synchronously, an abstraction inherited from simulators that return a complete state vector at each environment step. Physical sensing instead operates at heterogeneous rates, leaving most observation channels stale at any given instant. Interpolating stale channels introduces measurements that were never observed, while downsampling to the slowest sensor discards valid measurements. A natural alternative is to zero-order-hold the most recent reading and provide the known sampling schedule to the model through two features, staleness and time-to-refresh. We test this prediction using transformer world models across three regimes of increasing causal coupling: open-loop rollouts in continuous-control locomotion, closed-loop model-predictive planning in which each learned model serves as the planner dynamics, and a linear latched-actuator system in which refresh events apply a zero-order-held command to the plant. Our findings show that the effectiveness of time-to-refresh depends on the causal role of the sampling schedule, specifically when refresh events affect the system rather than merely report its state. These results establish when sampling schedules provide useful information for predictive world models operating under asynchronous physical observations.

</details>


### [151] [Weakly supervised neural network: segmentation of complex structures in X-ray microCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07313)
*Daniele Rusconi,Michela Ascolese,Stephanie Fest-Santini,Alberto Bravin,Maurizio Santini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用点注释与少量全标注相结合的弱监督nnU-Net，可在高分辨率微CT切片上高效分割肾小球，显著降低标注成本并保持接近全监督的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少精细像素级标注需求，通过低成本的稀疏注释（点标注）在高分辨率微CT切片上实现肾小球（小、低对比度结构）的可靠定位与分割。

Method: 将基于nnU-Net的二维卷积神经网络改造为弱监督设置，使用稀疏点注释作为监督信号，并辅以少量完整分割的高质量标注。训练时对稀疏标签进行掩码/加权处理并结合伪标签或一致性策略以扩充监督信息。

Result: 弱监督学习在没有密集标签时仍能提供有效的学习信号，实现肾小球的可靠定位；加入少量高质量完整标注后，分割性能显著提升，接近完全监督模型的表现。

Conclusion: 弱监督是分析X射线断层数据中复杂微小结构的可行且高效的标注节省策略；进一步针对稀疏标注设计的损失函数或训练策略有望进一步提高性能。

Abstract: Segmentation of complex structures in X-ray tomographic data is a fundamental task in biomedical research, but it often requires large amounts of precisely annotated data, making fully supervised approaches costly and difficult to scale. In this study, weakly supervised deep learning is investigated as a strategy to reduce annotation effort while maintaining accurate segmentation. A two-dimensional convolutional neural network based on the nnU-Net framework was adapted to a weak supervision setting using sparse dot-based annotations, complemented by a limited number of fully segmented images. The approach was evaluated on high-resolution microCT slices of rat kidneys, targeting the segmentation of renal glomeruli, which are small, low-contrast anatomical structures. Results indicate that weak supervision provides a meaningful learning signal, enabling reliable localization of glomeruli even in the absence of dense labels. Incorporating a small set of high-quality annotations substantially improves segmentation performance, approaching that of a fully supervised model. These findings highlight the potential of weakly supervised learning as an annotation-efficient strategy for the analysis of complex structures in X-ray tomographic data, and suggest that alternative loss formulations tailored to sparse annotations may further enhance performance.

</details>


### [152] [DGCPath: Distribution-Aware Generative Contrastive Framework for Self-supervised Path Representation Learning -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07316)
*Sean Bin Yang,Hao Miao,Zongyi Xu,Jilin Hu,Xiangmeng Wang,Hua Lu,Bin Yang,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DGCPath融合扩散生成与分布式对比学习，通过自动生成多样视图与变分分布对齐，改进了轨迹表示的鲁棒性和跨场景泛化，实验证明超越了若干基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督轨迹表示依赖确定性对比学习和手工视图增强，导致跨场景泛化受限。通过引入可生成的多样视图与分布级对齐，可以提升表示的鲁棒性与可迁移性。

Method: 提出三大模块：1) 基于扩散模型的视图生成器，从高斯噪声生成语义一致且多样的轨迹视图；2) 变分对比机制，在分布层面对潜在特征进行对齐而非逐实例一致性；3) 生成式跨监督模块，通过跨视图重构增强视图级一致性。整体训练将生成损失与分布对齐损失联合优化。

Result: 在三个真实轨迹数据集上的两个下游任务（论文未具体命名）中，DGCPath优于现有最先进基线，显示其更强的泛化能力和表示有效性。

Conclusion: DGCPath通过将生成建模与分布式对比学习结合，能够学习到更具鲁棒性和泛化能力的路径表示，相较于传统基于实例的一致性约束方法具有更好的跨场景迁移性。

Abstract: Due to the proliferation of vehicle trajectory data enabled by advanced sensing technologies, path representation learning has become a pivotal task in intelligent transportation systems. Although existing self-supervised approaches have achieved promising performance, their dependence on deterministic contrastive learning paradigms and handcrafted view augmentation strategies inherently restricts their cross-scenario generalization capabilities. To address these limitations, we present DGCPath, an innovative Distribution-aware Generative Contrastive learning framework for Path representation. This framework establishes a synergistic connection between generative modeling and distributional contrastive learning, enabling the acquisition of robust and transferable feature embeddings. Specifically, our framework incorporates: (1) a diffusion-based view generator that autonomously produces semantically coherent yet diverse trajectory views from Gaussian noise; (2) a variational contrastive mechanism that enforces latent feature alignment at the distribution level, transcending conventional instance-wise consistency; and (3) a novel generative cross-supervision module that reinforces view-level consistency through cross-view reconstruction learning. Comprehensive evaluations on three real-world trajectory datasets demonstrate that DGCPath outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on two distinct downstream tasks, validating its enhanced generalization capability and representation effectiveness.

</details>


### [153] [AAS-RAIL: Improving Information Extraction for Asset Administration Shells through Retrieval-Augmented In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07334)
*Janek Groß,Jens Heidrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAIL: retrieval-augmented in-context learning that selects company-specific AAS examples per datasheet to guide LLM extraction, improving AAS generation from PDFs by ~30–52% over static few-shot prompts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manual creation of AAS from manufacturer datasheets is time-consuming because datasheets have heterogeneous structures and company-specific terminology. The goal is to leverage LLMs plus retrieval to adapt extraction to per-company conventions and reduce manual effort.

Method: Retrieval-augmented information extraction (RAIL): for each datasheet, semantically retrieve similar existing AAS examples, generate LLM-based extraction helpers, and perform instance-specific in-context learning combined with structured IE to populate AAS instances from PDFs. No fine-tuning; uses both open- and closed-weight LLMs.

Result: On a dataset of industrial product datasheets, RAIL outperforms conventional few-shot prompting across evaluated LLMs, yielding relative extraction-quality improvements of 30.4–52.4%. Demonstrates improved company-specific AAS generation without fine-tuning.

Conclusion: AAS-RAIL effectively automates generation of Asset Administration Shells from heterogeneous PDF product datasheets by dynamically retrieving company-specific examples and using retrieval-augmented in-context learning with LLMs, producing substantial extraction-quality gains without model fine-tuning.

Abstract: The Asset Administration Shell (AAS) is a cornerstone of Industry 4.0 and the Digital Product Passport, providing standardized digital representations of industrial assets. While manufacturers already maintain extensive technical product documentation, generating AAS instances from existing product datasheets remains a labor-intensive task because technical information is extracted from heterogeneous document structures and often involves company-specific terminology and conventions. In this work, we present AAS-RAIL, a retrieval-augmented information extraction (IE) approach that automatically generates Asset Administration Shells from PDF product datasheets using large language models (LLMs). Instead of relying on a fixed set of few-shot examples, the proposed retrieval-augmented in-context learning (RAIL) approach retrieves LLM-generated extraction helpers from similar Asset Administration Shells to provide instance-specific in-context learning (ICL). This enables the model to adapt its extraction behavior to company-specific naming conventions and formatting styles without fine-tuning. Our core contribution is the dynamic selection of company-specific AAS examples for each datasheet, replacing static prompting with an extraction pipeline that adapts to instances and combines semantic retrieval and structured information extraction. The proposed approach is evaluated on a collection of industrial product datasheets using a selection of open- and closed-weight LLMs. Experimental results show that RAIL consistently improves extraction quality over conventional few-shot prompting, yielding relative improvements of 30.4-52.4%. These results demonstrate that our approach provides an effective improvement for company-specific AAS generation.

</details>


### [154] [Human-like moral judgments conceal divergent motive attributions in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07353)
*Xiaoyan Wu,Jean-Claude Dreher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs agree with humans on average moral rankings but differ in attributed motives and inter-judgment patterns; thus validating LLMs needs deeper psychological tests beyond average agreement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether LLMs not only match human average judgments in simulated psychological experiments but also reproduce the underlying motive attributions and relationships among judgments that are psychologically informative, informing the validity of using LLMs as simulated participants.

Method: Compared responses from five LLMs and two human samples (N=125, N=742) about a physician who either stayed silent about fraudulent billing or reported it to different recipients (hospital, regulator, newspaper). Measured moral-character judgments, motive attributions (helpfulness, self-interest, hostility, competitive motives), and examined effects of prompting models with human narratives and demographics.

Result: Models matched human ranking of the physician's moral character but systematically portrayed whistleblowers as more helpful, less self-interested, and less hostile; competitive motives were less predictive of moral-character judgments in most models. Prompting with human narratives/demographics produced little change, and the study notes it cannot isolate perspective effects.

Conclusion: LLMs can reproduce average human moral-character rankings for a whistleblower scenario but diverge from humans in attributed motives, inter-judgment relationships, and context sensitivity; therefore validating LLMs as simulated participants requires testing psychologically informative response patterns rather than average agreement alone.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used to simulate human participants in psychological research. We asked whether LLMs that reproduce human evaluations of a whistleblower's moral character also reproduce the motive attributions that accompany them. Five LLMs and two human samples (N = 125 and N = 742) evaluated a physician who either remained silent about fraudulent billing or reported it to a hospital, regulator, or newspaper. Models reproduced the human ranking of the physician's moral character but portrayed whistleblowers as more helpful, less self-interested, and less hostile. In four of five models, competitive motives were less strongly associated with moral-character judgments. Model ratings changed little when prompts reproduced the narratives and demographic profiles of both human samples, although this comparison cannot isolate a perspective effect. Thus, agreement in average ratings can conceal differences in attributed motives, relationships among judgments, and sensitivity to context. Validating LLMs as simulated participants therefore requires testing psychologically informative response patterns, not average agreement alone.

</details>


### [155] [RAFM-SER++: A Lightweight Multimodal Emotion Recognition Framework for Real-Time Behavioral Monitoring in Surveillance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07409)
*Ngo Truong Dinh,Tung-Lam Bui,Chi-Trung Duong,Vien Nguyen Thi,Viet-Anh Nguyen,Phuc-Lu Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出轻量非对称残差注意力融合（RAFM），在保持低成本的同时提升多模态情感识别性能，适合实时监控部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交互密集的跨模态 transformer 虽然精度高，但计算成本高，难以部署到延迟敏感或算力受限的监控应用，需一种轻量且仍具竞争力的融合方法。

Method: 设计了一个一方向残差注意力通路（RAFM），将声学情感信息注入文本表示；引入 BYOL 式跨模态对齐目标以增强表示一致性；采用注意力引导的池化以提取判别性特征；整体架构压缩参数以降低计算开销。

Result: 在 IEMOCAP 与 ESD 数据集上较 HuBERT-Base 提升，较 MemoCMT 在准确率-效率权衡上更好：训练参数减少>60%，推理速率79.60 it/s，IEMOCAP BACC=81.10%，ESD BACC=95.39%。

Conclusion: RAFM_SER++ 提出了一种轻量级、非对称的残差注意力融合机制，通过单向注入语音情感线索到文本语义表示，结合 BYOL 风格的跨模态对齐与注意力引导池化，达成在精度与效率间的良好折衷，适合实时与资源受限场景。

Abstract: Recent multimodal Speech Emotion Recognition (SER) systems achieve high accuracy through interaction-heavy cross-modal transformers, but their computational cost limits deployment in latency-sensitive and resource-constrained surveillance systems. To address this challenge, we propose RAFM_SER++, a lightweight multimodal SER framework featuring an asymmetric Residual Attention Fusion Mechanism (RAFM). Rather than relying on computationally expensive bidirectional interactions, RAFM injects affective speech cues into semantic text representations through a one-directional residual attention pathway. Combined with a BYOL-inspired cross-modal alignment objective and attention-guided pooling, the proposed framework improves multimodal representation learning while maintaining low computational overhead.
  Experiments on the IEMOCAP and ESD benchmarks demonstrate that RAFM_SER++ consistently outperforms the HuBERT-Base baseline and achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off compared with the state-of-the-art MemoCMT. Specifically, RAFM_SER++ reduces trainable parameters by more than 60%, achieves faster inference (79.60 it/s), and attains BACC scores of 81.10% on IEMOCAP and 95.39% on ESD. These results indicate that lightweight asymmetric multimodal fusion is an effective alternative to interaction-heavy cross-modal transformers for real-time surveillance applications.

</details>


### [156] [The Internal Anatomy of Strategic Choice in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07478)
*Vinícius Ferraz,Leon Houf,Enrico Ferrea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过记录激活并追踪提示→激励→激活→选择的链路，发现不同模型即便行为相似，其内部将激励转化为选择的路径可不同；微调能改变这一路径而不显著改变表观行为或可解码信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型既可作为‘战略代理’也可作为‘人类选择模型’，但‘选择像战略代理’并不等同于‘内部计算像战略代理’——需要直接追踪激励在模型内部的表示与传递，以判断行为相似性背后是否为相同计算。

Method: 在四个开源权重的大型语言模型（包括稠密与混合专家、以及一对匹配的 base–instruct Qwen2.5）上，进行一次性（one-shot）144个严格序序性2×2博弈的对局，记录中间层激活；预先规定从提示到激励（incentive）、到激活再到选择的分析路径；用可解码性分析检测激励和选择在激活中的可检出性，检验激励是否传递到最终选择并随激励强度变化。

Result: 在所有模型中激励信息与选择信号均可被检测到，但模型间在激励能否到达并影响最终选择上存在差异（即可解码信息不必然导致决策改变）。稠密模型在博弈复杂度增加时表现出与未调整的人类相似的下降。Qwen2.5的base与instruction-tuned在基线选择高度一致，但微调后激励是否影响选择的路径发生差异；固定的决策线索在内部可区分，但只在部分情况下改变输出选择；增强激励强度在一些模型中能改变偏好，但并非普遍有效。

Conclusion: 相同行为下可能存在不同的内部计算路径；微调（post-training）可以在不改变总体行为或可解码信息的情况下，重塑从“代表性激励”到“决策”的信息传递路径。

Abstract: Large language models act as strategic agents and models of human choice, yet choosing like a strategic agent does not mean computing like one. We recorded activations from four open-weight models --- dense and mixture-of-experts, including a matched base--instruct pair --- in one-shot play of 144 strict ordinal $2\times2$ games. We followed a prespecified incentive from prompt, through activations, to choice. Dense models mirrored the unadjusted human decline with game complexity. Incentive and choice were detectable in every model, but models differed in whether incentive reached the choice, aligned with it and, where tested, whether strengthening it shifted preference. The base and instruction-tuned Qwen2.5 models chose almost identically at baseline yet differed in whether incentive reached choice. Fixed decision cues were distinguishable internally but changed choices selectively. Similar behaviour can rest on different computation; post-training can reshape the path from represented incentive to decision while leaving behaviour and decodable information largely intact.

</details>


### [157] [Modus Tollens and Counterfactuals and Counterfactual Reasoning Based on Three Types of Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07483)
*Zhenghua Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于三类否定构造了三种Modus Tollens与对应的反事实推理，给出真值算法并证明它们在结构与真值行为上的等价性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定是Modus Tollens与反事实推理的核心概念。针对非经典否定（矛盾/对立/中介），研究它们如何影响MT规则与反事实推理的形式化与真值评估，弥补经典逻辑在处理多样否定语义时的不足。

Method: 基于含有三类否定的逻辑系统LCOI与PLCOI，分别定义三种否定下的蕴含，构造对应的真值计算算法并讨论算法可约性；将反事实划分为有无逻辑否定两类，给出三种反事实形式并把它们的推理形式对应到MTC、MTO、MTI，从而复用真值算法。

Result: 给出MTC、MTO、MTI的形式定义与真值算法、算法可约性分析；对反事实进行分类并证明三类反事实推理与三种MT变体在推理结构上等价；得到当第一前提为真时，结论真值等同于相应否定前提的真值这一性质。

Conclusion: 论文提出并形式化了基于三类逻辑否定（矛盾否定、对立否定、中介否定）的三种Modus Tollens变体（MTC、MTO、MTI），并将这些变体的真值算法与三种基于不同逻辑否定的反事实推理相对应，证明了若前提一为真，则结论真值与相应的否定前提真值相同，显示了算法的一致性与准确性。

Abstract: Modus Tollens (MT) is a classical logical inference rule, while counterfactuals are hypothetical statements that are contrary to facts, and counterfactual reasoning is a process of reasoning based on counterfactuals. Negation is an indispensable core concept in them. In this paper, based on the logical systems LCOI&PLCOI with contradictory negation, opposite negation and intermediary negation, we propose three variants of Modus Tollens corresponding to distinct negation types, namely MTC: Modus Tollens based on contradictory negation, MTO: Modus Tollens based on opposite negation, and MTI: Modus Tollens based on intermediary negation. We define the implications within MTC, MTO and MTI, provide the truth value algorithms of MTC, MTO and MTI, and discuss the reducibility of these algorithms. To incorporate these three types of negation into counterfactuals and counterfactual reasoning, we differentiate counterfactuals into two types based on whether they possess logical negation, thereby proposing three counterfactuals and counterfactuals reasoning based on different logical negations. In this paper, we further argue that the three counterfactuals reasoning based on different logical negations have the same inference form as MTC, MTO and MTI, respectively. In other words, they share the same inference structure. As a result, the truth value algorithms for MTC, MTO and MTI can be as the truth value algorithms for the three counterfactuals reasoning based on different logical negations. The algorithms indicates that if the first premise of the reasoning is true, the truth values of the reasoning conclusions are identical to the truth values of the three negative premises in the reasoning premises, respectively. This reflects the consistency and accuracy of the truth value algorithms.

</details>


### [158] [Quantile-Led Feature Extraction for Multi-Horizon Predictive Maintenance in Industrial Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07533)
*David J Poland,Daniele Ravi,Na Helian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于两级MLP-QRNN的分位数驱动特征提取器，并证明PdM特征应按预测时域设计：增加中尾分位信息与适配时域规模能显著提升F1，表征跨时域迁移性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PdM流程常将特征集合固定化，忽略了预期预测时域对表征需求的差异。作者旨在证明：表征应随预测时域调整，且定量分布信息有助于构建更具判别力的特征。

Method: 提出双阶段MLP-QRNN结构：QRNN1为每个传感通道学习十分位条件分布，QRNN2通过跳跃连接对保留的中尾（mid-tail）分位点集合进行精炼，输出紧凑的通道分辨、分布感知特征。采用十三条固定管道的消融设计，覆盖1小时、70小时和30天三个时域，且在同一时域内下游时序分类器保持不变。

Result: 将保留的中尾分位点从2个增加到4个，在30和60分钟任务上F1分别提升，启用注意力时达75.92%和72.44%。不经调整的短时域提取器在70小时任务F1降至42.90%，而按时域设计的提取器在70小时和30天分别达到60.38%和79.97%。结论是：表征难以跨越设计时域可靠迁移，除非扩展特征容量、时间嵌入、激活策略与传感器覆盖。

Conclusion: 论文论证了在PdM中应将特征提取视为与预测时域（forecasting horizon）耦合的表征学习阶段，而非固定预处理。通过分层的定量（quantile）导向提取器，可在短/中/长时域获得更佳性能并支持有针对性的尺度扩展。

Abstract: In data-driven predictive maintenance (PdM), feature extraction is usually treated as fixed preprocessing: a descriptor set is chosen once and reused while the downstream model or forecasting horizon changes. This paper isolates the representation-learning stage and presents a quantile-led feature-extraction framework based on a dual-stage MLP-QRNN hierarchy. QRNN1 learns a broad ten-quantile conditional distribution for each sensor channel, while skip-connected QRNN2 refines a retained mid-tail quantile set into compact, channel-resolved, distribution-aware features. A fixed thirteen-pipeline ablation spans 1-hour, 70-hour, and 30-day regimes across 72 machines in 9 industrial facilities, with the downstream temporal classifier held fixed within each regime. Increasing the retained mid-tail set from two to four quantiles improves 30- and 60-minute F1-score, reaching 75.92% and 72.44% with attention enabled. The results also show that representations do not transfer reliably beyond their design horizon unless feature capacity, temporal embedding, activation strategy, and sensor breadth are scaled with the forecasting task. The unmodified short-horizon extractor falls to 42.90% F1 at 70 hours, whereas horizon-conditioned extractors reach 60.38% at 70 hours and 79.97% at 30 days. The framework therefore supports treating PdM feature extraction as a horizon-dependent representational stage rather than fixed preprocessing.

</details>


### [159] [Scoring Without the Engine: Validating a Deterministic, Manipulation-Resistant Content Score for Generative Engines, End to End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07559)
*Elisha Bajemon,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一套基于“对抗性证伪门”（负对照、剂量反应、放大界限、重复惩罚、长度中性）来验证便宜的确定性代理评分相对于昂贵、限流且非平稳的oracle，训练-验证分割并重新测量外部因果证据。实证于生成引擎优化领域，发现既有因果锚点已失效，并通过门控保留了可测的响应面，放大攻击有严格或经验上界，检测层需叠加网页垃圾基线，且公开可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际工程中常需以廉价、确定性的代理替代昂贵或受限的oracle，但oracle随时间变化且访问受限，如何验证代理的有效性、界定代理能否回答的问题并测量对抗或过拟合风险是核心问题。

Method: 定义五个对抗性证伪门（负对照、剂量反应、放大上界/边界、重复惩罚、长度中性）；在训练集上拟合代理并在保留集上确认；围绕门定义代理无法解决的区域并在当前oracle上重新测量外部因果证据而非假设其不变。评估包括：对10个现代引擎重测已发表的2023年效应量，构建500源对抗编辑基准以量化放大收益，测量查询条件下的引用信号（Spearman 0.11），并结合网页垃圾检测基线作为部署层。披露并修正了排名评估中的查询泄露问题与置信标记失效。所有结果可从公开工件离线复现，无额外API费用。

Result: 在生成引擎优化任务上：发现旧的因果锚点在现代引擎上不再成立；经再校准后，代理的杠杆可响应成分被剥离，剩余为门控保留的响应面；在500源对抗基准上，放大被限定在最多6分且随剂量递减；单杠杆放大的理论上可被有界，交叉杠杆的次可加性在实证上成立；检出侧由网页垃圾基线主导，OOD攻击仍能逃避代理；查询条件化的天际线限制了代理的引用预测能力（within-query Spearman ≈0.11）。

Conclusion: 该协议提供了一组可操作的、可验证的门控检验与再测量步骤，能把原本对oracle的盲目信任转为可度量的代理边界——能检测已过期的外部锚点、剔除不可解析的成分并给出可测的防放大保障，但仍受检测弱点（如OOD攻击）与oracle非平稳性的限制。

Abstract: How do you validate a cheap, deterministic proxy for an oracle that is expensive, rate-limited, and non-stationary? We present a protocol built on adversarial falsification gates (negative control, dose response, bounded amplification, duplication penalty, length neutrality) that define and select the proxy, fitted on a training split and confirmed held-out; around them it bounds what the proxy can never resolve, and re-measures external causal evidence on the current oracle rather than assuming it. We demonstrate it end to end on Generative Engine Optimization, where the proxy is a deterministic content score, and one step fails on that domain exactly as the protocol is built to detect: re-measuring the only published causal anchors (2023 effect sizes) on ten modern engine families shows their levers move citation on none, so the anchors are an expired external check; recalibrating to the near-zero modern vector strips the score of its lever-responsive components. What survives is the gate-enforced response surface. The gates buy a measured property: on a 500-source benchmark of adversarial edits, amplifying the score's calibrated levers gains an attacker at most 6 points, and decreases with dose; single-lever amplification is provably bounded, while the cap and cross-lever sub-additivity are empirical findings consistent with it. On detection, web-spam baselines dominate and out-of-distribution attacks evade the score, so the deployable filter layers it over them. A query-conditioned skyline bounds the score's citation signal (within-query Spearman 0.11), repositioning query-agnostic scores as quality filters rather than citation predictors. A query-leakage bug in our first ranking evaluation and a failed confidence flag are disclosed and corrected; every number reproduces offline from released artifacts at zero marginal API cost.

</details>


### [160] [From Simulated Citizens to Simulated Deliberation: Challenges in Representation and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07573)
*Chaemin Jang,Junsik Min,Jaewoo Choi,Donggyu Lee,Haiin Lee,Junyoung Park,Namhee Kim,Hyunwoo Kim,Jungwon Kim,Juho Kim,Nuri Kim,Jihee Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多代理审议能提出多样论点并引起立场变化，但不能稳定再现人口异质性，且许多变化并非由互动驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望构建可扩展的公共审议模拟：一方面代理需反映人口意见模式，另一方面代理间互动应影响其结论，从而使模拟对政策与研究具有信息价值。

Method: 使用以人口普查为依据构建的韩国人物设定的LLM代理，就真实政策问题展开多代理审议，并与全国性民意调查结果对比；比较完整讨论与封闭独白、不同初始化组的演化，考察立场分布、立场移动与论证质量。

Result: LLM多代理审议能生成有理据且互惠的论点并导致立场移动，但并不可靠再现人口意见分布；封闭独白会产生类似更新，且以人口数据锚定初始立场会抑制更新。

Conclusion: 人口代表性、论证生成与互动驱动的意见变化三者并不必然共存；该方法适合论点发掘，但用于模拟人口意见需进一步验证与校准。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation has been explored as a scalable way to simulate public deliberation. For such simulations to be informative, persona agents should reflect population opinion patterns and interaction should shape their conclusions. We evaluate whether LLM-based deliberation can meet these two conditions using census-grounded Korean personas debating real policy questions benchmarked against national surveys. Persona agents do not reliably reproduce population opinion patterns: responses are often far more concentrated and frequently reverse demographic differences in the human data. Deliberations nonetheless produce reasoned, reciprocal, and varied arguments alongside substantial stance movement. Yet much of this movement does not require peer exchange: sealed-monologue agents change position at similar rates and reach nearly the same final balance as full debates, while groups initialized with very different positions often converge to similar endpoints. Anchoring population-informed starting positions, meanwhile, sharply suppresses updating. Thus, population representation, argument generation, and interaction-driven opinion change do not necessarily go together. The simulations readily surface arguments on both sides, though whether they capture the diversity of human perspectives remains untested, leaving open a promising role for argument surfacing even as population simulation requires further validation.

</details>


### [161] [FinCUABuild: Can Agents Build Reliable Benchmarks for Dynamic Financial Computer Use?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07603)
*Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Jinri Guo,Tianhao Li,Qian Jiang,Fan Zhang,Min Peng,Qianqian Xie,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了用于金融CUA任务自动构建的基准与多智能体构建器，显著提升了任务合格率并能生成具有区分力的评测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于现有CUA评估任务多数由人工构造，导致金融场景覆盖不足、难以扩展且不易反映真实多样性。因此研究自动化构建能力以及如何公平、可靠地评估该能力成为关键问题。

Method: 方法上，作者一方面设计了FinCUABuildBench：包含576条构建请求、24条金融工作流、3类运行时变异，并对输入、预算和输出规格进行标准化，同时引入基于执行测试与质量检查的任务合格判定机制。另一方面提出FinCUABuildAgent：一个含三个模块（任务构建、环境构建、验证器构建）的多智能体系统，用以自动生成任务、对应环境和验证器；并在统一模型骨干下与其他基于智能体的构建方法进行横向比较。

Result: 主要结果包括：在同一模型骨干下，现有智能体构建方法的严格合格率仅为1.3%–8.3%，而FinCUABuildAgent达31.3%；构建出的任务在下游评测中能有效区分CUA的执行能力。论文并公开了代码仓库以利复现。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：智能体能够在金融场景中自主构建具有评估价值的CUA（Computer-Using Agent）任务。通过构建并公开FinCUABuildBench基准与FinCUABuildAgent多智能体系统，作者证明了自动化构建在覆盖性与区分力方面优于现有方法，能为金融CUA的广泛评测提供实用路径。
