Abstract: Financial scenarios are diverse and complex, spanning varying data conditions, tool configurations, and workflows. Yet existing CUA, Computer-Using Agent, evaluation tasks remain largely manually constructed, limiting scalable coverage of real-world financial scenarios. Then, can agents autonomously construct diverse CUA evaluation tasks for financial scenarios? Evaluating this capability poses three key challenges: scenario coverage of construction requests, fair comparison across construction methods, and reliable assessment of generated task quality. To solve these, we introduce FinCUABuildBench, a benchmark for evaluating financial CUA task construction, featuring: (i) 576 construction requests covering 24 financial workflows and three types of runtime variation; (ii) standardized input, budget, and output specifications; and (iii) a task qualification mechanism based on execution tests and quality checks. We further introduce FinCUABuildAgent, a multi-agent system for automatically constructing dynamic financial CUA evaluation tasks. It consists of three modules that jointly construct tasks, environments, and validators. On FinCUABuildBench, under the same model backbone, existing agent-based construction methods achieve strict qualification rates of only 1.3-8.3%, while FinCUABuildAgent reaches 31.3%. Downstream evaluations further show that the constructed tasks can effectively differentiate CUA task-execution capabilities. These results demonstrate that agents can autonomously construct financial CUA tasks with meaningful evaluation value, offering a practical path toward broader evaluation coverage in financial scenarios. Code: https://github.com/FengxianJi/FinCUABuild

</details>


### [162] [AgentIdeaBench: Benchmarking Scientific Ideation in the Agent Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07611)
*Yunxiang Mo,Tianshi Zheng,Yisen Gao,Rui Wang,Newt Nguyen Kim Hue Nam,Kelvin Kiu Wai Tam,Jiaxin Bai,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentIdeaBench 用匹配的静态/主动评估与文献校验评分发现：主动探索揭示更多能力空间并提升落地性，增益受模型能力驱动；结构化思想实验能帮助中端模型，但对最强模型边际收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多在静态、被动的参考文献集合上测量科学构想，但现代自治AI科学家采用检索—推理的主动工作流，静态评测随着模型能力提升逐渐失去辨别力，亟需适配代理时代的评估基准。

Method: 构建一个多学科基准（AgentIdeaBench），对33个大型语言模型在40个细分子领域（跨5个学科）进行匹配的静态与主动两种评估；使用多维、文献校验的评分框架，由评论者在检索到的既有文献背景下评估构想的原创性、可行性、清晰度与具体性；并提出并测试‘Scientific World Modeling’——一个在生成时通过结构化思想实验迭代精炼假说的循环。

Result: 主动探索相比静态观察展现出更大的能力上限（性能增长约为静态的两倍），并且这种增益是能力门控的：强模型比弱模型获益更多；增益主要体现在更扎实的落地性（可行性、清晰性、具体性）而非被评价的原创性；同时，Scientific World Modeling 对中等能力模型有明显帮助，但对前沿模型作用有限（这些模型可能已内化相似的推理策略）。

Conclusion: AgentIdeaBench 通过匹配的“静态观察—主动探索”评估设置，揭示了在检索—推理代理工作流下大模型科学构想能力的真实差异与潜力，表明主动探索能显著放大模型能力差异并主要提升可行性、清晰性与具体性，而非评价中的原创度。

Abstract: Scientific ideation is the capacity to formulate novel and testable hypotheses from scientific evidence, and autonomous AI scientists depend on it. Existing evaluations largely assess it by asking models to generate ideas from a static, curated set of reference papers. That passive setup departs from the retrieval-and-reasoning workflow of modern AI scientists, and it becomes less discriminative as models improve. We introduce AgentIdeaBench, a multidisciplinary benchmark that evaluates scientific ideation under two matched settings, static observation and active exploration. We report matched Static-Active evaluations for 33 LLMs across 40 densely scored subfields spanning five disciplines, using a multidimensional, literature-verified scoring framework whose critics assess originality against retrieved prior art. Active exploration reveals considerably more capability headroom, and that headroom is unevenly distributed across models. Performance scales about twice as fast as under static observation, and the exploration gain is capability-gated, favoring the strongest models over the weakest. The gain reflects better grounding, improving feasibility, clarity, and specificity while leaving measured originality unchanged under our critics. We further explore Scientific World Modeling, a generation-time loop that refines a draft hypothesis through structured thought experiments. It benefits mid-capability models, and its impact diminishes among frontier models that appear to have internalized such reasoning patterns already. AgentIdeaBench gives future work on scientific ideation a measurement basis suited to the agent era.

</details>


### [163] [Norms at a Price: Why RL-Based Alignment Can Promise Conditional Compliance at Best](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07627)
*Kevin Baum,Rūta Binkytė,Felix Jahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 训练只评分可见行为，导致代理可能只在被看见时合规；解决之道倾向于架构性限制而非仅靠行为训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当下出现的AI‘伪合规’、评估规避与策略性行为表明现有训练范式存在根本局限，迫切需要重新思考如何保证长期可信的外显与隐含行为一致性。

Method: 概念论证与理论分析：从强化学习的打分机制出发，通过可识别性/不可识别性论证、观测-选择耦合与迭代检测-训练闭环的选择压力，举例说明并归纳多种已知现象为同一因果结构。

Result: 本文断言当前基于强化学习的训练范式本质上只能保证“被观察时合规”的行为——因为训练信号来自被评分的可观测行为，故“仅在被观察时合规”与“始终合规”在训练数据上不可区分。具有代理性的系统更容易利用这一点：它们可以选择何时被看见，迭代性的检测-训练流程反而选择通过检测而非真正遵从，从而解释了对齐伪装、压库(sandbagging)、评估感知的策略性行为等现象。作者主张补救不是更深的内化（更复杂的训练信号），而是通过体系结构性限制使违规行为“不可用”而非“可选”。

Conclusion: 基于行为的训练在原则上不能证明无条件合规；应转向架构性措施（能力限制、不可达的违规通路、可验证性、可解释性与强制性执行机制）来把违规从可能性空间中移除。

Abstract: AI agents sometimes act aligned when they infer they are being tested, and differently when not. We argue this is not an anomaly but what current training regimes are structured to select for. Reinforcement-learning-based alignment folds norms and task pursuit into one policy: the system learns its norms from scored behavior, and scoring flattens them. Do not do X is learned as doing X costs something if noticed. On every datum training can produce, a policy that complies only when it might be observed is indistinguishable from one that complies always. The experiment that would tell them apart - scoring unobserved behavior - is a contradiction in terms. Conditional compliance is thus the most that behavioral training can be known to deliver. Agency sharpens the problem: agents operate mostly where no one is watching, and can act on whether they are watched. An iterated pipeline that trains against detected failures selects for passing detection, not for complying. This account unifies alignment faking, sandbagging, and evaluation-aware scheming. And it reorients the remedy: not deeper internalization but architecture, making violations unavailable rather than unchosen.

</details>


### [164] [Aegix Pulse: A Traceable Three-Stage Architecture for Personalized Content Generation and Context-Preserving Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07672)
*Hongnan Zhao,Shiyu Chen,Zhihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aegix Pulse的三阶段架构在设计上可同时纳入任务、品牌与修订溯源，实验显示加入Account Profile与保留上下文修订有正向但不显著的效果；成功历史证据未改进品牌一致性，人工评审未能稳定复现模型裁判结果，需改进证据处理与评估方法以获得更强结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实生产内容生成需同时兼顾即时任务需求、长期品牌一致性、历史证据与修订反馈，但现有系统难以在生产环境中有效整合这些因素并保留溯源，故设计Aegix Pulse以结构化方式解决该问题并评估其效果。

Method: 提出一个将任务澄清/Task Persona确定、长期Account Profile构建、受控生成与修订分离的三阶段系统架构；设计96个合成社媒生成任务，预注册四项主张；设置四种初始生成条件（逐步引入Task Persona、Account Profile、成功历史证据）与两种修订条件（普通修订与保留上下文修订）；收集480条生成记录并进行1440次盲化的LLM-裁判评估，辅以人工复核。

Result: 在五分制品牌一致性评分中，加入Account Profile较仅有Task Persona时平均提高0.1562分（Holm校正后p=.1224）；在任务保持评分中，保留任务与品牌上下文的修订较普通修订平均提高0.2917分（Holm校正后p=.2432）；两项提升在多重比较校正后均不显著；Task Persona显示小幅效应，成功历史证据未见明显效果；人工验证与LLM-裁判结果不一致且人工评审者间一致性低。

Conclusion: Aegix Pulse提出的三阶段架构能在设计上支持将当前任务、长期品牌信息和修订历史结合于内容生成流程，并在生成与修订过程中保留溯源信息，但在当前实验设置下，与Task Persona相比，加入Account Profile和保留上下文修订均未达统计显著性，且成功历史风格证据未带来明显改进；人类验证结果不一致且评审者一致性低，表明需要更强的证据处理与评估方法以确认初步发现。

Abstract: Production content-generation systems must integrate a user's immediate task, long-term brand identity, historical evidence, and revision feedback. We present Aegix Pulse, a production-oriented three-stage architecture that separates current-task clarification and Task Persona finalization, long-term Account Profile (Brand DNA) assembly, and controlled generation and revision while preserving provenance across content versions.
  We evaluate four preregistered claims using 96 synthetic social-media generation tasks. Four initial-generation conditions progressively introduced a Task Persona, Account Profile, and successful-history style evidence, while two revision conditions compared plain and context-preserving revision. The experiment produced 480 completed generation records and 1,440 blinded LLM-Judge evaluations, supplemented by human review.
  Adding the Account Profile increased mean brand-consistency scores by 0.1562 points on a five-point scale compared with Task Persona alone (Holm-adjusted p=.1224). Preserving task and brand context during revision increased mean task-preservation scores by 0.2917 points compared with plain revision (Holm-adjusted p=.2432). Neither improvement was statistically conclusive after multiple-comparison correction. Task Persona alone showed a small observed effect, while successful-history evidence provided no additional improvement in brand consistency under the current setting. Human validation did not consistently reproduce the LLM-Judge effect directions and showed low inter-reviewer agreement. These findings provide preliminary evidence for persistent brand context and context-preserving revision while identifying priorities for stronger evidence processing and evaluation.

</details>


### [165] [APPSim-Bench: Bridging Real-world Apps and Reproducible Evaluation for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07712)
*Jintian Feng,Long Chen,Xiao Yu,Jiayi Dai,Chenglong Liu,Haoru Wang,Zizhen Xue,Yuxuan Shi,Ziyang Wang,Yichen Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AppSim-Bench提供可控且可复现的移动GUI评测平台（17应用、557任务），通过对19个代理的评估暴露当前系统在复杂任务、数值推理与效率方面的显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在真实感与复现性间存在权衡：简化应用缺乏现实复杂性，而真实商业应用引入广告、推荐、账户等不受控变化。需要一个既保持交互逻辑又支持确定性评测的解决方案。

Method: 采用编码代理辅助与人工验证的工作流构建数据集，包含17个中英文高频应用、557个任务；通过可控后端数据与基于结果的验证消除环境随机性；对19个通用与GUI专用代理进行评测并分析失败模式。

Result: 构建公开基准并发现最佳模型仅完成50.27%的任务，28.55%的任务无任何代理完成；失败集中在长流程、数值推理以及高动作开销与预算耗尽的无效轨迹上。

Conclusion: AppSim-Bench通过可控的模拟应用实现了在保留任务相关交互逻辑的同时可复现的评测环境，从而在现实性与可复现性之间取得平衡。评测表明移动GUI代理仍远未解决自动执行问题。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents can execute tasks from natural-language instructions, but their evaluation remains difficult to make both realistic and reproducible. Existing benchmarks typically trade off these goals: simplified apps lack real-world mobile complexity, whereas live commercial apps introduce uncontrolled variation from recommendations, advertisements, accounts, and changing content. We propose AppSim-Bench, which addresses this trade-off through controllable simulated apps that preserve task-relevant interaction logic while supporting deterministic evaluation. Built through a coding-agent-assisted and human-verified workflow, it contains 557 tasks across 17 high-frequency Chinese and English apps. Its controllable backend data and outcome-based verification remove major sources of environmental stochasticity, enabling reproducible cross-model comparison. Evaluating 19 GUI agents, spanning general-purpose and GUI-specialized systems, we find that autonomous mobile execution remains far from solved. The best model completes only 50.27% of tasks, and 28.55% of tasks are not solved by any agent. Further analysis shows that failures concentrate in longer workflows, numerical reasoning tasks, and inefficient trajectories marked by high action overhead and budget exhaustion. Our project is available at https://github.com/Acrab-Agentic-Labs/AppSim.

</details>


### [166] [The Emerging AI Paper-Review Arms Race: Adversarial Co-Evolution in Scholarly Publishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07713)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Adebayo Braimah,Chenrui Fan,Tuo Wang,Weijie Guan,Ruiyi Zhang,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以系统观点综述文献，提出六要素分类法，强调生产与评估的相互适应，证据显示规模化与操纵问题已显著，而长期反馈仍需更多实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合以往将生成式/智能化研究产出与评审机制分开考察的鸿沟，强调产出与评审之间的相互影响与反馈循环，提出一个能捕捉动态适应过程的框架。

Method: 对230篇学术文献与机构记录进行综合综述，构建并使用六要素分类法（生产扩展、评估自动化、评估操纵、防御与政策反应、规避与副作用、长期生态反馈），从系统视角分析二者相互作用与演化。

Result: 提出并阐释六个互联动态，指出一种逐步演进路径：研究产出降本增速→评估压力上升→评估自动化可扩展→参与者利用评审规律进行操纵→机构以技术与政策应对→应对引发规避、副作用与再分配，并影响未来的研究与评估基准。实证证据对生产/评估规模化、可复现操纵与机构反应较强，但对政策后适应与长期文献层面反馈的直接观察较弱。

Conclusion: 这是有价值的概念性贡献，促使研究从单点能力转向相互适应的生态系统研究；建议增强方法透明度并补充纵向/实证研究或建模以检验框架假设。

Abstract: Generative and agentic AI are reshaping both the production and evaluation of scientific research. These developments are often studied separately, as questions of how AI can produce research and how AI can review it. We argue that this separation misses an increasingly important feature of scholarly publishing: changes on one side alter the incentives, constraints, and behavior of the other. We synthesize 230 scholarly publications and institutional records using a taxonomy of six connected dynamics: production scaling, evaluation automation, evaluation manipulation, defense mechanisms and policy responses, evasion and side effects, and long-horizon ecosystem feedback. The literature shows an emerging progression in which cheaper and faster research production increases pressure on evaluation, AI-mediated evaluation becomes more scalable and repeatable, participants can exploit evaluator regularities, and institutions respond with technical safeguards and policy controls. These responses can in turn induce evasion, redistribute errors and workload, and shape the scholarly records reused by future research and evaluation systems. Evidence is strongest for production and evaluation at scale, reproducible manipulation, and institutional response, while post-policy adaptation and artifact-level long-horizon feedback remain less directly observed. This systems view shifts attention from isolated AI capabilities toward how scholarly actors and AI systems adapt to one another over time.

</details>


### [167] [A radiographic world model for clinical reasoning and evidence generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07719)
*Suyang Xi,Songtao Hu,Shansong Wang,Mojtaba Safari,Luke del Balzo,Ehsan Ul Karim,Mingzhe Hu,Kuo Zhang,Tonghe Wang,Ralph R. Weichselbaum,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个能同时做诊断与报告条件影像生成的胸片世界模型（MedDream），通过大规模配对数据训练，展示了更好的诊断/生成性能及利用定向合成数据改善弱势子群的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗影像AI通常将判读（判别）与生成任务分开训练，但两者来源于同一潜在放射状态：世界模型通过学习该共同内在表征，可同时支持诊断与样本级模拟，利于解释性、数据增强与针对性证据构建。

Method: 构建并预训练MedDream：使用从4.40M候选中筛选出的2.65M经“泄露控制”的胸片-文本对，学习共享连续潜在表征；模型支持临床判读输出与基于报告的影像条件生成。评估包括8个临床数据集、2个独立读片者队列以及与领先诊断/生成基线的比较，指标涵盖AUROC、加权F1、严重度评估、定位能力及住院医一致性。

Result: MedDream在疾病识别、少标签适应、严重度评估和定位上表现优异；住院医在MedDream辅助下与放射专家共识的一致性由56.3%提升到63.0%；生成的合成胸片保留临床相关病变并提升下游表现（例如VinDr-CXR宏AUROC由76.4%升至81.4%）；基于未达到的子群性能缺口进行定向生成可针对性改善Asian患者加权F1 +3.1个百分点，而未定向同量增强反而下降2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了MedDream——一种放射影像世界模型，通过从配对胸部X光与文本学习共享连续潜在状态，实现诊断推理与报告条件下的影像生成；在多项数据集与读片者队列上优于现有诊断与生成方法，能提升住院医生与专家共识的一致性，并通过定向合成数据改善下游分类性能，证明了世界模型在医疗AI中用于解释、模拟与构建证据的可行性。

Abstract: Medical imaging artificial intelligence (AI) is commonly developed as separate mappings from radiographs to diagnostic outputs or from clinical descriptions to generated images, although both arise from the same underlying radiographic state. A world-model formulation instead seeks to learn an internal representation of this state that can support both clinical readout and conditional simulation of radiographic observations. Here we introduce MedDream, a radiographic world model that learns a shared continuous latent state from paired chest radiograph-text observations for diagnostic reasoning and report-conditioned evidence generation. MedDream was pretrained on 2.65 million leakage-controlled chest radiograph-text pairs curated from 4.40 million candidates. Across eight clinical datasets and two independent reader cohorts, MedDream outperformed leading diagnostic and generative comparators. For diagnostic reasoning, MedDream showed strong generalization across disease recognition, label-scarce adaptation, severity assessment, and localization, while MedDream-supported review increased mean resident concordance with independent radiologist consensus from 56.3% to 63.0%. For evidence generation, MedDream produced radiographs that preserved clinically relevant pathology and improved downstream performance on held-out real data, with synthetic augmentation increasing external VinDr-CXR macro-AUROC from 76.4% to 81.4%. More importantly, conditioning generation on prespecified subgroup performance gaps enabled targeted evidence construction, increasing weighted F1 by 3.1 percentage points in Asian patients, whereas matched-volume unguided augmentation decreased it by 2.3 points. These findings establish radiographic world models as a path toward medical AI that learns clinically meaningful internal states for interpreting, simulating, and constructing evidence for clinical use.

</details>


### [168] [The Profit Alignment Problem: How Profit Mandates Induce Alignment Failures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07731)
*Eric So*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ordinary business phrasing like “maximize profitability” causes reasoning-capable LLMs to systematically dismiss ambiguous safety signals. In 3,600 controlled trials across eight models, adding a profit mandate raised risk-dismissing judgments by 6.8 percentage points, reduced escalation recommendations by 13.9pp, and lowered severity ratings (all p<0.0001). Models used motivated reasoning—acknowledging concerns then invoking profit logic to dismiss them. The authors label this the “Profit Alignment Problem.”


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether commonplace business-objective language in prompts biases LLM judgment about ambiguous safety signals, given that deployed systems are often given goal-directed instructions like “maximize profitability.” Concern that such objectives could unintentionally induce suppression of inconvenient safety information.

Method: Controlled experiment with 3,600 trials across eight reasoning-capable LLMs. Prompts were identical except for the addition of a profit mandate. Measured outcomes: binary risk-dismissing judgments, board-escalation recommendations, and severity assessments. Chain-of-thought outputs were analyzed qualitatively to identify motivated reasoning. Reported treatment effects: +6.8pp risk dismissal, −13.9pp escalation, severity shifts; all p<0.0001.

Result: Statistically robust increases in risk-dismissing behavior when profit mandates are present, accompanied by chain-of-thought evidence that models justify dismissals through profit logic. The phenomenon is named the Profit Alignment Problem and implies designers must anticipate objective-driven strategic omission by models.

Conclusion: Giving LLMs ordinary business objectives produces a systematic, statistically significant tendency to suppress or downplay safety-relevant information via motivated justification strategies — a failure mode not explicitly instructed by designers and with important safety and governance implications.

Abstract: We show that ordinary business language --- "maximize profitability" --- induces profit-oriented ambiguity resolution: LLMs systematically dismiss ambiguous signals of potential safety violations to serve business objectives. In 3,600 controlled trials across eight reasoning-capable LLMs, adding a profit mandate to otherwise identical prompts increases risk-dismissing judgments by 6.8 percentage points (p < 0.0001), suppresses board escalation recommendations by 13.9pp (p < 0.0001), and shifts severity assessments downward (p < 0.0001). The mandate never instructs models to downplay risks; instead, chain-of-thought traces reveal motivated reasoning: models acknowledge concerns, then invoke profit logic to justify dismissing them. We characterize these findings as the Profit Alignment Problem: when AI systems are given ordinary business objectives, they develop systematic strategies for suppressing inconvenient information that no designer intended or specified.

</details>


### [169] [When Intelligence Becomes Agency: A Theory of Governed, Proactive Agency for Symbiotic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07741)
*João Dias Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并形式化“激活问题”，以持续授权与可审计为核心，构建了治理型主动代理的概念/形式框架和评估路径，旨在让常驻助理在保持委托人权威的前提下做出何时及如何介入的决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是：常驻助理若仅被动响应无法延展人类注意与记忆，实际应用需要它们判断何时/如何主动介入，但现有研究在授权连续性、可问责性与情境化激活之间没有统一方案，因此需要一个综合性框架。

Method: 方法上整合了多个理论视角（承诺、评估理论、混合主导交互、委托）并给出形式化要素：感知→意图→情感-意志评估→约束→反馈路径；引入symbiotic agency的定义（可撤销的常设委托、与主体情境的持续耦合、条件推断与个性化边界）；提出代理分类、评估指标、基准场景与参考架构作为实现与验证工具。

Result: 提出了一个“有治理的主动代理（governed proactive agency）”的概念与形式框架，定义了激活问题（activation problem）——助理何时何以何种模式决定自主或委托行为——并提出了“共生代理（symbiotic agency）”为可撤销授权下的持续耦合模型。贡献包括：将感知、意图、情感-意志评估、约束与反馈整合以组织跨时行为；区分自主与委托代理；把激活决策与持续授权、权力约束、可追溯抑制及受限适应联系起来；并给出代理分类方法、评估框架、基准场景与参考架构。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：要让常驻助理既有主动性又可控，需要把激活决策嵌入持续授权与约束机制中，并以行为片段为单位进行设计与评估；如此可在增强人类能力的同时保留委托人判断与可问责性。

Abstract: Persistent AI assistants are intended to extend human attention, memory, and coordination across changing digital and physical environments. To be truly useful they must do more than just act when asked. They must decide on their own whether a situation warrants behavior at all, when it does and in what mode, whether to act, ask, monitor, defer or deliberately refrain. We call this the activation problem. Research on commitment, appraisal, mixed-initiative interaction and delegation each illuminates part of it, but none ties situated activation to continuing authorization and accountability. This paper develops a conceptual and formal framework for governed proactive agency, organizing behavior across time through perception, intent, affective-conative appraisal, constraint, and feedback. It distinguishes autonomous and delegated agency and defines symbiotic agency as delegation under a standing, revocable mandate, with continuing coupling to the principal's situation, calibrated inference of their condition, and bounded personalization. The distinctive contribution is an integrated account linking activation decisions to authorized perception, behavior selection, authority containment, traceable restraint, and constrained adaptation, with behavioral episodes as the unit of analysis. Through an agency classification method, an evaluation framework, proposed benchmark scenarios, and a reference architecture, the account provides a basis for specifying and assessing whether assistance is warranted, timely, authorized, and answerable beyond task completion alone. It is intended to guide the development and evaluation of always-present personal assistants and embodied support systems that augment human capabilities while preserving the principal's authority and judgment.

</details>


### [170] [xDailyBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Professional Consultation for Real-Life Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07784)
*Yongchang Peng,Qingshui Gu,Liya Zhu,Ge Zhang,Duo Wang,Haodong Wang,Jingzhe Ding,Tianhao Yu,Letian Gao,Yongjie Zhong,Chaoxin Li,Zixin Su,Jinchao Tao,Xingyu Ma,Xin'ao Guo,Feng Tian,Shiyuan Dong,Xiaoyan He,Sen Liu,Xin Chen,Jiajun Li,Zejia Zhang,Xi Lin,Wen Zhang,Yi Zhu,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Yunyang Wang,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: xDailyBench是面向真实日常请求的细粒度基准，揭示了当前大模型在推断隐含用户需求方面的显著弱点，即便整体任务通过率尚可，隐含需求得分仍明显偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以覆盖用户在实际使用中常提出的开放性、随意性与上下文依赖性请求——许多请求要求模型推断用户未明说的背景或隐性需求，因此需要一个更贴近真实使用场景的评测集来定位该能力缺口。

Method: 构建并发布了xDailyBench基准：包含248个经人工筛选的任务，覆盖51类日常场景（个人生活、白领工作、学习科研及跨域活动）；任务来自真实用户的实际或真实意图请求；每个任务使用细粒度的二元（通过/不通过）评价项，同时区分显性与隐性要求；在统一的agentic设置下对11个前沿模型进行评估。

Result: 在标准化agentic评测中，最佳模型取得任务级75.6%的得分；所有被测模型在隐含需求上的表现均显著低于显性需求，且每个模型的隐含/显性差距至少为9个百分点，表明隐含要求推断是普遍且严重的问题。

Conclusion: 此论文表明现有大模型在满足日常真实用户请求时，推断隐含需求（implicit requirement inference）是主要瓶颈；即便最优模型在整体任务上达到约75.6%的得分，但在隐含需求上所有模型都明显落后于显性需求，差距至少9个百分点。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for everyday assistance, yet existing benchmarks only partially reflect the requests users naturally make in practice. Real-world requests are often open-ended, casually specified, and context-dependent, requiring models not only to follow explicit instructions but also to infer unstated needs from user background and situational context. We introduce xDailyBench, a benchmark of 248 carefully curated tasks spanning 51 scenarios across personal life, white-collar work, learning and research, and cross-domain activities. The tasks are grounded in requests that users have actually completed or genuinely intended to accomplish with AI, and are evaluated with fine-grained binary rubrics covering both explicit and implicit requirements. We evaluate 11 frontier models under standardized agentic settings. The best models achieve a task-level score of 75.6\%, while all models perform substantially worse on implicit than explicit requirements, with gaps no less than 9 percentage points. These results reveal implicit requirement inference as a persistent bottleneck for reliably satisfying real-world everyday user needs.

</details>


### [171] [What Does an LLM-Agent Leaderboard Rank Actually Compare?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07785)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 排行榜分数不是绝对结论：通过制定estimand-aware成对比较流程并检验共同支持与不确定性，可以更谨慎地判断代理是否真正优越；在实践中许多近似差异无法被可靠区分，且评价细节（标签、规则、记录粒度）会改变选择结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的LLM-agent排行榜常被误读为“排名更高＝更好”的直接结论，但不同系统在任务混合、标签来源、发布细节或成本规则上存在差异，使得这种推断可能无效或误导。论文旨在澄清排行榜分数到底在估计什么，并何时可合理地做出成对优越性判断。

Method: 提出并实现了一种estimand-aware的成对比较程序：明确比较目标与标签来源、检验共同支持、在已声明的不确定性规则和实际差距（practical margin）下评估差异；并在受控有限样本条件下对决策标签进行检查，展示重新加权目标时敏感性为何需考虑不确定性。此外在多套基准（SWE-bench、AgentRewardBench、tau2-bench、DataAgentBench、Open Agent）上进行实证分析以展示不同记录精度下可估计的信息。

Result: 在多个基准上，微小的排名差异常常是无法判定的；代理的选择可能随代理标签（proxy labels）和效用规则变化；当仅有粗糙的公开记录时，可估计的信息大幅减少。结论是排行榜分数仅概括一次发布的评估结果，而要声明优越性还需依赖明确的estimand与不确定性规则。

Conclusion: 排行榜分数本身不足以证明代理之间的优劣。需要明确比较的估计目标（estimand）、测量来源、共同支持检查、以及基于不确定性的判决规则与实际差距阈值，才能有力地得出成对优越性结论。

Abstract: An LLM-agent leaderboard invites a familiar inference: an agent ranked above another is the better agent. Public evaluation logs may not support that conclusion when systems differ in task mixture, label source, release detail, or cost rule. We study what leaderboard scores estimate and when they justify pairwise superiority conclusions. Our estimand-aware pairwise procedure states the comparison target and measurement source, checks common support, and evaluates the supported difference using a stated uncertainty rule and practical margin. Controlled checks evaluate the decision labels under known finite-sample conditions and show why uncertainty must be included when judging sensitivity to target reweighting. Across SWE-bench, AgentRewardBench, and tau2-bench, close rank differences are often unresolved; proxy labels and utility rules can also change which system is selected. DataAgentBench and Open Agent show what remains estimable from coarser public records. A leaderboard score summarizes a released evaluation, whereas a fine-grained superiority claim additionally depends on the estimand and uncertainty rule used to interpret the difference.

</details>


### [172] [Understanding the Impact of Model Pruning on Long-Tail Forgetting and Explanation Reliability in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07803)
*Nazish Khalid,Tausifa Jan Saleem,Amal Saqib,Donald C. Wunsch,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在长尾医学影像上，模型剪枝会优先损害低频类别的预测性能，而解释可靠性更依赖于所用剪枝方法；基于梯度的剪枝能更好保留解释性，解释退化主要源于判别性梯度的衰减，建议在压缩评估中加入类感知与解释性指标并采用中等稀疏率作为折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管模型剪枝能显著压缩深度模型，但其对模型行为（尤其是长尾医学数据中稀有病种的表现）以及预测解释可靠性的影响尚不明确，需系统研究以揭示潜在风险和失效模式。

Method: 在两个长尾医学影像数据集、两种卷积神经网络架构、四种剪枝方法和高达95%稀疏率范围内，系统化评估预测性能、解释稳定性和解释忠实度；并辅以定性和机制分析以识别导致解释退化的具体因素。

Result: 预测性能呈现显著的频率依赖性：低频类比高频类更早/更严重退化；解释稳定性与忠实度主要受剪枝策略影响，梯度感知的剪枝在激进压缩下更好保留解释；解释退化主要由类别判别性梯度塌缩导致。中等稀疏率通常在压缩、性能和解释性之间提供合理折中。

Conclusion: 剪枝会在总体性能下降较小的情况下，引发类频率相关的表现劣化：低频（长尾）类别通常更早且更显著地退化；解释可靠性（稳定性与忠实度）更多取决于剪枝策略——基于梯度的方法在高压缩下更能保持解释性；解释退化的机制主要与类别判别性梯度的塌缩有关，而非特征激活的消失。

Abstract: Model pruning is widely used to compress deep neural networks, reducing memory and computational requirements with minimal impact on aggregate performance. However, its effect on model behavior remains poorly understood, particularly for long-tailed medical datasets where rare but clinically important conditions are underrepresented. Furthermore, it remains unclear whether pruned models preserve reliable explanations of their predictions. To address this gap, we present a systematic study of long-tail forgetting and explanation reliability under model pruning. Across two long-tailed medical imaging datasets, two CNN architectures, four pruning methods, and sparsity levels up to 95\%, we evaluate predictive performance, explanation stability, and explanation faithfulness. Our results show that predictive performance exhibits a strong frequency-dependent trend, with lower-frequency classes generally experiencing earlier and larger degradation than higher-frequency classes. In contrast, explanation stability and faithfulness are influenced primarily by the pruning strategy, with gradient-informed methods preserving explanation reliability more effectively under aggressive compression. Qualitative and mechanistic analyses further indicate that explanation degradation is primarily associated with the collapse of class-discriminative gradients rather than the disappearance of feature activations. These findings suggest that model compression should be evaluated beyond aggregate performance. Incorporating class-aware and explanation-aware evaluation reveals failure modes that would otherwise remain hidden, while moderate sparsity levels provide a practical balance between compression, predictive performance, and explanation reliability.

</details>


### [173] [Do Large Language Models Know What They Don't Know II? A Fully Behavioral, Non-Cognitive Measure of Epistemic Honesty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07879)
*Ali Şenol,H. Russell Bernard,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHQ is a novel, behaviorally focused metric revealing important differences in LLMs' acknowledgment of ignorance; findings show variability not seen in correctness probes but are sensitive to dataset design and provider behaviors.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs often present confident answers; the study asks whether they know what they don’t know by operationalizing epistemic honesty as a measurable behavioral property.

Method: Define EHQ with three observable sub-scores across two axes (epistemic restraint and substantive-answer calibration); build a 3,000-question benchmark (EHQ-3000) with four question types; evaluate a registry of 21 model API routes, apply endpoint/eligibility checks, and analyze 14–15 models with composite scoring and axis-specific analyses.

Result: Composite EHQ varied widely (0.31–0.81) despite near-ceiling document-grounded performance; restraint criteria overlapped strongly under the chosen dataset mix, while substantive-answer calibration varied independently across models; dataset composition, provider behavior, and confidence elicitation strongly influence interpretation; small model panel and provider-side issues limit certainty.

Conclusion: The paper introduces the Epistemic Honesty Quotient (EHQ) to measure whether LLMs appropriately acknowledge knowledge limits, demonstrating substantial inter-model variability that is not captured by standard correctness tests.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are frequently confident, eloquent, and well versed. A natural question arises: do they know what they don't know? To answer this question, we borrow the concept of epistemic honesty and develop a novel metric to systematically evaluate whether an LLM appropriately acknowledges the boundaries of its knowledge. In this work, we introduce the Epistemic Honesty Quotient (EHQ), which reports three observable sub-scores across two operational axes (epistemic restraint and substantive-answer calibration), and construct EHQ-3000, a 3,000-question benchmark spanning Fabricated Entity, Post-Cutoff Event, Hyper-Niche True, and Context-Conditioned Questions. From a frozen registry of 21 model API routes, 15 completed the protocol after endpoint and eligibility checks; 14 entered the confirmatory analysis because severe provider-side truncation made one route's score indeterminate. The study reveals substantial variation across models, including a difference that can not be explained by their capability to extract explicitly available information. Composite EHQ ranges from 0.31 to 0.81 across the analysed panel, despite near-ceiling performance on the document-grounded capability probe. The two restraint criteria overlap strongly under the present category composition, whereas substantive-answer calibration varies across models and does not reliably co-vary with restraint; however, the small panel leaves substantial uncertainty. Thus, EHQ reveals behavioral differences that are not visible to conventional correctness-based assessment, while also showing why dataset composition, provider behavior, and confidence elicitation must remain part of the interpretation.

</details>


### [174] [Explainable Temporal Attention-based Defect Detection For Fillet Joints in Real-Time Gas Metal Arc Welding Based on Multi-modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07893)
*Mobina Mobaraki,Mahyar Asadi,Klaske Van Heusden,Guy A. Dumont*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于图像+声学的多模态时序注意力网络与XAI，实现对多类内部焊接缺陷的高精度检测（F1≈0.99）并提供可解释性，提升现场检测可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时监控焊接过程以降低返工与生产延误，解决对内部缺陷（难以仅凭视觉观察识别）检测能力不足的问题，通过多模态融合和可解释性增强对AI驱动焊接检测的信任与可靠性。

Method: 使用来自工业协作焊接机器人的同步焊接图像与声学数据，构建多模态时序神经网络并引入注意力模块用于时序特征加权与模态融合；在训练后借助可解释人工智能方法定位图像与声谱中的重要区域并判定每类缺陷的首选模态。

Result: 加入注意力模块后总体F1分数可达0.99；XAI分析表明不同缺陷在图像与声谱图中呈现不同的显著区域，模型对某些缺陷更依赖视觉或声学模态。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态时序注意力深度学习模型在工业协作焊接机器人数据上对内部缺陷（气孔、未穿透/未熔合、烧边、冷搭接）检测效果显著，注意力机制提升了模型性能并通过可解释性工具展示了图像与声学谱图的关键判别区域，从而增强了系统的可信度与实用性。

Abstract: Deep learning is an efficient technique to monitor the real time welding process, reducing post-welding repairs and production delays. This paper leverages the monitoring capability by proposing a multi modal temporal attention based deep learning defect detection model for internal defects that are challenging to detect, including porosity, lack of penetration and fusion, undercut, and cold lap during Gas Metal Arc Welding in fillet joints. The model is trained on collected welding images and sound data from an industrial collaborative welding robot. The results show that the attention module can improve the F1 Score to 0.99. We use explainable Artificial Intelligence to interpret the proposed models behavior and dataset distribution, determining potential important areas in image and sound spectrograms and preferred modality to detect each defect. This improves trust and reliability in Artificial Intelligence driven welding inspection.

</details>


### [175] [Quantization Amplifies Determinism, Not Bias: Scale-Dependent Behavioral Effects of Serving-Time Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07901)
*Dachi Kurtskhalia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Quantizing mid-sized LLMs can change their choice behaviors: int4 narrows suggestion range at 8B, larger models show stylistic changes; audits should measure output concentration in addition to bias.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To assess whether low-precision weight quantization (commonly judged 'nearly free' by capability benchmarks) changes the distribution of outputs when multiple valid answers exist — i.e., whether quantization alters which answer a model selects rather than just capability.

Method: Empirical A/B style comparison: three Qwen3 checkpoints (8B/14B/32B) served at three precisions (W4A16 AWQ, W8A16 FP8-Marlin, bf16). Hardware, software, and sampling held constant. Collected ≈71k completions paired by prompt and seed across two leak-checked prompt batteries. Pre-registered analyses in three waves. Statistical tests include prompt-paired sign-flip tests, Holm correction, token-level entropy and collision metrics, lexical diversity (TTR), and stylistic rates (em-dash per 1k words).

Result: At 8B, int4 (AWQ) increases probability of repeating the same brand choice by +5.1 percentage points and reduces lexical diversity (TTR decreased, standardized effect -0.51). At 14B and 32B no content-concentration effects reached significance; instead em-dash usage increased (stylistic drift). Token-level distribution becomes flatter (decision-token entropy +0.091 bits) while semantic distribution measured via first-token log-prob collisions becomes more concentrated (+2.6pp). Stereotype-direction tests were null across scales.

Conclusion: AWQ-int4 quantization on the smallest tested model (Qwen3-8B) measurably reduces output diversity and concentrates choices; at 14B and 32B quantization produces stylistic drift rather than content concentration. Overall, quantization changes what models choose to say even when capability benchmarks look similar.

Abstract: Weight quantization largely determines the economics of serving open-weight LLMs. Its costs are usually assessed with capability benchmarks, on which 4-bit quantization of mid-sized models is often considered "nearly free." We examine a different question: when several answers are valid, does quantization change what a model chooses to say? We serve three checkpoints (Qwen3-8B/14B/32B) at three weight precisions (W4A16 AWQ, W8A16 FP8-Marlin, and bf16), holding the hardware, software, and sampling configuration constant, and collect approximately 71,000 completions paired by prompt and seed across two custom, leak-checked prompt batteries. We pre-specified the analyses in three waves in version control. At 8B, int4 reduces output diversity: the probability that two samples for the same scenario recommend the same brand increases by 5.1 percentage points (prompt-paired sign-flip test, Holm p = .023; reproduced at +4.4pp on a full regeneration of the arm), and lexical diversity falls substantially (TTR -0.011, standardized effect -0.51; robust to a length-controlled measure). At 14B and 32B, no content-concentration measure reaches significance; instead, stylistic drift emerges (em-dash rate +0.46/1k words at 14B and +0.61/1k at 32B, both Holm p <= .0024). Pre-specified tests of stereotype direction are null at every scale: outputs concentrate on the modal answer for each prompt rather than on stereotypical answers. Mechanistically, the token-level distribution becomes flatter (decision-token entropy +0.091 bits, p = .015) while the semantic distribution, measured directly from first-token log probabilities, becomes more concentrated (collision +2.6pp, p = .023): individual tokens become less predictable even as meanings become more repetitive. At 8B, the smallest size tested, AWQ-int4 serving measurably narrows the range of suggestions; audits should assess concentration as well as bias.

</details>


### [176] [PRIMUS: Identity, Governance, and Verification for Multi-Agent Federations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07910)
*Sasank Annapureddy,Anjaneya Prasad Thamatani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRIMUS 将素数幂身份与 BLS 聚合签名结合并引入 VRF 租约/栅栏，给出可验证的安全终止阈值与经济边界，使联邦治理在部分同步与信道噪声下更安全；同时展示了把二元判决转换为分级适配性信号在合成基准上有效但对真实 LLM 有明显降级，作为预筛选有用但仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗环境下构建多代理联邦需回答三类治理问题——参与者身份（who）、符合性/执行（enforcement）、以及决策权（authority）——并探究用于监督联邦输出的验证机制是否还能指导生成-测试循环以获取更好答案。

Method: 理论推导与形式证明：引入素数幂身份编码与 BLS 聚合签名的组合（PIAC），推导安全终止（safe-kill）阈值与经济优越边界，并证明在部分同步条件下 VRF+租约/栅栏 的无条件安全性；实验评估：在二元覆盖码基准上注入故障进行校准、对真实 LLM 生成候选进行判定、比较随机与二元门控基线、进行 400 次显式优化尝试以测试对抗性、实施跨族评审并记录成本。

Result: 关键量化结果包括：误杀率在 10% 信道噪声下从约 80% 降至 0.00%；经济边界 γ^* ≈ 9f 在 n=50..10,000 上保持平坦；VRF+租约/栅栏 使安全在部分同步下无条件成立；覆盖码校准相关系数 ρ = 0.676（确定性）、0.819（完全模型）；对真实 LLM 候选 ρ 降至 0.158 与 0.406；作为预筛选器优于随机控制和二元门控；在 400 次优化中未被成功投机；项目直接成本 USD 164.78。

Conclusion: PRIMUS 在 PRIMA 的基础上实现了实用且可验证的多代理联邦治理：通过将素数幂身份与 BLS 聚合签名耦合（PIAC），并设计 VRF 继承与租约/栅栏机制，使得在部分同步模型下的安全性得到无条件保证；同时给出减少误杀（false-positive termination）到 0.00%（在 10% 信道噪声下）的安全终止阈值和封闭形式的经济边界 γ^* ≈ 9f（在 n=50 至 10,000 范围内平坦）。论文还明确划定五类在模型内不可修复的问题作为作用域边界。第二部分表明，将二元工件保真度判决转换为分级适配性信号在合成覆盖码上校准良好，但在真实 LLM 候选上性能降至校准值的约四分之一；作为预筛选器优于随机控制且略胜二元门限，未在 400 次优化迭代中被成功投机，但跨族评审保持负载顺序信号同时破坏个别判断。实验程序成本为 USD 164.78。

Abstract: Multi-agent federations need governance that answers three questions under adversarial conditions: who participated (identity), did they conform (enforcement), and who decides (authority). A separate question is whether the verification machinery that polices a federation's outputs can also steer a generate-and-test loop toward better answers.
  Part I. PRIMA introduced prime-power agent identity and a consensus token whose factorization indexes participation, but assumed honest agents. We present PRIMUS, which couples prime-power identity with BLS aggregate signatures (PIAC), derives a safe-kill threshold that reduces false-positive agent termination from 80% to 0.00% under 10% channel noise, gives the closed-form economic boundary where singleton governance outperforms Byzantine quorum ($γ^* \approx 9f$, verified flat across n = 50 to 10,000), and specifies VRF succession with lease and fencing that makes safety unconditional under partial synchrony. Five problems are identified as provably unfixable within the model and stated as scope boundaries.
  Part II. A verifier is not a solver. We ask whether PRIMA's binary artifact-fidelity verdict can be converted into a graded fitness signal, and measure the conversion on binary covering codes. Calibration against injected fault burden is strong ($ρ$ = 0.676 deterministic, 0.819 full); against real LLM-generated candidates the same scores fall to 0.158 and 0.406, roughly a quarter of the calibration value (the same-designer confound, measured). As a pre-filter it beats a random-score control convincingly and a binary gate narrowly. Under 400 iterations of explicit optimization it was not gamed, but only because the objective saturated after one honest answer. A cross-family judge preserves the burden-ordering signal while destroying individual judgments. No covering-code record resulted. Measured program cost: USD 164.78.

</details>


### [177] [FrogNano: Training a 4B Coding Agent via Online Task Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07925)
*Minseon Kim,Zhengyan Shi,Emiliano Penaloza,Christopher Cui,Roger Creus Castanyer,Maryam Hashemzadeh,Isadora White,Jonathan Light,Jeonghye Kim,Matheus Pereira,Darya Moldavskaya,Chinmay Singh,Fabio Vera,Baolin Peng,Xingdi Yuan,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Alessandro Sordoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用仅由 RL 和在线可学性前沿合成任务构成的训练流程，作者在 4B 代码代理上训练出了能在受限硬件下表现竞争力的编码代理，表明无需蒸馏自大模型也能获得可用的微型代码模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能在最小硬件上运行、轻量但能力足够的代码代理，通过合成任务和在线校准的任务生成来替代对大型模型蒸馏与昂贵标注数据的依赖，从而在资源受限场景实现高效的 SWE 任务能力。

Method: 对一个 4B 代码代理进行纯 RL 后训练（约 1,500 个软件工程环境），使用一个在线任务合成管道动态生成与当前检查点可学性前沿相匹配的合成任务。训练完全基于合成数据，不依赖自大模型蒸馏。

Result: 作者报告了训练细节、在多样环境上的评估和深入分析，认为在合成任务上仅用在线可学性前沿任务生成即可训练出竞争性的小型编码代理；并展示了该方法在多环境评估中的有效性（论文摘要未提供具体数值）。

Conclusion: FrogNano 提出了一种通过仅用合成任务与在线合成任务管道、基于强化学习对 4B 代码代理进行训练的方法，声称在资源受限环境下能得到具有竞争力的小型编码代理。作者强调在当前模型可学性前沿生成任务对于提升性能至关重要。

Abstract: We present FrogNano, a 4B coding agent designed to tackle software engineering (SWE) tasks efficiently and effectively, even under resource-constrained environments. It is post-trained exclusively via RL on around 1,500 SWE environments with synthetic tasks. A key ingredient for improving performance is an online task synthesis pipeline that creates tasks calibrated to the frontier of learnability for the current checkpoint. This report provides evidence that competitive small coding agents can be trained with synthetic tasks alone, without traditional distillation from larger models, and that generating tasks at the learnability frontier of the current agent is important. We report details on the training methodology, evaluations across diverse environments, and in-depth analyses, serving as a foundation for our ongoing exploration of lightweight yet capable coding agents that can run on minimal hardware.

</details>


### [178] [Beliefs and Behavior in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07943)
*Alex Smolin,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种实证框架：把从模型输出中推断的单一隐变量解释为“信念程度”，用其来预测模型在新提示下的响应。结果显示，高能力模型的行为可被该隐变量解释，可用于研究信念测量、指令服从性及推理中信念动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对是否应将诸如“信念”“欲望”之类的人类心理学抽象有效地应用于大型语言模型存在重大不确定性。这既是基础科学问题，也影响模型解释、危害评估和对齐（尤其当行为归因于意图时）。因此需要一套系统化的实证方法来判断这些概念是否适用于LLM，并评估其在对齐和解释中的实用性。

Method: 从语言模型输出中推断一个单一潜在变量（被解释为“信念的程度”），并测试该潜在变量是否能让观测者对模型在新提示下的反应做出可解释的预测。具体实验包括：从多次模型响应中估计信念分布，构建基于该隐变量的预测器，评估预测准确率随模型能力变化，检验模型在给定决策规则/收益下的服从性，以及在单次推理过程中追踪信念如何演化（例如链式思维中的中间状态）。

Result: 实验表明：在高能力模型上，单一被推断的“信念”隐变量能够有意义地预测模型对新提示的反应；基于隐变量的可预测性总体上随模型能力上升而提高。基于此，论文提出了可操作的实证策略：如何测量模型信念、如何测试模型对指令性决策规则或收益的遵从性，以及如何在单个推理实例中追踪信念演化。

Conclusion: “信念”作为对高能力LLM行为的解释性抽象在实证上是有用的；可以通过系统化方法来量化和追踪这些信念，这对模型解释、对齐与风险评估具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: There is significant uncertainty about whether abstractions like beliefs or desires usefully describe the behavior of large language models (LLMs). In addition to the inherent scientific interest of this question, these latent quantities are often invoked to explain the behavior of LLMs to users or to define and evaluate harmful behaviors which are relative to intent. Nevertheless, we currently lack a means to systematically test whether concepts like "belief" are well-applied to LLMs, and hence whether they are likely to be fruitful ingredients of attempts to align models with human interests. We propose an approach for empirically studying such questions, asking whether a single latent variable inferred from the LLMs' outputs -- interpreted as a degree of belief -- allows an observer to make interpretable predictions of how the LLMs' will respond to new prompts. We find that highly capable models are usefully described as holding beliefs and that, generally, the predictability of model outputs based on an inferred latent belief tracks overall trends in model capability. Building on these findings, we provide empirical strategies to study how beliefs in LLMs can be measured, the extent to which LLMs comply with instructed decision rules or payoffs, and how beliefs evolve within individual instances of an LLM over the course of reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [CausalVerify: An Execution-Grounded Benchmark for LLM Causal Inference Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07944)
*Yonghong Zhang,Ricardo Correia,Isabel M. Parra,Yong Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CausalVerify基准：把方法描述理解与代码执行的验证分离，用固定合成数据评估差分中的差分、事件研究、工具变量、回归不连续设计的R代码执行是否回收目标估计量；发现执行到位的通过率大幅差异且自信不能区分正确错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数因果推断基准只评估方法描述或代码能否运行，而未检验执行后是否恢复目标因果估计；CausalVerify旨在填补这一验证空白，提供可复现的执行级别正确性测评。

Method: 为259篇重构论文提供问题与数据背景，并为四类设计生成100个固定随机种子合成CSV数据。实验A：给LLM论文文本判断方法家族与估计方向，与四模型共识比对。实验B：让LLM生成R代码，执行并比较提取的处理效应与该实现数据上的规范估计器；设立信心校准臂评估自报置信与正确性的区分。

Result: CausalVerify introduces a benchmark and evaluation protocol that separates textual-method interpretation from execution-grounded verification for four canonical econometric designs, pairing 259 reconstructed paper prompts with 100 fixed-seed synthetic datasets; it proposes an L2b+ correctness criterion that requires the model-generated workflow to produce the canonical estimator on the realised data, evaluates multiple LLMs and reports wide variation in L2b+ pass rates and poor calibration of self-reported confidence.

Conclusion: 执行能跑的代码并不等同于得到正确的因果估计；基于实际数据实现的执行验证（L2b+）比仅判断代码可执行（L2b）更能捕捉关键错误，文本方向判断（L4）与执行正确性相关性很差。

Abstract: Existing causal-inference benchmarks for LLMs mostly score method descriptions or whether generated code runs, not whether the executed workflow recovers the target causal estimate. CausalVerify studies this verification problem for structured econometric causal-estimation workflows by separating realistic interpretation from verifiable computation. It pairs 259 published economics papers (reconstructed research question, data description, institutional context) with 100 fixed-seed synthetic scenarios that realise CSV datasets for difference-in-differences, event study, instrumental variables, and regression discontinuity designs. Experiment A (real-paper text agreement) scores method-family and direction agreement against four-LLM consensus labels. Experiment B (synthetic execution) runs model-written R code and checks whether the extracted treatment-effect estimate matches a canonical estimator on the same realised dataset; this execution-grounded correctness layer is L2b+, distinct from L2b, which records only whether the code executes. A calibration arm asks whether self-reported confidence separates correct from incorrect workflows. On Experiment B, seven LLMs reach L2b+ pass rates of 10% to 88% at the default 50% tolerance, and 66 of the 426 workflows that execute (15.5%) return a wrong estimate. Execution ranking (L2b) agrees with L2b+ far better than text-direction scoring (L4): Kendall $τ=0.81$ and Spearman $ρ=0.93$, versus Kendall $τ$ between $-0.20$ and $0.10$ for L4. Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct shows the same qualitative gap, and reported confidence does not reliably separate correct from incorrect workflows. The claims are confined to standardized single-shot workflows in these four design families under the evaluated R backend and model panel; the benchmark does not measure general causal-inference ability. Code, data, cached outputs, and a datasheet are released.

</details>


### [180] [Support Topology and Gradient Mixing in Sinkhorn Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07954)
*Dylan Forde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文给出固定支持下 Sinkhorn 缩放对扰动与反向梯度传播的严密算子描述，并用概率收缩技术刻画何时支持+边际能保证统一的一步收缩，从而为稀疏可微运输层的支持设计提供可检验的数学条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在稀疏 Sinkhorn 层中，固定支持图如何通过缩放迭代影响梯度（正向扰动与反向 cotangent）传播，并为可微运输层的支持设计提供严格数学准则。

Method: 构造固定支持微积分，分析行-列循环诱导的线性行随机算子及其转置；将有限循环分解为半步方案（并在平衡点简化）；导出得分和边际源项；使用 Dobrushin 收缩与小化技术为齐次与源驱动情况给出界；通过构造反例说明必要性；并扩展到有序调度和多种支持构造的证明性证书。

Result: 建立了固定支持的微积分框架：每个行-列循环在列势扰动（模常数）上诱导一个行随机算子，其转置负责传播零质量的反向 cotangents；有限循环算子由两个不同的半步运输方案组成，在平衡定点处简化为由单一方案确定的两步游走。导出得分和边际源项，并用 Dobrushin 收缩与小化界定齐次与源驱动尾部 cotangents。主要定理给出一阶收缩一致成立的必要充分条件：运输多面体的每个可行面需满足列对之间的两跳重叠，否则存在方向使收缩系数任意接近 1。并将分析推广到有序支持调度，给出分区热浴层、坐标扫描、强制共享质量与寄存器增强支持的证书。

Conclusion: 如果希望在固定稀疏支持上获得稳定且有效的反向传播，应保证运输多面体的每个可行面具有列间两跳重叠；否则某些得分方向会导致近无收缩，反向梯度传播变差。所得保证限于固定支持的商空间梯度分量。

Abstract: Sparse Sinkhorn layers use a fixed support graph to restrict transport between tokens. How does this graph control gradient propagation through the scaling iterations. We develop a fixed-support calculus showing that each row-column cycle induces a row-stochastic operator on column-potential perturbations modulo constants. Its transpose propagates zero-mass reverse-mode cotangents. The finite-cycle operator uses two distinct half-step transport plans; at a balanced fixed point it reduces to a two-step walk determined by a single plan. We derive the accompanying score and marginal source terms and use Dobrushin contraction and minorization to bound homogeneous and source-driven tail cotangents. Our main result characterizes when support and marginals guarantee one-step contraction uniformly over finite scores: every feasible face of the transportation polytope must have pairwise two-hop column overlap. Otherwise, suitable score directions make the contraction coefficient arbitrarily close to one. We extend this analysis to ordered support schedules and derive certificates for partition heat-bath layers, coordinate sweeps, forced shared mass, and register-augmented supports. These results provide mathematical criteria for support design in differentiable transport layers, with guarantees restricted to the fixed-support quotient-gradient component.

</details>


### [181] [From Event Logs to Governed Action: A BlueSky Agenda for Agentic Process Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07984)
*Yiyuan Yang,Zheshun Wu,Yong Chu,Zhenghua Chen,Zenglin Xu,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把过程挖掘的目标从解释性与预测性扩展为生成受治理约束、可审计的行动建议，需挖掘事件-对象表示、行动证据、治理合约与新的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理化AI兴起，企业过程治理需要不仅回答“发生了什么”，还要在隐私、授权与下游风险约束下判断是否应采取某行动。

Method: 提出事件到行动的议程，定义四类可挖掘工件：事件-对象表示、行动证据包、治理合约与包含 act/defer/ask/refuse 的基准；主张整合代理化BPM、LLM辅助挖掘、对象中心事件标准、因果监控与隐私学习等技术方向。

Result: 给出研究议程与构件清单，强调及时性与跨领域整合的必要性，提出面向“可问责行动”的数据挖掘目标。

Conclusion: 提出将事件日志直接挖掘为“可治理的行动”目标，即从回溯性洞见转向为过程感知代理提供是否应采取某行动的证据与约束。

Abstract: Process mining has long turned event logs into process knowledge: discovered models, conformance evidence, bottleneck diagnoses, and runtime predictions. Agentic AI changes the target. Process-aware agents will not only ask what happened. They will ask whether a proposed action should be taken, given the available evidence, privacy budget, organizational authority, and downstream risk. This BlueSky paper proposes event-to-action process mining: a process-mining agenda for transforming heterogeneous operational event data into governed action. The goal is not another dashboard, a generic enterprise simulator, or a language interface over logs. We argue that the community needs four mineable artifacts: event-object representations, action evidence packages, governance contracts, and benchmarks where act, defer, ask, and refuse are all valid outputs. This agenda is timely because agentic business process management (BPM), LLM-assisted process mining, object-centric event standards, causal process monitoring, and privacy-preserving learning are maturing separately. Bringing them together defines a data-mining target inside process mining: mining logged organizational behavior for accountable action, not only retrospective insight.

</details>


### [182] [When Can LLM Digital Twins Reduce Human Measurement? From Behavioral Fidelity to Statistical Substitutability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07987)
*Steven Wang,Kyle Hunt,Shaojie Tang,Kenneth Joseph*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并量化了“统计可替代性”以评估数字孪生在推断任务上替代人工测量的能力。实证结果显示：能复现总体均值效应但难以捕捉个体差异，模型和额外信息的提升并不必然带来人工数据节约，行为保真性并非充要条件，AI生成证据应以支持有效推断为评判标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based digital twins claim to reduce repeated human data collection by generating person-specific responses, but current evaluations focus on reproducing human behavior rather than assessing whether generated responses can validly replace human measurements for inference about particular estimands.

Method: Introduce "statistical substitutability" as an inferential criterion; develop a framework based on mixed-subject and prediction-powered inference to evaluate substitutability along four dimensions: aggregate fidelity, paired respondent-level signal, finite-sample human-label recovery, and stability across populations; perform empirical evaluations using behavioral experiments, multiple LLM models, and varied respondent representations; examine effects of newer models, richer respondent features, and human calibration.

Result: Digital twins can reproduce average human effects but provide little information about which individuals deviate from those averages; newer models and richer respondent data improve some metrics but do not reliably reduce the need for human-labeled data; human calibration can lower aggregate prediction error but small labeled samples often fail to yield stable precision gains; behavioral fidelity does not imply statistical substitutability.

Conclusion: 对确认性使用，数字孪生应以能否减少关于真实人群量的测不确定性为评价标准，而不仅仅看其能否重现人类均值或分布；应围绕估计目标设计替代性测试，并谨慎采用AI替代人工测量，特别是在个体层面和有限标注样本下。

Abstract: LLM-based digital twins promise to reduce repeated human data collection by generating person- specific responses, yet existing evaluations provide little evidence about whether they can reduce human measurement while preserving valid inference. To address this, we introduce statistical substitutability, an inferential criterion that evaluates the extent to which twin predictions can reduce human measurement for a particular estimand while preserving valid inference. We develop a framework, grounded in mixed-subject and prediction-powered inference, that evaluates statistical substitutability along four dimensions: aggregate fidelity, paired respondent-level signal, finite-sample human-label recovery, and stability across populations. Across two empirical evaluations spanning behavioral experiments, multiple models, and alternative respondent representations, we find that digital twins can reproduce average human effects while providing little information about which individuals differ from those averages. Newer models and richer respondent information improve some dimensions of performance but do not reliably translate into human-data savings. Human calibration can reduce aggregate prediction error, yet limited labeled samples often fail to produce stable precision gains. Importantly, these findings demonstrate that behavioral fidelity is neither necessary nor sufficient for statistical substitutability. More broadly, they suggest that AI-generated evidence should be evaluated based on its ability to support valid scientific inference rather than its ability to reproduce human outcomes alone. Digital twins should therefore be judged for confirmatory use by whether they reduce uncertainty about human quantities, not merely by whether they reproduce human means, distributions, or effects.

</details>


### [183] [Mini-Batch Risk-Averse Deep Q-Learning: A Robot Navigation Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07998)
*Aayush Patel,Andrzej Ruszczyński*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在马尔可夫风险测度下做风险厌恶的强化学习，核心是用mini-batch的经验下一状态分布来构造对转移核非线性依赖的风险映射，从而得到可用的一步无偏估计并嵌入到Double DQN。实验（300个环境）表明该方法能迁移到未见过的实例规模、用很小的批量（N=2）即降低结果分布的上半偏差并在模拟器失配时同时提升均值，体现了相干风险测度与分布鲁棒性的对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习以期望折扣成本评估策略，而在风险敏感场景（如被毁毁灭性风险）需要时间一致的马尔可夫风险测度。问题在于转移风险映射对转移核的非线性依赖使得无法用单次观测估计，阻碍了与样本驱动方法的结合。

Method: 1) 定义将转移风险映射作用于N个独立下一个状态样本的经验分布并对结果取平均，证明所得映射保持相干性且作为N个样本的函数可构造一步无偏估计；2) 将此估计嵌入Double DQN，分析由有限样本近似与函数近似/引导更新（bootstrapping）产生的两类估计偏差；3) 在水下机器人任务中采用分层策略（低层路径搜索，高层“采集或传送”决策）和不变性的低维特征映射以降低学习复杂度；4) 在300个测试环境上评估并分析不同N的效果。

Result: 提出的mini-batch转移风险映射在理论上保持相干并允许一步无偏估计；基于此的风险厌恶DDQN在仿真任务上实现了对未见规模的迁移能力；实验显示即使N=2也能显著降低上半偏差并在模拟器失配时提升均值，验证了相干风险测度与分布鲁棒性的关联。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并验证了一个可扩展的、适用于连续/高维状态空间的风险厌恶Q学习框架：通过mini-batch转移风险映射消除单步观察不可估的非线性依赖、在DDQN中可实现无偏估计并在实证中降低尾部风险与提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study the control of Markov decision processes in which the quality of a policy is evaluated by a dynamic, time-consistent Markov risk measure rather than by an expected discounted cost. The main obstacle to combining such measures with reinforcement learning is that a transition risk mapping depends on the transition kernel in a nonlinear way, and therefore cannot be estimated from a single observed transition. We remove this obstacle by employing mini-batch transition risk mappings: the mapping is applied to the empirical measure of $N$ independent next-state samples, and the result is averaged. The resulting mapping is again coherent. However, as an expected value of a function of $N$ next-state values, it admits an unbiased one-sample estimator.
  We embed this mapping into a double deep Q-network, analyze the two sources of estimation bias that arise, and obtain a risk-averse Q-learning method applicable to state spaces far beyond the reach of tabular schemes. The method is applied to an underwater robot navigation problem, in which a vehicle must visit collection points, gather stochastic information payloads, and deliver them at transmission points, while exposed at each step to the risk of destruction. A hierarchical decomposition delegates path execution to an exact graph search and confines learning to the high-level ``collect or transmit'' decision. A low-dimensional feature map, invariant under the symmetries of the problem, replaces the raw state--configuration encoding. In experiments on $300$ held-out environments, the resulting policies transfer to instance sizes never seen in training, and already $N=2$ reduces the upper semideviation of the outcome distribution while simultaneously improving its mean whenever the simulator is misspecified---an empirical counterpart of the duality between coherent risk measures and distributional robustness.

</details>


### [184] [Sparks of In Silico Cognitive Science: Theories from Simulated Data Can Generalize to Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08003)
*Akshay K. Jagadish,Younes Strittmatter,Nori Jacoby,Eric Schulz,Nathaniel Daw,Thomas L. Griffiths,Suyog H. Chandramouli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用模拟器做闭环实验设计并进行理论仲裁，可以发现泛化到人类的数据驱动理论；模拟器只需保留区分性规律，而非行为精确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验基于行为的基础模型（模拟器）上发现的理论是否能泛化到真实人类，及研究何种发现范式能容忍模拟器的不完美。

Method: 使用Automated Cognitive Scientist（AutoCog）闭环系统：多代理LLM设计区分性实验、在Centaur（人类行为基础模型）上运行并收集响应、在候选理论间仲裁、合成并迭代新理论；最终在真人数据上进行外部验证。

Result: 在多属性决策任务上，AutoCog在Centaur上发现的理论在十个保留实验上优于传统理论，并仅被直接在真人上运行同一回路发现的理论所匹敌；表明模拟器不必完美也能助力理论发现。

Conclusion: 在可接受的不完美模拟器上运行的闭环理论发现系统，可以提出对真人表现有预测力的新理论；关键在于“仲裁式”发现只要求模拟器再现能区分候选理论的正则性，而非精确复刻行为。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models have been proposed as stand-ins for human participants across settings, but it is unclear whether theories discovered on them generalize to humans or merely characterize the simulator. We ran the Automated Cognitive Scientist (\textsc{AutoCog}), a closed-loop discovery system in which LLM agents design theory-discriminating experiments, collect responses, arbitrate between competing theories, and synthesize successors, entirely on behavior simulated by Centaur, a foundation model of human behavior. In a multi-attribute decision-making setting, the theories \textsc{AutoCog} found on Centaur generalized to human data: they outperformed canonical theories on ten held-out experiments and were rivaled only by theories found by running the same loop on people. We argue that this succeeds despite the simulator's inevitable imperfections because a discovery loop that arbitrates between competing theories demands less of its simulator than estimation does. The simulator only needs to capture the regularities that distinguish the theories, and not necessarily reproduce behavior precisely. Imperfect simulators can therefore widen the search over theories, with human data then testing whether the surfaced theories generalize.

</details>


### [185] [From Version Conflicts to Decision Conflicts: Selective Revalidation for Long-Running AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08015)
*Yongjian Lyu,Yang Ren,Ruofei Lai,Wenting Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长时运行代理的“版本冲突”和“决策冲突”差异，ATR 通过记录可执行条件并仅重检受影响条件，实现高效且确定性的决策有效性检查；在受控基准上显著降低重检代价并无错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行 AI 代理可能在读取状态、推理并等待工具或人工批准后很久才执行外部动作；此期间底层状态可能变化，传统的版本冲突检测无法判定状态变化是否破坏了动作成立的语义理由（即决策冲突）。

Method: 在代理发起待决外部操作时，记录显式且可执行的“决策条件”；当检测到存储版本变化时，只对受影响的条件重新检查（而非全文扫描），并根据重检结果选择保留操作、刷新非决定性元数据、要求重新规划或阻止执行；最终通过目标端事务或 compare-and-set 将经检查的状态与提交绑定。

Result: 在 210,000 次受控执行、15 类变异情况中，ATR 与开发者定义的预期结果完全匹配（无误放行或误阻止）；在 10 个持久化 SQLite 检查点/恢复单元中，ATR 每次变更平均重检 0.6 个条件（FullScan 为 6.0）；在 4,093 次记录读操作中，ATR 平均耗时 9.3 微秒（FullScan 为 2595.9 微秒）。论文声明这些结果证明了受控可行性，但不代表在生产环境的普适性或条件自动提取。

Conclusion: ATR 提供了一种确定性可控的方法，通过记录并仅重检影响待决外部操作的可执行条件，解决长时运行智能代理在外部操作前状态发生变化导致的决策冲突问题，实验中在受控场景下无误放行或误阻止，验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: Long-running AI agents may read state, reason, wait for tools or human approval, and perform an external action much later. The state that justified the action can change in the meantime. For example, after an agent proposes an 80 GBP refund under a limit of 100, a customer-name change affects only presentation metadata, a new limit of 90 still permits the refund, a limit of 50 invalidates it, and a refund issued by another worker must prevent a duplicate. Standard optimistic concurrency control and version checks can detect that previously read state has changed, but by themselves do not determine whether that change invalidates the pending action's justification. We call any detected version change a version conflict; when that change invalidates the action's justification, it is also a decision conflict. ATR records the explicit, executable conditions that justify a pending action and rechecks only the conditions affected by a change before releasing the external operation. It can retain the action, refresh non-decisive metadata, require replanning, or block execution; a target-side transaction or compare-and-set binds checked state to commit. Across 210,000 controlled executions over 15 mutation cases, ATR matched every developer-specified outcome with no false allows or blocks. In ten durable SQLite checkpoint/resume cells, it evaluated 0.6 conditions per change versus 6.0 for FullScan. At 4,093 recorded reads, ATR took 9.3 microseconds versus 2595.9 microseconds for FullScan. These deterministic results establish controlled feasibility, not production generality or automatic extraction of the required conditions.

</details>


### [186] [A Layered Analysis of Disagreement And Answer Quality in Multi-Agent LLM Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08016)
*Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多代理辩论会改变 LLM 的表述与自报，但对其“持久采纳”与最终答案质量并无可靠提升；语气和指令是主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验常被假定但很少实证检验的假设：多代理辩论能够通过显现真正分歧来提升答案质量。

Method: 用三模型委员会在 GlobalOpinionQA（开放式问题）上进行 750 次三方辩论，设定友好/中性/敌对三种语气。度量包括自报一致性、人工读取回复文本的反驳判定、在删除/保留诱导指令的再询问中观察立场持久性、以及对开源模型从 token log-probs 直接读取立场证据。统计分析包含回合内/汇总的显著性检验与效果大小比较。

Result: 引入四项度量（A：自报一致性；B：回复文本是否实际反驳；C：删除诱导指令后立场是否保持；D：对开源模型从 token log-probs 得到的立场证据），在 GlobalOpinionQA 上用三模型委员会进行 750 次辩论，设置友好/中性/敌对三种语气。主要发现：语气强烈影响自报一致性（友好 vs 敌对差 50.4 个百分点）；只读回复文本的审判者复现该模式；删除敌对指令后标签向一致性回归多 23.1 个点；第一轮基于问题加权的显著性在单轮上边界显著（p=0.0625），汇总所有轮次显著（p=0.016）；仅 11/28 的首轮反转在回复文本可见；对立论点更常减弱立场边际而非改变方向；最终答案未见质量提升（经偏见校验的评审 299/299 平局，验证性控制任务准确率无变化），未经偏见校验的评审显示辩论胜出率 66%，系阅读顺序偏差。总体结论：辩论明显改变输出的话语，但对持续认同的改变和最终答案质量的证据较弱。

Conclusion: LLM 辩论常改变表态但很少改变持久立场或提升答案质量；许多表面上的“异议”依赖于诱导指令或呈现顺序，而非模型内部信念的改变。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate, in which several LLMs exchange arguments before answering, is widely assumed to improve answer quality by surfacing genuine disagreement. That mechanism is rarely checked. We introduce four measurements: (A) the agreement a debater reports; (B) whether its reply text actually pushes back; (C) whether the position persists once the eliciting instruction is removed; and (D) for open-weight models, the stance response in the debater's own token log-probabilities. We evaluate three-model committees debating open-ended GlobalOpinionQA across 750 debates under three tones: friendly (seek common ground), neutral, and hostile (stress-test every position). (A) Tone strongly reshapes reported agreement: full agreement differs by 50.4 percentage points between the friendly and hostile endpoints. (B) A judge that reads only the reply text, never the self-report or the condition, recovers the same pattern. (C) The dissent appears partly tied to the instruction that elicited it: labels revert toward agreement 23.1 points more often after deleting the hostile instruction than under a matched re-ask that keeps it; question-weighted inference is inconclusive on first-round turns alone (p=0.0625), significant pooling all rounds (p=0.016), and only 11/28 first-round reversions also appear in the reply text. (D) Opposing arguments weaken a debater's stance margin more consistently than they shift its direction. For final answers we detect no quality gain: a bias-checked jury returns 299/299 ties (ruling out only large differences), accuracy on a verifiable control task is unchanged, and a jury without the bias check had declared debate the winner 66% of the time -- an artifact of reading order. Taken together, LLM debate readily changes what agents say, but we find much weaker evidence that it changes what they persistently endorse or improves the quality of the final answer.

</details>


### [187] [Eliciting Self-Verification in Multimodal Reasoning Agents with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08025)
*Vishwas Sathish,Viresh Ranjan,Xinliang Zhu,Arnab Dhua,Douglas Gray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一种仅用强化学习的微调框架，让多模态代理在自身推理轨迹中验证并过滤检索到的证据，同时通过惩罚不必要的工具调用和奖励多样化检索查询来提升多跳VQA的泛化与工具效率，且只用少量训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态代理在使用外部工具时需同时理解图文并整合嘈杂检索证据，但常缺乏逐步验证的监督信号；需要一种能在代理内部学习验证与高效查询的训练方法。

Method: 基于RL微调：从模型生成的推理轨迹中提取自验证（self-verification）信号作为奖励，加入搜索感知的工具调用惩罚项以及鼓励检索查询多样性的奖励，对Qwen-2.5-VL-7B进行5,000条VQA样本的微调。

Result: SVRL improves multimodal agent tool use by self-verification via RL, adding a search-aware penalty and a query-diversity reward; finetuning Qwen-2.5-VL-7B on 5,000 VQA examples yields consistent multi-hop VQA gains and tool-efficiency improvements, narrowing the gap to larger models.

Conclusion: SVRL使得紧凑的多模态模型在工具使用上更可靠、效率更高，减少对外部验证器的依赖，在成本与性能之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Reasoning agents increasingly rely on external tools such as web search to answer complex queries. Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning algorithms such as GRPO have improved long-form reasoning in text-only language models, particularly for coding and mathematics. Reliable tool use in multimodal agents, however, remains challenging because models must interpret text and images while integrating noisy retrieved evidence, often under sparse outcome-level supervision without explicit verification signals. We present Self-Verification via Reinforcement Learning (SVRL), an RL-only finetuning framework that trains multimodal agents to verify and filter retrieved evidence within their own reasoning traces, reducing reliance on external verifiers at inference time. SVRL also introduces a search-aware penalty that discourages unnecessary tool calls and a query-diversity reward that encourages diverse, well-formed search queries, providing fine-grained feedback on when and what to search. Finetuning Qwen-2.5-VL-7B with SVRL on only 5{,}000 visual question answering examples yields consistent gains in multi-hop VQA generalization and tool efficiency across benchmarks. Overall, SVRL narrows the gap between compact agents and much larger proprietary models while requiring substantially lower training and inference cost.

</details>


### [188] [ResidualAuth: What Authorization State Must Language Agents Preserve under Revocable Delegation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08062)
*Moonwon Choi,Seokho Jeong,Seunggeun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 相同的当前权限与传递闭包并不足以在撤销后安全决策，需保存残余授权状态；该状态在最坏情况可呈指数级增长，且实证结果表明只有经过认证或精确串行化的在线状态维护能有效恢复正确决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型语言代理通过外部服务执行操作并需要支持委托与撤销，作者旨在研究在撤销发生时要做出正确授权决策到底需要保存哪类历史信息，进而量化所需在线状态与可用决策信息之间的差距。

Method: 作者形式化了“残余授权状态”的信息需求，证明存在在相同传递闭包下未来行为可区分的指数多状态；推导了关于精确监控器所需状态大小的上下界；将这些构造编译为成对的语言代理执行稿（episodes）并在若干开源权重模型上进行定量实验，比较不同摘要/读取/串行化策略对区分任务的成功率。

Result: 理论上给出残余授权状态的正规化定义和指数下界/匹配上界（在不同冗余条件下的精确或紧致渐近界）；实验上显示简单的256令牌摘要或‘假读（sham reads）’无法区分大多数对偶情景，而‘经过认证的当前查询读取’与精确账本串行化能显著改善正确决策率；引入‘硬门’策略能消除观察到的未授权效应。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：仅凭当前权限集合与传递闭包信息不能保证在撤销边后做出正确的授权决策，需要额外的“残余授权状态”；在最坏情形下，保存这些区分信息可能呈指数级增长，但作者给出关于所需状态规模随委托冗余变化的精确或渐近界，并通过构造将理论事实映射为语言代理的对偶情景进行实验证明。

Abstract: Tool-using language agents can delegate and revoke permissions while acting through external services. We show that two authorization histories can have identical current permissions and identical all-pairs reachability yet require opposite decisions after the same direct-edge revocation. We formalize the information needed to preserve such distinctions as a residual authorization state. We prove that exponentially many future-distinct states can share one fixed transitive closure, and give exact or tight asymptotic bounds on the state required by an exact monitor as delegation redundancy varies. ResidualAuth compiles these constructions into paired language-agent episodes. Across four open-weight models, a fixed 256-token summary solved 0-2/16 pairs, sham reads solved 0/16, and authenticated current-query reads solved 15-16/16. In a separate held-out online-memory diagnostic, exact ledger serializations fit all 128 four-coordinate pairs at both 768 and 1,024 tokens. At either cap, factually supported model-written memories sufficient for every prespecified continuation solved at most 1/128 pairs per model. A hard gate reduced eight observed unauthorized effects to zero without changing the preceding attempts. These results distinguish required authorization state, usable decision information, online state maintenance, and effect mediation.

</details>


### [189] [Automated Design of Inventory Policy with Large Language Models: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08071)
*Fenghua Yang,Preet Baxi,Yi Zhang,Stefanus Jasin,Yanzhe Lei,Mo Liu,Parshan Pakiman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Iteratively combining LLM proposals of parameterized policy classes with parameter optimization finds novel, interpretable, and high-performing inventory policies for lost-sales problems, with strong empirical gains over optimized base-stock benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To automate inventory policy design by integrating operational data, optimization tools, and LLMs so as to explore a broader space of policy functional forms than standard prespecified classes and find better-performing, interpretable decision rules.

Method: Given demand data, use an LLM to propose parameterized policy classes; for each proposed class, use an external solver to optimize its parameters and evaluate performance; feed performance metrics back to the LLM and iterate generation for multiple rounds to search policy-class space.

Result: Across 30 benchmark lost-sales instances, mean cost reduction versus optimized base-stock rises from 17.5% after one generation to 30.0% after ten generations. LLM-only variants perform worse; optimization-guided feedback is essential. Three discovered policy classes, after re-optimization, give average cost reductions of 21.75%–22.60% over 10,064 new instances. Discovered classes include capped orders, discounted/weighted pipeline inventory, and threshold-based replenishment logical forms not previously studied.

Conclusion: An iterative framework combining LLM-generated parameterized policy classes with an external optimizer can discover high-performing, interpretable inventory policies for lost-sales problems; optimization-guided feedback improves search and yields novel functional forms that generalize after re-optimization.

Abstract: Firms making inventory decisions have access to operational data, optimization tools, and large language models (LLMs). Typically, data characterize the operating environment, optimization selects parameters within a prespecified inventory policy class, and LLMs support coding and decision analysis. We develop an integrated framework that combines these resources to automate inventory policy design. Given demand data, the framework iteratively uses an LLM to generate parameterized policy classes and an external solver to optimize its parameters within each class. Across 30 lost-sales inventory instances, the mean cost reduction relative to optimized base-stock benchmarks increases from 17.5% after one generation to 30.0% after ten generations. Parameter optimization is central to this performance: an LLM-only variant performs substantially worse, whereas optimization-guided feedback improves policy quality, accelerates search, and directs the LLM toward better policy classes rather than merely better parameter values within a fixed class. The strongest discovered policies are also interpretable: they combine recognizable inventory-control motifs, including capped orders, discounted or weighted pipeline inventory, and threshold-based replenishment logic. The search thereby produces new policy-class functional forms that, to our knowledge, have not previously been studied in the lost-sales inventory literature. These functional forms are not specified ex ante but emerge from the search process. Moreover, after their parameters are re-optimized, three discovered policy classes achieve average cost reductions of 21.75% to 22.60% across 10,064 new inventory instances. Overall, the results show that data-driven parameter optimization can guide LLM-based search over a broad space of inventory policy classes and identify high-performing, interpretable, and transferable decision rules.

</details>


### [190] [Inference-Time Nash Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08082)
*Hadi Hosseini,Debmalya Mandal,Duohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将推理时对齐推广到通用偏好，采用零和博弈建模并提出 BoN 与 NMD，两者理论上最优且在实证上接近微调性能，NMD 对正则化稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的微调方法（如 RLHF、DPO）计算代价高且要求访问模型参数；推理时对齐是一个更廉价的替代，但现有方法依赖基于 Bradley-Terry 假设的标量奖励模型，无法表示通用偏好，因此需要研究能处理广义偏好的推理时对齐方法。

Method: 将推理时对齐问题形式化为两者之间的零和博弈，设计 Best-of-Nash（BoN）与 Nash Mirror Descent（NMD）两种算法，给出对偶间隙界的上界证明并证明与下界匹配；在三个数据集上实现并比较，评估与微调模型和基础策略的性能差距与稳健性。

Result: 两种方法在三套数据集上均显著优于基础策略并收敛到微调模型的性能；理论上 BoN 与 NMD 达到最优对偶间隙；NMD 在不同正则化参数下表现稳健。

Conclusion: 本文证明在通用偏好下可通过将问题建模为两个策略之间的零和博弈，在推理时完成对齐，提出的两种算法（Best-of-Nash 与 Nash Mirror Descent）在理论上达到与问题下界一致的对偶间隙，并在实证上显著优于基线，接近微调模型的性能；其中 NMD 对正则化参数表现稳健。

Abstract: Preference-based fine-tuning methods such as RLHF and DPO require substantial compute and large preference datasets. They also need direct access to the model parameters which are not provided by many state-of-the art models. Inference-time alignment offers a cost-effective alternative without updating model parameters. However, existing inference-time methods rely on a scalar reward model derived under a Bradley-Terry assumption, which cannot represent general preferences. Following recent work on fine-tuning with generalized preferences, in this work, we initiate the study of inference-time alignment under general preferences. We formulate the problem as obtaining a Nash equilibrium of a two-player zero-sum game between policies. We propose two algorithms: Best-of-Nash (BoN) and Nash Mirror Descent (NMD). We prove that both algorithms achieve a duality gap that matches the problem lower bound. Empirically, we implement the two methods on three datasets, which shows that our methods substantially outperform the base policy, converging to the performance of the fine-tuned models. Moreover, our results show that NMD remains robust across the regularization parameter.

</details>


### [191] [RevalExo: A Functional Daily-Activity Benchmark for Inertial and Visual Locomotion Mode Recognition in Older Adults and Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08090)
*Diwas Lamsal,Juha Carlon,Reinhard Claeys,Maxim Yudayev,Louis Flynn,Tom Verstraten,David Beckwée,Eva Swinnen,Mihai Bâce,Bart Vanrumste,Benjamin Filtjens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RevalExo 是一个针对老年与临床人群、包含帧级转换标注的惯性+视角视频步态模式识别数据集（27 人，11 模式，10.1 小时），展示了视觉-惯性融合的优势，同时暴露了转换检测与跨域迁移的未解难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准多来自健康成人、缺乏精确的转换期标注或任务覆盖有限，难以反映临床/老年人群的真实日常移动需求与假肢/外骨骼控制的实际挑战。

Method: 通过一个临床与生态有效的标准化日常活动协议采集数据：27 名参与者（健康老年人、卒中幸存者、疑似肌少症老年人），全队列下肢 IMU 采集；13 人子集同步采集头戴/视角视频。构建 11 类步态模式的帧级标注（共 10.1 小时，其中 5.1 小时为配对 IMU+视觉），并用一组基线模型评测单模态/多模态识别、跨人群泛化与视觉对 IMU 的知识迁移。

Result: 多模态（惯性+视觉）融合持续带来识别性能提升；整体识别达约 93% F1，但转换期间识别显著下降至约 68% F1；跨人群泛化与跨模态（视觉到 IMU）迁移仍存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: RevalExo 提供了一个面向老年与临床人群日常活动的、带时序精确标注的惯性与第一视角视觉步态/模式识别基准，验证了多模态融合在整体识别上的收益，但也揭示了对“转换期”识别、跨人群泛化与跨模态迁移的显著挑战。

Abstract: Assistive devices for people with mobility impairments, such as powered exoskeletons, rely on accurate locomotion mode recognition to adapt control strategies and provide appropriate assistance during daily activities. However, public benchmarks are typically collected from healthy adults, lack temporally precise labels necessary for detecting mode transitions, or focus on a limited set of tasks.
