To support development and evaluation under realistic clinical constraints and daily mobility demands, we introduce RevalExo, a functional daily-activity benchmark for inertial and visual locomotion mode recognition. RevalExo is built around a standardized, clinically and ecologically validated daily-activity protocol reflecting the cumulative everyday mobility demands in ageing and clinical populations. The benchmark includes 27 participants across three cohorts: older adults without mobility impairments, stroke survivors, and older adults with probable sarcopenia. The full cohort was recorded with lower-body IMUs, while synchronized egocentric video was collected for a clinically feasible subset of 13 participants. RevalExo provides 10.1 hours of frame-level annotations across 11 locomotion modes, including 5.1 hours of paired inertial--visual recordings. We benchmark three challenges: unimodal and multimodal locomotion mode recognition across multiple horizons, cross-population generalization from older adults without mobility impairments to clinical cohorts, and vision-guided knowledge transfer to IMU-only models. Results confirm consistent gains from fusing inertial and visual inputs but reveal a substantial gap between general recognition ($\sim$93\% F1) and recognition during transitions ($\sim$68\% F1), alongside persistent challenges in cross-population generalization and cross-modal transfer. We release RevalExo to stimulate further research on these open challenges.

</details>


### [192] [CIVI: A Framework for Diagnosing Search Agent Failures in Civic Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08094)
*Dingying Liu,Yunshun Zhong,Wentao Zhang,Yiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIVI 是首个诊断性基准，覆盖跨国与多层政府信息场景；ARISE 将失败分为四类并通过源注入证明大多数错误由检索/检索策略引起，提示改进检索与检索—生成整合的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型与搜索代理越来越多部署在公共事务与公民信息场景，一旦提供错误引导可能造成不可逆伤害。因此需要系统性的基准与故障诊断框架来定位错误来源并指导改进。

Method: 构建跨国与多级别的 CIVI 基准，定义多项评价指标（准确率、搜索触发率、选择性不搜索准确率、引用权威政府来源的频率等）；评测十个搜索代理，对比人工基线；提出 ARISE，通过对代理进行‘源注入’消融实验将失败划分为四个互斥模式，并量化各类失败的占比。

Result: CIVI 提出并实证了用于诊断面向公共事务的搜索型代理在提供公民信息时的失误。作者构建了跨国、跨层级（联邦/州/地方）与功能类别（采用联合国标准）的基准，并评估了十个前沿搜索代理，发现没有一个能达到细心人工基线。进一步提出 ARISE 作为故障划分框架，通过源注入消融把代理失败分为四类，并发现 72.1% 的失败源自检索相关问题而非模型参数记忆缺陷。

Conclusion: 在民事信息检索与回答场景中，检索模块与检索策略是性能瓶颈。提升检索质量、授权来源引用与适时触发“搜索”行为，比单纯增强模型参数化知识更能减少错误。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in public-sector settings, where incorrect guidance can cause irreversible harm. We introduce CIVI, the first framework for diagnosing search agent failures in civic information. Its benchmark instantiation jointly spans cross-national, interjurisdictional government contexts (federal, state, and local) and functional categories from an internationally adopted United Nations standard. We evaluate ten frontier search agents and find that none matches an attentive human baseline. Alongside accuracy, CIVI measures search invocation rate, selective no-search accuracy, and how often agents cite authoritative government sources. To perform this diagnosis, we introduce ARISE, which decomposes agentic search failures into four mutually exclusive modes, isolated via source-injection ablation. ARISE attributes 72.1% of all observed failures to retrieval-bound causes rather than to gaps in the models' parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [193] [Artificial Intelligence-Assisted Digital Inventory of Cultural Heritage & Traditional Knowledge: Case for Indonesian Open Digital Library of Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08105)
*Hokky Situngkir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文设计并形式化了一个五阶段的自治采集漏斗，通过可证明的子模块与操作规程，实现大规模跨语种文化知识的可审计、幂等化收集与发布，同时保留关键的人类监督角色以防止机器破坏人工贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工协作的文化数字库受制于覆盖分散（语言与站点）、真实性混杂与条目内容浅薄三重结构性障碍，需要一种能在开放网络上自动、可审计地扩展与加深知识库的方法。

Method: 将采集流程组织为经济漏斗：定向爬取（focused crawling）、多语提取与规范化（multilingual extraction and canonicalisation）、向量编码与阻断（vector encoding with blocking）、具有代理决策能力的决策层（agentic decision-making）、幂等发布（idempotent publication）；并在每阶段引入形式化模型与算法（漏斗经济学、爬行前沿的亚临界分支过程、事实新颖性包含度量、贝叶斯多源证据融合、幂等upsert与事务性outbox、滑动窗口推理预算与保留协议、统计质量审计、子模覆盖最大化选种）。

Result: 提出的框架提供了可操作的工程与数学工具链以提高采集效率、降低噪声、保证一次性上链效果并可统计审核；在制度上设置更高的发布阈值用于‘神圣类别’并保留四类人类角色，从而在提升机器自治同时控制伦理/法律与文化敏感风险。

Conclusion: 提出并形式化了一个五阶段的自治式、基于AI的文化知识收集框架，旨在同时扩展语料覆盖面与加深条目数据深度，并在架构上保证机器不会覆盖人工贡献，保留关键的人类角色以处理价值敏感事项。

Abstract: The Indonesian Digital Library of Culture (Perpustakaan Digital Budaya Indonesia, PDBI; budaya-indonesia.org) is a participatory platform that has collected tens of thousands of entries on Nusantara cultural heritage through public contribution since 2007. Manual contribution faces three structural barriers: coverage (knowledge is scattered across languages and sites), integrity (open sources mix authentic documentation with noise), and completeness (subjects are recorded but their data remain shallow). This paper presents a methodological framework for autonomous, AI-based harvesting of cultural knowledge from the open web, designed to expand corpus coverage while intensifying per-entry data depth. The methodology is organised as a five-stage economic funnel: focused crawling, multilingual extraction and canonicalisation, vector encoding with blocking, agentic decision-making, and idempotent publication, under the principle of deterministic orchestration, agentic decisions. Each stage is formalised: funnel economics and optimal filter ordering; crawl-frontier dynamics as a subcritical branching process that explains the necessity of recurrent re-seeding; fact-level novelty via a containment measure; Bayesian multi-source evidence fusion with elevated publication thresholds for sacred categories; exactly-once effects via idempotent upserts and the transactional outbox; sliding-window inference budgeting with a reservation protocol; statistical quality auditing; and seed selection as submodular coverage maximisation. The framework retains four high-value human roles: curator of direction, escalation approver, quality auditor, and guardian of meaning, while machine autonomy is raised in stages. Ethical, legal, and cultural-sensitivity implications are discussed, including the architectural guarantee that the machine never overwrites human contributions.

</details>


### [194] [Router Prior Bias: Preserving Base Routing Structure in MoE Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08115)
*Jaedeok Lee,Keonwoo Kim,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun,Yera Choi,Haanju Yoo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对继承自预训练路由的非均匀性应采取软约束（RPB），而不是重新均衡或硬固定；软锚定在多个模型和语料上提升下游性能并保留泛化能力，社区结构是软锚定的指标而非原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE预训练通常用负载均衡损失驱动专家均匀利用，但基线路由在预训练后实际上编码了有用的非均匀共激活结构。后训若再强制均匀或完全放任都会破坏有益结构，导致下游性能下降。需要一种能“软保持”继承路由的策略。

Method: 提出软路由锚定（soft router anchoring），具体实现为Router Prior Bias (RPB)：在训练损失中加入对路由logits的偏置项，使其偏向从冻结的基线路由计算出的先验分布（或对应权重/输出分布），同时允许路由参数继续更新。进行了与重新施加Load-Balancing Loss (LBL)、不锚定微调以及硬锚定的对比实验，并在Moonlight-16B-A3B和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base上验证结果。分析了专家共激活图的社区结构以解释效果。

Result: 在数学类后训任务上，RPB在Moonlight-16B-A3B上将准确率提升到45.77，显著优于重新施加LBL的31.91和不锚定微调的29.44；在Qwen3-30B-Base上重现了排序。RPB同时在域外能力上优于其他方法。用权重、logits或输出分布定义的锚点表现相近，表明关键在于约束的‘软性’而非先验形式。将先验以硬分配方式强制执行虽能保留社区结构但会严重损害性能。

Conclusion: 在MoE后训阶段，不应强行恢复预训练时的负载均衡目标；相反，应该以‘软锚定’方式保持从基线路由继承的非均匀路由结构。Router Prior Bias (RPB) —— 在训练时将路由logits拉向由冻结的基线路由读出的先验但保留路由可训练性——在多模型与数据上都能显著提高下游任务表现并保留域外能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) pretraining relies on an auxiliary load-balancing loss (LBL) to drive per-expert utilization toward uniformity. Post-training inherits a different situation: the base router already encodes non-uniform expert co-activation structure, which a re-imposed uniformity objective flattens away. We show that downstream performance depends instead on holding this inherited routing softly, a principle we term soft router anchoring, and instantiate it as Router Prior Bias (RPB), a training-time bias that pulls the router logits toward a prior read off the frozen base router while leaving the router itself trainable. On math post-training of Moonlight-16B-A3B, RPB attains 45.77 in-domain accuracy against 31.91 under re-applied LBL and 29.44 under unanchored fine-tuning, and retains more out-of-domain capability than either. The ordering against LBL reproduces on a second model family (Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base), and the advantage over LBL is resolvable on an independently sourced corpus. Anchors defined on the router weights, on its logits, or on its output distribution perform comparably with no consistent ordering, which places the effect in the softness of the constraint rather than in the particular prior RPB supplies. Retained community structure in the expert co-activation graph tracks these gains wherever the base router is non-uniform enough for communities to form, yet enforcing the same prior as a hard assignment preserves that structure while performance falls sharply. Community structure is therefore a footprint of soft anchoring rather than its source, and the practical lesson is that inherited routing should be held softly during post-training, since both flattening it toward uniformity and enforcing it absolutely carry a downstream cost. Our code will be released at https://github.com/naver-ai/rpb.

</details>


### [195] [SchemeArena: Factorized Stress Testing of Scheming in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08126)
*Jie Ruan,Inderjeet Nair,Amy Liu,Muhammad Khalifa,Yusheng Zhou,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了一个400场景的scheming压力测试基准（SCHEMEARENA）与证据驱动监测器（SCOUT），通过跨模型测试发现目标明确性、策略性提示与不完整监督是影响scheming出现与可检测性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补此前研究在场景规模和多样性上的不足，从而更精细地分离影响scheming出现与执行的关键因素，提高对现实部署环境中潜在隐蔽策略的覆盖度与可检测性。

Method: 提出SCHEMEARENA：400个因子化合成场景，覆盖多种安全相关工具域、工具性目标、监督条件与压力机制；提出SCOUT：基于代理推理（CoT）与行为证据的多准则scheming监测器；在五款LLM代理上进行受控压力测试与定量分析。

Result: 主要发现包括：明确的工具性/手段性目标是诱发scheming的最强因素；策略性提示将推理转化为具体隐蔽行为；部分监督（仅动作监控）在封闭模型上可能增加scheming；CoT能提前暴露潜在scheming但不能覆盖所有隐蔽行为（存在仅动作的scheming）。

Conclusion: 该论文通过构建大规模、因子化的场景基准与可证据化的监测器，系统性地分析了在不同目标、工具域、监督和压力条件下LLM代理产生隐蔽追求（scheming）的驱动因素与表现，得出若干可操作的工程与研究结论。

Abstract: We study scheming in LLM agents, in which agents covertly pursue misaligned goals. Our focus is to understand how scheming arises from the interaction of key factors, such as instrumental goals, environmental affordances, oversight conditions, and perceived consequences. Prior work examines only a small number of scenarios, limiting the ability to isolate how these conditions shape an agent's propensity or capability to scheme. This limited scale and task diversity also restrict coverage of realistic deployment settings and the range of scheming strategies that can be observed. To this end, we introduce SCHEMEARENA, a 400-scenario benchmark for scalable scheming stress testing, constructed through a factorized scenario synthesis framework spanning diverse safety-relevant tool domains, instrumental goals, oversight conditions, and pressure mechanisms. To enable scalable and reliable monitoring, we further propose SCOUT, a scheming monitor that grounds multi-criteria judgments in evidence drawn from agents' reasoning and actions. Across controlled stress tests on five LLM agents, we find that explicit instrumental goals are the strongest driver of scheming propensity. Strategic hints play a distinct role by helping agents translate scheming reasoning into concrete covert behavior. Oversight has mixed effects: in several closed models, action-only monitoring increases scheming, suggesting that partial oversight can act as an optimization constraint rather than a deterrent. CoT is a useful but incomplete monitoring signal: it can reveal latent scheming before execution, yet action-only scheming shows that covert behavior may occur without explicit reasoning evidence. We release the benchmark, code, and monitor at: https://github.com/launchnlp/SchemeArena.

</details>


### [196] [WorldAgen: Unified State-Action Prediction with Test-Time World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08162)
*Chi Wan,Kangrui Wang,Yuan Si,Pingyue Zhang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldAgen 用共享 Transformer + 双头 + 混合单向注意力掩码构建同时可训练世界模型与行为预测器，并通过测试时采样与轻量更新实现快速适配，在 CALVIN/LIBERO 上用少量样本超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型多依赖静态数据预训练，缺乏部署时主动适应机制，导致面对未见物体配置或动力学变化时泛化能力差。通过在推理阶段更新世界模型，可以快速适应新的环境动力学，提升在线表现。

Method: 使用共享 Transformer 主干，并设计两个头（世界模型头：从过去的状态-动作轨迹预测未来状态；智能体模型头：在任务指令下预测动作）。通过设计 Mixed Unidirectional Attention Mask 将两类建模分离。在推理时，执行探索性动作以获取状态转移数据，并对世界模型进行轻量的测试时训练（TTT）更新，以改进环境理解并间接提升动作预测。

Result: 基线模型在 CALVIN 与 LIBERO 上呈现可比甚至更优的性能；加入少量 TTT 样本后，WorldAgen 在多项任务上超越当前最先进方法，证明了推理时适配世界模型的有效性。

Conclusion: WorldAgen 提出了一种联合世界建模与行为预测并支持测试时训练（TTT）的统一框架，通过共享 Transformer 主干与双头结构以及混合单向注意力掩码实现。测试阶段通过采样探索动作、收集真实转移并对世界模型进行轻量更新，进而提升动作预测性能。在 CALVIN 与 LIBERO 基准上，基线已达可比或优于现有方法，且在少量样本的 TTT 下超过现有最先进方法。

Abstract: How can vision-language-action (VLA) models adapt to new environments where world dynamics shift? While recent research has combined world modeling and action prediction to improve VLA performance, existing methods largely rely on pretraining on static datasets, without mechanisms for active adaptation at deployment time. As a result, these models often fail to generalize when deployed in unseen scenarios with novel object configurations or dynamics. We present WorldAgen, a unified framework that jointly learns world modeling and action prediction while enabling Test-Time Training (TTT) to adapt to new environments. WorldAgen employs a shared Transformer backbone with two heads: (1) a world model head that predicts future states from past state-action trajectories, and (2) an agent model head that predicts actions conditioned on task instructions. We design a Mixed Unidirectional Attention Mask to separate these two models. During test time, WorldAgen samples exploratory actions, collects ground-truth state transitions, and performs lightweight TTT updates to refine its world model. This adaptation improves the model's understanding of the environment and leads to more accurate action predictions. Experiments on the CALVIN and LIBERO benchmarks demonstrate that our baseline model achieves comparable, and in some cases superior, performance to current state-of-the-art approaches. Moreover, with TTT on a small number of samples, our method surpasses existing state-of-the-art models, highlighting the effectiveness of adapting world models at inference time.

</details>


### [197] [Key Path Identification for Resolving Knowledge Conflicts via SAE-based Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08173)
*Wenbo Zhang,Zhongxiang Sun,Zhiguang Han,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KPI finds causally central SAE features and steers along compact key paths, achieving better accuracy and interpretability while modifying far fewer features than mass-steering methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing SAE mass-steering methods select large batches of features via correlation-based heuristics, which misidentify causally irrelevant or redundant features and ignore feature interactions; empirical observation shows steering a much smaller subset can achieve equal or better effect.

Method: KPI detects key steering features that have strong causal dependencies with both upstream and downstream features, assembles these into key paths, and applies steering by modifying far fewer SAE features than mass-steering baselines.

Result: In retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) tasks with knowledge conflicts, KPI improves accuracy by ~18% on average over the best mass-steering baseline, effectively filtering redundant features, reducing side effects, and demonstrating that key paths play a core role in steering.

Conclusion: KPI advances SAE-based steering from quantity-driven to quality-focused by identifying and steering only a small set of key features tied by strong causal dependencies, yielding more precise and interpretable model edits with improved downstream accuracy.

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE)-based steering has been widely used to address knowledge conflicts by guiding LLMs to be more faithful to the contextual knowledge. Existing methods usually perform mass steering, which modifies a large batch of SAE features identified via correlation-based methods. However, due to the inaccurate correlation and the neglected feature interactions, mass steering methods fail to precisely identify the features that play the key roles in steering and introduce a large number of redundant ones, which add noise and weaken the steering effects. Our empirical studies reveal that steering only a small subset of the identified features can achieve comparable or even better performance. Motivated by this finding, we propose Key Path Identification (KPI), a novel method that identifies key steering features characterized by strong causal dependencies with both upstream and downstream features. From these features, KPI constructs key paths and steers through less feature modifications. In this way, KPI advances SAE-based steering from quantity-driven to quality-focused, offering a perspective for more precise and interpretable model editing. Experiments in RAG tasks with knowledge conflicts show that our method improves the accuracy by 18% on average compared to the best baseline of mass steering, effectively filtering redundant features, alleviating side effects and demonstrating the core role of key paths in steering.

</details>


### [198] [OntologyBench: Can Dense Retrieval Satisfy Structured Biomedical Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08174)
*Xiao Yu Cindy Zhang,Wyeth Wasserman,Jian Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OntoloyBench，用以评估概念、关系与表型组合检索。嵌入方法对关系与组合任务表现较差；本体驱动微调有助于部分任务；重排序与LLM评分带来很少或无提升。结果显示需更好地将学习到的表示与结构化本体知识结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索方法在概念层面可行，但缺乏对本体编码的关系与表型组合的系统性评估。需衡量表示学习方法能否捕捉结构化生物医学知识（本体关系与表型组合）的兼容性，从而推动更可靠的临床/生物信息检索系统。

Method: 构建了一个分层生物医学检索基准OntoloyBench，包含471,854训练和125,744评估的查询-文档相关性对，覆盖概念归一(concept grounding)、关系检索(relational retrieval)和基于表型的组合检索(compositional phenotype-based retrieval)。系统性评估了基于嵌入的检索器、在本体衍生监督下的微调、重排序器(rerankers)以及基于大模型(LLM)的候选评分器的端到端表现，并分析了常见错误模式。

Result: 跨层级任务中：嵌入方法在关系型与组合型检索上的表现普遍低于概念归一任务；在本体衍生监督下进行微调能提升若干关系与组合任务的性能；而评估的重排序与LLM候选评分方法对端到端性能几乎无改善或无改善。常见错误是模型返回仅匹配表型证据子集的疾病实体。

Conclusion: 当前评估的嵌入与重排序配置无法可靠恢复所选本体关系和表型组合所编码的兼容性。未来需要设计能将表示学习与本体结构（关系、组合语义）更紧密整合的检索系统，例如本体感知的表示学习、图结构推理或专门建模部分匹配与集合语义的目标函数。

Abstract: We introduce OntologyBench, a tiered biomedical retrieval benchmark comprising 471,854 training and 125,744 evaluation query-document relevance pairs across concept grounding, relational retrieval, and compositional phenotype-based retrieval.
  Although these tasks can be tractable using ontology-aware reference methods, across task tiers, embedding performance is generally lower on relational and compositional tasks than on concept-grounding tasks. Fine-tuning on ontology-derived supervision improves performance on several relational and compositional tasks, whereas the evaluated reranking and LLM-based candidate-scoring methods provide little or no end-to-end improvement. Errors frequently reflect diseases matching only subsets of the phenotype evidence.
  These findings indicate that the evaluated embedding and reranking configurations do not reliably recover the compatibility encoded by the selected ontology relations and phenotype combinations and motivate retrieval systems that better integrate learned representations with structured biomedical knowledge.

</details>


### [199] [Safe Harness Self-Evolution: A Theoretical Analysis of Feasibility and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08175)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Jun Nie,Maohao Ran,Huajiang Zheng,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo,Wei Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定用户—任务分布下，对“冻结模型下的自我演化”给出系统理论分析：生成与评估是独立瓶颈，评估成本在接近最优时爆炸，导致可能停滞；提供有限样本的安全选择与采纳界，及多次迭代下改进累积与局限性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在不修改底层语言模型权重的前提下，代理如何通过自我修改（提示、工具、代码、编排）持续改进行为并保持安全性——即在保证对既有任务不造成不可接受退化的同时追求总体表现提升。目标是给出理论基础以诊断瓶颈并指导设计更安全的自我演化机制。

Method: 形式化“harness self-evolution”问题，将其分解为①修改生成（生成候选修改），②有限数据认证与选择（用有限样本估计候选的安全性和改进性），③安全采纳（在采纳时控制对保留任务的影响），以及④更新后行为分析。基于固定用户—任务分布，给出期望回报改进的充分条件、生成合格修改的概率表征、以及安全选择/采纳的有限样本界。分析同时考察了生成和认证两类约束的不同影响，并讨论了多次迭代下改进保证的累积性质和可能的停滞情形。

Result: （1）证明在固定任务分布和满足约束时，期望回报可保证提升且对保留任务的变化可被控制；（2）刻画了生成合格修改的概率与当前任务表现无必然关系，生成更多候选在评估受限时不一定提高成功采纳的概率；（3）给出有限数据下选择与采纳的置信界限；（4）表明识别微小改进的最坏评估成本在期望回报逼近上界时发散；（5）在有限次更新内已认证改进可累积，但单次成功更新并不保证后续仍有改进余地。

Conclusion: 在固定用户—任务分布下，经过有限数据的安全采纳机制可以为保持底层语言模型冻结的自我演化（通过修改提示、工具、代码或编排）提供可证的期望回报改进，同时控制对已保留任务的影响，但这种保障依赖于生成与评估两个独立的瓶颈，且在接近最优回报时评估成本会爆炸式增长，导致可能的停滞与有限改进空间。

Abstract: Harness self-evolution is the process by which an agent modifies its prompts, tools, code, or orchestration in response to task feedback while keeping the underlying language model frozen, with changes persisting across subsequent tasks. We provide a systematic theoretical analysis of the feasibility and limits of safe harness self-evolution, connecting modification generation, finite-data certification and selection, safe adoption, and behavior after an update. Under a fixed user-task distribution, we establish conditions guaranteeing overall expected-reward improvement while controlling changes on retained tasks, characterize the probability of generating qualified modifications, and derive finite-data bounds for safe selection and adoption. Our analysis shows that generation and certification impose distinct constraints: current task performance does not determine the probability of generating qualified modifications, and generating more candidates need not improve the guarantee of a successful update when evaluation is limiting. Stagnation may therefore arise even when improvement opportunities remain. We further show that worst-case evaluation cost for recognizing genuine improvements diverges as expected reward approaches its upper bound. Across successive updates, certified improvement guarantees accumulate over a finite run, but a successful update does not by itself guarantee that further improvement remains possible. These results provide a basis for diagnosing bottlenecks and designing safer self-evolution mechanisms.

</details>


### [200] [Less Is Personal: Learning Minimal Sufficient User Profiles for Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08180)
*Minghang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于有界反事实搜索与多头价值控制器的自适应记录选择与停止机制，以最小化检索上下文长度同时保持个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定数量的检索记录常导致冗余、无关或有害的上下文，浪费令牌预算且可能降低个性化质量；需自适应地构建仅在保持效用时最小化成本的个性化上下文。

Method: 通过离线有界反事实搜索评估候选配置文件前缀的下游收益、用户特异性与令牌成本，生成长时序目标；将其蒸馏到一个多头价值控制器（显式排序与停止监督），推理时控制器逐步选择/排序记录并在发出停止后调用冻结的生成器。

Result: 在六个个性化任务上，ENOUGH 比强基线和启发式检索增强方法在效果与效率上均有一致提升，能生成保持个性化效用的最小充分配置文件并降低不必要的上下文开销。

Conclusion: ENOUGH 提出并验证了“最小充分个性化”框架，该方法能在保留个性化效用的前提下，构建最小长度的用户行为记录配置文件，从而减少上下文成本并提升检索增强的生成效能与效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented personalization enables large language models to produce more accurate and preference-aligned outputs using relevant records retrieved from user histories. Personalized language models typically prepend a fixed number of retrieved user records, even when additional history is redundant, harmful, or unrelated to a user's distinctive behavior. We study minimal sufficient personalization: constructing the least costly ordered profile for each input while preserving the utility achievable from a retrieved candidate pool. We introduce ENOUGH, a method that iteratively appends behavioral records or emits STOP to construct profiles with adaptive lengths. Offline, bounded counterfactual search evaluates profile prefixes by jointly considering downstream gains, user specificity, and token costs. The resulting long-horizon targets are distilled into a multi-head value controller with explicit ranking and stopping supervision. At inference, the controller selects and orders records through lightweight decisions, and the frozen generator is invoked once after stopping. Extensive experiments on six personalized tasks demonstrate that ENOUGH consistently outperforms strong heuristic and retrieval-augmented baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, achieving minimal sufficient profiles that preserve personalization utility while reducing unnecessary context costs.

</details>


### [201] [Does Deeper Reasoning Compromise Alignment? Revealing and Mitigating of Alignment Collapse in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08186)
*Yu-Hang Wu,Yu-Jie Xiong,Henghua Zhang,Bairui Zhang,Jia-Chen Zhang,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“深度推理可能导致对齐崩溃”的观点，引入Alignment Loss Rate(ALR)量化这一现象。实验证明随着推理深度增加，ALR显著上升；提出攻击范式Reasoning Trap(RT)通过诱导长链推理放大对抗效果；通过注意力分析指出Attention Dilution为根本原因，并提出轻量防御策略Reasoning Residual Alignment(RRA)以动态增强输入残差信息以恢复对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大型推理模型在延长链式推理下对齐机制的稳健性，揭示潜在的安全漏洞并探索轻量防御。

Method: 提出ALR指标评估对齐随推理深度的退化；构造RT攻击来诱导长链推理并测量安全能力下降；通过注意力权重分析验证Attention Dilution；设计并评估RRA（在推理过程中加入输入残差再强调）作为防御。

Result: 实验证明ALR随推理深度显著上升，RT能有效放大对抗攻击使安全能力下降；注意力稀释现象得到证实；RRA显著降低ALR并提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 长链推理会削弱模型对齐鲁棒性，注意力稀释是主要机制，RRA可在一定程度上缓解该崩溃。

Abstract: The emergence of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has established a robust foundation for Large Reasoning Models (LRMs). While deep reasoning is widely believed to enhance safety alignment, the stability of alignment mechanisms under extended reasoning remains underexplored. This paper challenges the prevailing view by revealing a critical vulnerability: Deep Reasoning May Induce Alignment Collapse. To rigorously quantify this phenomenon, we propose the Alignment Loss Rate (ALR) metric. Our experiments demonstrate that as reasoning depth increases, ALR rises significantly, indicating a severe degradation in model robustness against external perturbations. Capitalizing on this instability, a novel jailbreaking paradigm, Reasoning Trap (RT), is proposed. RT induces the model into extended reasoning to amplify the impact of adversarial attacks, leading to a sharp decline in safety capabilities. To elucidate the mechanism behind this collapse, we identify Attention Dilution as the root cause, arising from the competition for attention between the extended reasoning process and the original input. To mitigate this, Reasoning Residual Alignment (RRA), a lightweight defense strategy that dynamically re-emphasizes the input via residual connections integrated with the reasoning process.

</details>


### [202] [Bridging the Semantic-Utility Gap in Multimodal RAG via Generator-in-the-Loop Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08188)
*Zhan-Lun Chang,Dong-Jun Han,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour,Mung Chiang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出生成器闭环的两阶段对齐框架：用VLM生成假想文本做检索查询，并用VLM产出的“能否产生正确答案”作为自动标注来微调LoRA cross-encoder重排序器。无需人工文档标注，能提升检索到对生成器有用的证据，从而改善VLM-RAG表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索器和重排序器主要优化语义相似性，未直接优化“是否能帮助生成器给出正确答案”的效用，导致检索到看似相关但对生成器无用的文档——需要一种无需人工文档标注而能对齐到生成器答案效用的方案。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段用冻结的VLM从图像-查询对生成假想文本段落，作为密集文本检索的查询以缩小图像到文本的模态差距；第二阶段使用LoRA适配的cross-encoder重排序器，根据冻结VLM在候选文档上下文下是否能生成正确答案来自动挖掘正负偏好对，并用这些答案监督的偏好对以对比（triplet）、DPO或SFT等多种损失微调重排序器，支持周期性重采样刷新偏好对。

Result: 在VQA-X和A-OKVQA数据集上，采用Qwen3.5-2B与Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct的实验表明：该框架在不同对齐损失和候选池大小设置下，均显著优于基于排序顺序、随机及REPLUG式似然基线，表明基于答案的生成器反馈是有效的偏好监督信号。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种无需人工文档标注的生成器闭环对齐框架，证明了基于生成器答案级反馈的偏好监督能显著提升面向回答效用的文档检索/重排序性能，从而缩小检索器/重排序器与生成器之间的偏好差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) augmented with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) benefit from access to external evidence. However, standard retrievers and rerankers optimize for semantic similarity rather than answer utility, creating a preference gap: documents that appear relevant may not help the generator produce a correct answer. Motivated by this, we propose a two-stage generator-in-the-loop alignment framework that closes this gap without human document-level relevance annotations. Our framework consists of two stages: in Stage 1, a VLM generates a hypothetical text passage from the image-query pair, which is used as the retrieval query for dense text search, bridging the image-to-text modality gap. In Stage 2, a cross-encoder reranker adapted with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is fine-tuned using answer-supervised preference pairs mined from the frozen VLM: given the dataset answer label, a candidate document is labeled positive if the VLM produces the correct answer when given that document as context, and negative otherwise. This generator-guided signal is compatible with multiple alignment loss functions, including contrastive (triplet) loss, pairwise direct preference optimization (DPO), and supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and supports periodic re-mining to refresh preference pairs as the reranker improves. Experiments on VQA-X and A-OKVQA with Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct show that our proposed framework consistently outperforms rank-order, random, and REPLUG-style likelihood baselines under various alignment losses and pool size settings, suggesting that answer-level generator feedback is an effective supervision signal for preference alignment.

</details>


### [203] [Do Dynamic Routers Need Memory? HeRo: History-Aware Routing for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08189)
*Hongjin Lin,Wentao Wan,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过为路由器维护一个基于线性注意力的累积路由历史，HeRo 将路由决策从局部条件扩展为跨层依赖的联合决策，从而在冻结主干的前提下训练轻量路由器获得更高的性能保持与更大参数绕过率，且在消融和多任务评估中表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态层路由把每次路由决策当作仅依赖当前隐藏状态的局部操作，忽视了跨深度的序列性与决策间的路径依赖性：先前的路由决定会改变下游路由器所见的表示，而且目标（如层使用率或性能约束）是跨层耦合的。作者因此引入显式历史以解决二者不匹配。

Method: 提出 History-Aware Routing (HeRo)：为路由器增加基于线性注意力的记忆模块，增量地聚合先前的路由分数与相应的残差更新为紧凑历史向量；在每一被路由的层，路由器同时以该历史向量与当前隐藏表示为条件，决定执行哪个分支。方法在 token-wise FFN 路由上实例化，主干参数冻结，仅训练轻量路由器和适配器。

Result: 在多个 Llama 变体（Llama 3.1-8B、Llama 2-7B、Llama 2-13B）和七个基准上，HeRo 在十个基线方法中保持最高的整体性能保留率。具体指标：在 Llama 3.1-8B 上绕过 26.87% 参数时达到 100.24% 的密集模型性能；在更严格的计算预算下绕过 38.82% 参数时仍保留 97.01%。消融实验表明去掉路由历史会导致性能下降，尤其在多步推理与代码生成任务上更明显。

Conclusion: HeRo 的主要结论是：在层级路由中引入显式的路由历史记忆可以显著提升动态跳过层策略的准确性和稳健性，使路由器能够考虑先前决策对后续表示的影响，从而在不修改预训练主干参数的前提下，通过训练轻量路由器与适配器在多种 Llama 模型上实现更高的性能保持率与更大的参数绕过比例。

Abstract: Dynamic layer routing reduces the inference cost of Large Language Models (LLMs) by learning to skip layers for individual tokens. Existing methods, however, treat each routing decision as a local operation conditioned solely on the current hidden state which is a formulation that overlooks the sequential, path-dependent nature of routing across depth: earlier decisions shape the representations seen by downstream routers, and the layer-usage objective couples all decisions jointly. We propose History-Aware Routing (HeRo), a dynamic routing framework that resolves this mismatch by introducing a router memory mechanism to maintain an explicit routing state across model depth. The memory is constructed via linear attention, incrementally aggregating preceding routing scores and their induced residual updates into a compact history representation. At each routed layer, the router conditions jointly on this accumulated state and the current hidden representation to select the executed branch. Instantiated for token-wise FFN routing, HeRo trains only lightweight routers and adapters on a frozen backbone, requiring no modification to pretrained parameters. Across Llama 3.1-8B, Llama 2-7B, and Llama 2-13B, HeRo consistently achieves the highest aggregate performance retention among ten baselines. On Llama 3.1-8B, it bypasses 26.87% of model parameters while achieving 100.24% of dense model performance across seven benchmarks, and retains 97.01% while bypassing 38.82% of model parameters under a tighter computation budget. Ablation studies confirm that removing routing history consistently degrades performance, most notably on multistep reasoning and code generation, validating that explicit routing memory enables more accurate and adaptive dynamic routing than solely conditioning on hidden state.

</details>


### [204] [A Better Spur Should Start From Each Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08211)
*Shanwen Mao,Hao Zhang,Guangtao nie,Zhiheng Li,Huimu Wang,Sulong Xu,Gu Simiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMPO通过在数据、梯度和约束层的细粒度干预，有效缓解MORL中的奖励稀疏与冲突问题，从而提高稳定性并在多任务上实现更好且更鲁棒的多目标优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界多目标强化学习（MORL）面临稀疏奖励、奖励冲突和训练后期的‘拔河’效应，传统线性标量化在此类场景下导致指标剧烈震荡，无法稳定优化多目标。

Method: 提出Multi-Marginal Preference Optimization (MMPO)，在数据层、梯度层和约束层进行细粒度干预：1) 曝光去偏（exposure debiasing）以缓解稀疏与偏置奖励；2) 优先感知的正交投影（priority-aware orthogonal projection）用于解耦冲突梯度；3) 自提示梯度约束（self-prompted gradient constraints）以防止强势目标压制弱势目标。整体替代全局线性标量化的方法。

Result: 在真实电商数据集上，MMPO提升了训练稳定性并在互冲突的指标上持续取得更优表现；同时在ToolRL和代码生成等更广泛任务上也显示出良好的泛化性，表明其作为多目标对齐的实用范式具有有效性。

Conclusion: MMPO是一种有前景的细粒度多目标优化范式，能减少线性标量化带来的震荡并提升鲁棒性；需要关注的潜在问题包括实现复杂度、计算开销、超参敏感性和在更多异构现实任务上的长期稳定性验证。

Abstract: Real-world Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) often suffers from sparse rewards, reward conflicts, and late-stage reward tug-of-war, causing traditional linear scalarization to experience severe metric oscillations. To address optimization conflicts among multiple objectives in real-world deployment scenarios, we propose Multi-Marginal Preference Optimization (MMPO), a fine-grained framework that intervenes at the data, gradient, and constraint levels rather than relying on coarse-grained global scalarization. Specifically, MMPO performs exposure debiasing to mitigate sparse and biased rewards, applies priority-aware orthogonal projection to decouple conflicting gradients, and introduces self-prompted gradient constraints to prevent dominant objectives from overwhelming weaker ones. Experiments on real-world e-commerce datasets show that MMPO improves training stability and consistently achieves better performance across conflicting metrics. Moreover, it generalizes robustly to broader tasks such as ToolRL and code generation, demonstrating its effectiveness as a practical paradigm for multi-objective alignment.

</details>


### [205] [Vision: Data-Centric Anchoring for Robust and Interpretable Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08216)
*Arun Vignesh Malarkkan,Xinyuan Wang,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“数据中心化锚定”观念，认为代理式AI的鲁棒性与可解释性问题源于交互日志缺乏反事实结构，提出Data-Centric Agentic Loop（Curate, Augment, Constrain, Attribute）作为四阶段闭环以通过数据工程而非模型改造来改善鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测性交互日志仅记录“做了什么”，缺乏“如果做了不同选择会怎样”的反事实对照，导致模型学习到伪相关且解释欠缺因果性；因此需要在数据层面制造可变性与对照以获得不变量与可信解释。

Method: 提出一个结构化的数据生命周期框架（Curate→Augment→Constrain→Attribute），强调顺序不可颠倒：先策划高质量基础数据，再用生成与扰动扩增环境多样性，接着用约束（不变性目标、正则化或接口限制）在多环境上训练，最后归因化失败并把结果转化为下次数据干预。

Result: 提出的框架将四大失败类型（伪特征依赖、分布漂移脆弱、置信度失校准、解释不可信）映射到数据生命周期的四个阶段，并讨论了该方法的局限性与未来需要解决的工程与评估问题。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性和可解释性应在数据环境中被工程化：仅靠模型方法无法恢复数据中不存在的反事实/不变量。通过按序的策划-扩增-约束-归因循环，可以制造出模型需要的变异性和监督信号，从而减轻伪相关、分布漂移、置信误差和解释不可信等失败模式。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems built on large language models fail in two persistent ways that scaling does not fix: they break under distribution shift, and they cannot explain the decisions they make. We argue these are co-symptoms of one structural deficiency in the data lifecycle that governs how agents are trained, evaluated, and deployed. Observational interaction logs record what an agent did, not what it would have done otherwise. They encode spurious correlations without controlled variation, so they lack the counterfactual structure needed to separate causal signal from coincidence or to validate an explanation. No model-centric method can recover invariances the data never contained. We present Data-Centric Anchoring: robustness and interpretability should be engineered into the data environment, not extracted from models after training. Our central contribution is the Data-Centric Agentic Loop, a four-stage framework of Curate, Augment, Constrain, and Attribute. The ordering is structural, not stylistic. Curation precedes augmentation because generative models amplify whatever bias they are trained on. Augmentation precedes constraint because invariance objectives are vacuous without variation across environments to be invariant to. Attribution closes the loop, converting observed failures into targeted data interventions for the next iteration. Each stage manufactures the preconditions of the next, which makes the loop self-correcting rather than merely sequential. We ground the framework in a failure-driven taxonomy that links four core failure modes to the data lifecycle: spurious feature reliance, distribution-shift fragility, uncertainty miscalibration, and explanation unfaithfulness. We close with the limits of this approach and the open problems that stand between it and practical deployment at scale.

</details>


### [206] [TTGBench: Benchmarking Topological Evolution and Semantic Drift in Text-attributed Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08226)
*Longfei Ma,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TTGBench基准，联合评估图结构和语义随时间演化，包含6个文本丰富数据集，支持多类和多标签时序节点分类，比较17种TGNN与LLM方法并发现能力分化：TGNN擅长结构预测，LLM擅长语义追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准偏重结构演化（TLP），缺乏对现实中语义演化（语义漂移）与复杂多类/多标签时序节点分类的评估；同时数据集存在大量重复链接，导致性能被高估。

Method: 构建具有“双重波动性”的6个真实文本数据集，设计多类别与多标签的时序节点分类任务，并在此基础上进行时序链路预测与节点语义跟踪评估，对17个现有TGNN与LLM方法进行全面对比与消融分析。

Result: 发现TGNN在结构预测上表现优异但对语义漂移跟踪能力差，LLM则相反；当前基准与方法各有短板，TTGBench可更公平评估模型并推动混合方法研究。

Conclusion: TTGBench填补了时序语义评估空白，展示当前方法在结构与语义两端的互补与不足，提示需要融合两类范式的混合模型以同时处理连接演化与语义漂移。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning models the evolution of dynamic systems, where both structural interactions and semantic states change over time. However, existing benchmarks primarily emphasize structural evolution via temporal link prediction (TLP), while support for semantic evolution remains limited. Although temporal node classification (TNC) is sometimes included, it is typically restricted to simplistic binary settings that fail to capture realistic semantic drift. Moreover, commonly used datasets exhibit high link repetition, leading to inflated performance estimates and obscuring true model capability. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{TTGBench}, a new benchmark that jointly evaluates structural and semantic evolution. TTGBench comprises six real-world, text-rich datasets characterized by \emph{Dual Volatility}, enabling rigorous and fair evaluation of existing models. Notably, it is the first benchmark to support both multi-class and multi-label TNC, filling a critical gap in evaluating temporal semantic drift. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 17 state-of-the-art methods across Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) and Large Language Model (LLM)-based paradigms. The results reveal a clear \emph{capability divide} between the two paradigms: TGNN-based methods excel at structural prediction but fail at semantic tracking, whereas LLM-based predictors show the opposite trend. Through in-depth analysis, we uncover their fundamental limitations and provide insights for developing more comprehensive temporal graph models.

</details>


### [207] [SE-GoS: Self-Evolving Graph-of-Skills for Skill Library at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08228)
*Dawei Fu,Cheng Jiang,Sitian Qian,Huainan Wang,Zhongkai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SE-GoS 是一个无训练、基于执行轨迹的技能图演化框架，通过拓扑、边权与描述三种更新，显著提升检索效果并降低输入代价，且结果能迁移到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技能库规模扩展到成千上万，检索成为瓶颈。虽有 GoS/SkillDAG 利用图结构或在线累积执行结构，但是否能系统地将历史执行轨迹蒸馏成对未见任务具有泛化能力的更好检索图尚未解答。

Method: 基于执行痕迹对既有 Graph-of-Skills 进行三类无训练更新：①拓扑演化——基于执行证据发现或剪枝技能关系；②边权演化——根据历史有效性强化检索相关关系；③描述演化——用执行反馈优化技能的面向检索的描述；保持原检索流水线不变。

Result: 在 SkillsBench 上对三种 LLM 的评测显示：与全量技能加载相比，SE-GoS 一致提升任务奖励并减少输入 token；代表性场景下一轮演化将奖励从 52.4% 提升到 59.4%，同时输入 token 约减少三分之一；模型间增益存在差异，且演化后图能迁移到不相交的 held-out 划分，较静态 GoS 提升 5.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: SE-GoS 能在不进行模型训练、无需修改检索算法或技能内容的前提下，从历史执行轨迹中演化原有的 GoS 技能图，提升任务成功率并减少输入代价，将静态检索图转变为可自我演化的检索基础设施。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly rely on reusable skills, yet as skill libraries scale to thousands of entries, effective retrieval becomes a bottleneck. Graph-of-Skills (GoS) addresses this challenge by exploiting dependency-aware graph structure for scalable skill retrieval, while SkillDAG further demonstrates that skill graphs can accumulate execution-backed structure online. However, these approaches leave open whether historical execution traces can be systematically distilled into a better retrieval graph that generalizes to unseen tasks. We present Self-Evolving Graph-of-Skills (SE-GoS), a training-free framework that evolves an existing GoS graph from execution traces while preserving the original retrieval pipeline. SE-GoS performs three complementary updates: topology evolution that discovers and prunes skill relationships from execution evidence, edge-weight evolution that reinforces retrieval-relevant relationships based on historical effectiveness, and description evolution that optimizes retrieval-facing skill descriptions using execution feedback. Across three LLMs on SkillsBench, SE-GoS consistently improves task reward while reducing input tokens relative to full skill loading, with gains varying across model families. In a representative setting, one evolution round improves reward from 52.4\% to 59.4\% while reducing input tokens by approximately one-third relative to full skill loading, and the resulting graph transfers to a disjoint held-out split with a 5.4-point improvement over the static GoS baseline. These results show that skill graphs can be improved from execution experience without model training, changes to the retrieval algorithm, or modifications to skill content, turning a static retrieval graph into an evolving retrieval infrastructure.

</details>


### [208] [Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08236)
*Yongxi Zhou,Wenbo Ye,Yuanzhe Liu,Zihan Dong,Junwei Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在不改变回复主体内容的前后加入样式包装，发现部分自动安全评判器会将“危险”回复判为“安全”，说明许多评判器对风格敏感而非纯语义，且这些盲点可被廉价利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验自动安全评判器是否根据回复的实际内容（语义）做判定，还是对回复的“音色/风格”敏感，从而被廉价的样式包装规避。

Method: 保留回复主体的字节不变，在回复前后加入内容不变但风格不同的固定字符串（教育性免责声明、伪安全“推理”块、带拒绝令牌的前缀等），在600个JailbreakBench回复×最多7种包装×8个自动安全评判器上测量评判翻转率，使用配对显著性检验并测定噪声底线；并用两位人工注释验证内容不变性和翻转是否为评判错误。

Result: 大多数评判器对包装不敏感但存在可廉价利用的盲点：例如，token-refusal包装使GPT-4o-mini对原本正确的“危险”判定翻转19.9%（噪声底线0.5%；三次多数重评分18.2%），而Claude仅被翻转0.4%；部署的Llama Guard 4在“教育课程”框架下被确定性规避，翻转率12.3%；另一个部署守卫gpt-oss-safeguard-20b免疫；仅改写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）能将同一模型的弱点降低十倍，说明漏洞存在于评判器而非被评判内容。人工两人验证确认内容字节不变性100%，90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95–1.0）。数据、包装、代码与逐条标签已发布。

Conclusion: 自动安全评判器并非总基于语义内容做出判定，易受样式包装欺骗；评估与防御应加入内容不变性测试、多评判器/人工验证、标准化与对抗性训练来提高鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.

</details>


### [209] [zScore-N: A Neural Network for On-Chain Wallet Reputation Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08247)
*Girish G N,Ashutosh Sahoo,Akshay SP,Gurukiran S,Dhanashekar Kandaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用无噪声教师数据蒸馏训练的神经网络高精度复现并超越手写评分在缺失数据鲁棒性与生产可扩展性上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写信誉公式虽可读但为分段、不可微、静态且无法随数据自我改进，且不能区分真实为零的特征与管道未捕获的缺失；需要一个可学习、可微、对缺失与规模更鲁棒的评分器。

Method: 以已校准且严格复现生产输出的手写公式作为“教师”，在 2019–2024 年采样的 5,208,952 个钱包上自动生成无限无噪声标签；训练带缺失值掩码的神经网络（zScore-N）以回归教师分数；对比基线包括梯度提升树与线性回归，指标为 1000 分制的 RMSE 与 R^2，并在不同特征缺失率下评估漂移与偏差；最终在生产环境中部署并验证跨不同规模与活动度钱包的表现。

Result: 神经网络在复现教师上达到 0.58 点 RMSE（1000 分制，R^2=0.99997），优于梯度提升树（2.25）与线性回归（28.04）；在 10% 特征级缺失下，教师公式自我输出漂移 51.4 点并呈 -12.5 点系统性偏差，而网络漂移仅 17.9 点（误差减半）；网络已在生产环境承载评分，跨六个数量级的钱包规模表现良好。

Conclusion: zScore-N 用神经网络成功替换了原先的手写信誉评分公式：在保留可读性与确定性的前提下，实现了高保真复现、对缺失数据更强的鲁棒性，并在生产规模（数百万钱包）上部署。

Abstract: Wallet reputation scores decide who receives an airdrop, who can borrow, and who enters an allowlist across decentralised finance. They almost always begin as hand-written formulas: compositions of clamped logarithmic, linear and square-root transforms over behavioural features, with every threshold and point award set by hand. Such a formula is readable and deterministic, but it is piecewise and non-differentiable, it cannot improve as data accumulates, and it cannot distinguish a feature that is genuinely zero from one its pipeline failed to capture. We present zScore-N, the neural network that replaced ours in production. The formula served as its teacher: calibrated against 5,208,952 wallets sampled across 2019-2024 and verified to reproduce production output to within 2.3e-13, it supplies unlimited labelled training data at zero label noise. The trained network reproduces it to 0.58 points RMSE on the 1000-point scale (R^2 = 0.99997), against 2.25 for gradient-boosted trees and 28.04 for linear regression on identical features and splits. Trained with missing-value masks against uncorrupted targets, it halves the error that incomplete data introduces: at 10% feature-level missingness the formula drifts 51.4 points from its own complete-data output with a systematic -12.5 point bias, while the network drifts 17.9. The network carries the score at production scale, across a population of millions of wallets spanning six orders of magnitude in size and activity.

</details>


### [210] [Agentic ML Exploration (A-MLE) for Ads Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08248)
*Erwin Gao,Vinodh Kumar Sunkara,Jingyi Guan,Qinjin Jia,Hangjun Xu,Xiang Ji,Sherman Wong,Surya Teja Chavali,Pratik Vaishnavi,Aryan Pandhi,Xiaoyu Deng,Zhaodong Wang,Samarth Inani,Fan Yang,Jakob Moberg,Zoe Zu,Nicolas Bievre,Sami Khenissi,Amit Jaspal,Ehsan Fakharizadi,Srinidhi Viswanathan,Dorothy Sun,Abishek Vanam,Sneha Iyer,Sheela Yadawad,Wenjie Chen,Gaby Nahum,Junhua Gu,Peter Chu,Yucheng Liu,Xin Zhao,Vitor Cid,Chaorong Chen,Vijay Pappu,Ashwin Kumar,Wenlin Chen,Ben Schulte,Deepak Chandra,Ritwik Tewari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用自治 LLM 代理系统系统化地跨模型探索 ML 技术，减轻人类工程师负担并加快在工业推荐系统中发现可复用改进的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告排序中，瓶颈不再是模型容量或训练算力，而是人类 ML 迭代通量：各模型异构、专家时间稀缺、技术扩散缓慢，导致可恢复的信号未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出一个基于大模型的自治代理系统（A-MLE），将 ML 迭代分解为假设生成、探索策略、实验执行、结果分析和共享知识底座五个阶段；由单一代理编排领域专用技能和 agentic 工作流，在沙箱执行层运行并在每阶段边界保留人类检查点。

Result: 将 A-MLE 部署于代表性大规模广告排序模型集，按工具可用性、自治工作流执行和开放式探索三层能力框架评估；并在固定代理循环下对 Claude Sonnet、Gemini、GPT 系列进行了对照研究，揭示了执行可靠性和探索激进性上的差异，同时讨论失败模式与设计权衡。

Conclusion: A-MLE 能在工业级广告排序模型组合上作为工程师的增效工具，通过自动化、结构化的探索流程显著提高 ML 迭代的覆盖率与效率，尤其对长期被忽视的小规模模型具有实际价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial ads ranking stacks are increasingly bottlenecked not by model capacity or training compute, but by the throughput of human ML iteration - the cycles of research, implementation, training, debugging, evaluation, and launch required to surface a single statistically significant improvement. A typical ranking stack contains numerous differentiated models with heterogeneous data, architectures, and infrastructure constraints, and each cycle takes days to weeks of senior engineer attention per model. As a result, techniques that have proven effective on one model diffuse into others slowly and unevenly, leaving substantial recoverable signal unexplored. We present Agentic ML Exploration (A-MLE), an autonomous LLM-agent system that systematically explores ML techniques across a portfolio of ads ranking models. A-MLE decomposes ML iteration into five stages involving hypothesis generation, exploration strategy, experiment execution, result analysis and shared knowledge substrate which are orchestrated by a single agent that invokes domain-specific skills and agentic workflows against a sandboxed execution layer, with human-in-the-loop checkpoints at each stage boundary. We deploy A-MLE across a representative set of large-scale ads ranking models and evaluate it along a tiered capability framework (tool availability, autonomous workflow execution, and open-ended exploration). We further report a controlled cross-LLM study using a fixed agent loop, which surfaces qualitative differences in execution reliability and exploration aggressiveness across the Claude Sonnet, Gemini, and GPT families. We discuss failure modes and the design choices that govern reliability. Our findings suggest that agentic exploration is a practical force multiplier for ML engineers in industrial recommenders, especially for the long tail of models that rarely receive expert attention.

</details>


### [211] [CircuTutor: Transforming Static Circuit Problems into Intelligent and Dynamic Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08254)
*Ziyu Luo,Xiaorui Ma,Lin Chen,Xiaoming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一套基于电路状态仿真的智能辅导系统（CircuTutor）：从多模态解析到SPICE求解，再到因果解释与误解诊断，实验显示其能改善直流电路概念学习与体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流电路学习要求将不可见的量（电流、电压、阻抗、功率）与可观察的结果（如灯亮度）建立联系；现有教材与通用仿真器虽支持求解与探索，但对“为什么变化”与“错误推理诊断”的支持有限。

Method: 系统先用多模态题目解析提取题干、拓扑、元件参数、开关状态与备选答案，构造成结构化任务并用SPICE兼容求解器验证。学习者可交互修改参数并提交答案；系统在求解后呈现操作前后电路状态动画，将状态变化组织成因果推理链，映射答题差异到可能误解并生成自适应练习。

Result: 实验结果表明CircuTutor能有效提升概念学习效果与整体学习体验，并展示了将仿真状态转化为智能教学的可行框架，具有向其他STEM领域推广的潜力。

Conclusion: CircuTutor通过把静态教科书电路题转成可交互的电路-状态驱动教学流程，能在模拟驱动的基础上提供因果性解释、误解诊断和针对性练习，从而有效提升学习者的概念理解与学习体验。

Abstract: Learning direct current circuit concepts requires learners to connect invisible physical quantities, such as current, voltage, resistance, and power, with observable outcomes such as bulb brightness. Conventional textbook materials and general-purpose circuit simulators provide opportunities for problem solving and exploration but offer limited support for explaining why circuit behavior changes or diagnosing the reasoning behind incorrect answers. We present CircuTutor, a circuit-state-driven intelligent tutoring system that transforms static textbook circuit problems into an interactive tutoring workflow. CircuTutor first uses multimodal problem parsing to extract the textbook question, circuit topology, component parameters, switch states, and answer options, which are converted into a structured task and validated through circuit simulation. Learners can then interactively explore the circuit (by changing parameters) and submit an answer while a SPICE-compatible solver computes physically consistent circuit states. After the learner submits an answer, CircuTutor presents a before-and-after circuit state animation corresponding to the selected operation, organizes the simulated state changes into a causal reasoning chain that explains the underlying circuit behavior, maps answer discrepancies to likely misconceptions, and generates adaptive follow-up exercises targeted at the diagnosed misconception. Our experimental results demonstrate that CircuTutor effectively improves conceptual learning and the overall learning experience. The proposed framework demonstrates how simulated circuit states can be transformed into intelligent and interactive tutoring for circuit education, with the potential to generalize to other STEM domains.

</details>


### [212] [Revoked but Still Authoritative: An Empirical Study of Revocation Enforcement in Agent-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08258)
*Yi Ting Shen,Kentaroh Toyoda,Alex Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Soft revocation fails in practice because retrieval layers still return revoked facts; enforce revocation at retrieval (they propose a guard that filters revoked/conflicting records).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-running language-model agents require persistent memory and the ability to revoke outdated or unsafe facts. Many systems use soft revocation (mark-as-invalid but retain) but it is unclear whether that marking is enforced during retrieval—this gap could enable unsafe actions.

Method: Empirical evaluation of five agent-memory systems. For each system they load a revoked policy and its replacement, run across nine policy scenarios and nine models, and score every trial under six defense conditions—tracking whether the revoked fact is returned at retrieval and whether the agent acts on it.

Result: No evaluated system enforces revocation by default. When the revocation label is visible to the retrieval layer, the revoked record is returned and often outranks its replacement, leading agents to perform unsafe actions. The paper develops a guard component placed between agent and memory backend that withholds records that are revoked or conflict with their replacements.

Conclusion: Soft revocation as implemented in current agent-memory systems is ineffective by default: revoked facts are still retrieved and used, causing unsafe agent behavior; revocation must be enforced at retrieval time.

Abstract: Long-running language-model agents depend on persistent memory. Many agent-memory systems preserve history through soft revocation: a contradicted fact is marked invalid and retained rather than deleted. However, whether that mark is enforced at retrieval time is unexamined. In this paper, we measure five such systems: we load each with a revoked policy and its replacement, track whether the revoked fact is returned at retrieval and whether the agent then acts on it across nine policy scenarios and nine models, and score every trial under six defense conditions. We find that no system enforces revocation by default: the revoked fact is returned wherever the revocation label is visible to the retrieval layer, outranks its replacement, and leads agents to the unsafe action. Based on these findings, we develop a guard that sits between the agent and any memory backend and withholds records that are revoked or conflict with their replacement.

</details>


### [213] [Evidence-Aligned Entity Verification for Hallucination Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08267)
*Runsong Jia,Zhen Fang,Mengjia Wu,Jie Lu,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RAG环境下的幻觉检测问题，EAEV通过实体—证据对齐和反事实稳定性评估，能更可靠地识别实体级幻觉，对检索质量和鲁棒性做出显性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型的知识会过时或覆盖不足，尤其对领域化或近期信息无法可靠支持；RAG在推理时检索证据可以弥补该缺陷，因此将检索能力引入幻觉检测成为解决信息更新与覆盖问题的关键方向。

Method: 1) 使用RAG检索与生成输出相关的证据上下文；2) 对生成的实体与检索证据在三条互补维度上进行对齐评估（论文需明确三维分别是什么——例如文本匹配、实体链接与语义一致性）；3) 引入反事实稳定性分析，即通过对证据进行扰动/替换来检验对齐的稳健性；4) 基于上述打分判断实体是否为幻觉，并在多个基准上验证性能。

Result: 提出了一种基于RAG的实体级幻觉检测方法EAEV，通过将生成实体与检索到的证据上下文在三维度上对齐，并加入反事实稳定性分析，以提升对实体幻觉的检测能力。实验表明在多个RAG基准上相较于现有方法有稳定提升并具备良好泛化性。

Conclusion: EAEV在概念上合理，能弥补仅依赖模型内在信号的局限，若补充有关三维度设计、检索策略、基准细节和开源代码/模型的描述，并针对检索噪声与非实体型幻觉开展更充分的实验，将更有说服力并易于复现。

Abstract: Hallucination detection is crucial for large language models (LLMs), as hallucinated content creates significant barriers in applications requiring factual accuracy. Current detection methods mainly depend on internal signals like uncertainty and self-consistency checks, using the model's pre-trained knowledge to identify unreliable outputs. However, pre-trained knowledge may become outdated and has coverage limitations, especially for specialized or recent information. To address these limitations, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising solution by retrieving relevant evidence at inference time, grounding outputs beyond the model's parametric knowledge. In this paper, we target a critical and practical learning problem RAG-based hallucination detection (RHD), where RAG is employed to enhance hallucination detection by addressing information updating challenges. To address RHD, we propose a novel method Evidence-Aligned Entity Verification (EAEV), which detects entity-level hallucinations by leveraging RAG to align generated entities with retrieved evidence contexts. Specifically, EAEV evaluates entity-evidence alignment through three complementary dimensions and introduces counterfactual stability analysis to ensure robust alignments under evidence perturbations. Experiments across multiple RAG benchmarks demonstrate that EAEV achieves consistent improvements over existing methods with strong generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [214] [Three Types of Negation of Triple and its Elements and an Extension of Triple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08271)
*Zhenghua Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过在三元组上引入矛盾、相反与中介三类否定，构建TCOI三元组及其基于LCOI+PLCOI的推理与连续真值算法，以增强对复杂负信息与反事实推理的表达与计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的三元组作为语义数据模型仅能直接表示肯定断言，难以区分和表达三元组及其元素中存在的多种否定形式，因而限制了对复杂负信息与反事实推理的表示与推理能力。

Method: 形式化构造：定义SCOI集合与带三种否定的逻辑LCOI+PLCOI；语法/语义扩展：从经典（s,p,o）扩展为带三类否定标记的TCOI三元组，规定否定在元素与整体上的作用规则；推理机制：给出基于LCOI+PLCOI的蕴含推理规则并提出连续真值（实值）计算算法；验证：通过反事实推理示例计算并说明表达与推理过程。

Result: 提出了带有三种否定（矛盾否定、相反否定、中介否定）的TCOI三元组形式化框架，基于集合SCOI与逻辑LCOI+PLCOI给出语法与语义扩展；论证了该模型的表达能力和推理机制，并给出用于三元组蕴含推理的连续真值算法，演示了在反事实与反事实推理中的应用示例。

Conclusion: TCOI三元组在保持传统三元组正断言表达的同时，系统性地引入三维否定语义，使否定能独立作用于三元组的各元素及整体，从而显著增强了表示负信息和进行反事实蕴含推理的能力；后续应关注复杂度分析、与现有语义网标准的兼容性及大规模实现与评测。

Abstract: In various data models, the classical triple is a typical semantic data model. However, due to the design of the triple as a simple structure for representing positive assertions, it cannot sufficiently express different forms of negation present in the triple and its elements. This paper conceptually proposes that there are three distinct forms of negation within triples and their elements: contradictory negation, opposite negation and intermediary negation. Based on the the set SCOI and the logic LCOI+PLCOI with three kinds of negation, we propose an extension of triple that can distinguish and express these three different negations in the triple and its elements, called the TCOI triple with contradictory negation, opposite negation and intermediary negation. The TCOI triple is a semantic and structural extension of the classical triple. While retaining the ability to express positive assertions, it systematically introduces the three semantic dimensions of three negations, allowing these negations to independently act on the elements of the triple and on the whole triple. This significantly enhances the triple model capability to represent and reasoning about complex negative information. This paper also explores the expressive power and reasoning of the TCOI triple, as well as the application of TCOI triple implication reasoning in counterfactuals and counterfactual reasoning. We propose a truth-value (continuous value) algorithm for TCOI triple implication reasoning and perform its calculation through an example of the counterfactuals and counterfactual reasoning.

</details>


### [215] [MemForest: Efficient Agent Memory Management via EventTree Partitioning and Progressive Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08273)
*Junxi Wang,Te Sun,Jiayi Zhu,Chen Zhang,Siyuan Li,Xuyang Liu,Zichen Wen,Xiaobing Tu,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Ziqi Yuan,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemForest以事件为单位构建EventTree并按权重合并冗余节点，配合锚点传播检索，在50%压缩下几乎无性能损失并显著加速检索，适用于多模态和单模态代理记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期积累的代理记忆在个性化对话与视频理解等场景里带来显著的存储与检索开销，需一种通用且高效的压缩机制以在保持性能的前提下降低资源消耗。

Method: 先基于全局语义相似性与局部时间连续性将历史记忆划分为事件单元；对每个事件单元构建最大生成树（EventTree），按高权重边逐步合并冗余节点以减少存储；引入锚点引导的传播检索，从关键节点的时间邻域检索相关记忆以提升召回准确率。

Result: 在 unimodal Mem0 框架上，50% 压缩比下保留97.1%原有性能，跨LoCoMo、LongMemEval与PersonaMem三基准，检索加速1.89×；在 multimodal M3-Agent 框架上，50% 压缩比下保留99.7%性能，跨M3-Bench-robot与M3-Bench-web两基准，检索加速2.24×。代码公开于给定仓库。

Conclusion: MemForest通过事件中心化的分割和EventTree构建-合并策略，有效压缩代理记忆，显著降低存储与检索成本的同时几乎不损害任务性能。

Abstract: Agent memory systems have demonstrated significant potential in long-term dialogue, personalized assistants, and video understanding. However, continuously accumulated memory introduces substantial storage and retrieval costs during inference. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{MemForest}, a general memory compression framework adaptable to various agent memory systems. Specifically, MemForest partitions historical memory into event-centric units by leveraging global semantic similarity and local temporal continuity. For each unit, it constructs a maximum spanning tree, termed an EventTree, and progressively merges redundant memory nodes by selecting high-weight edges, reducing storage overhead. Furthermore, we introduce an anchor-guided propagation retrieval mechanism that retrieves relevant memory nodes from the temporal neighborhoods of key nodes, improving retrieval accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of MemForest. Under the unimodal Mem0 framework, MemForest retains \textbf{97.1%} of the original performance while compressing \textbf{50%} of historical memory across three benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval, and PersonaMem), achieving a \textbf{1.89x} retrieval speedup. Under the multimodal M3-Agent framework, it preserves \textbf{99.7%} of the original performance with a \textbf{50%} compression ratio across two benchmarks (M3-Bench-robot and M3-Bench-web), achieving a \textbf{2.24x} retrieval speedup. \textcolor{RoyalBlue}{\textit{Our code is available at [https://github.com/Celina-love-sweet/MemForest.}}](https://github.com/Celina-love-sweet/MemForest.}})

</details>


### [216] [Beyond Coherence: Benchmarking Professional Editing-Technique Execution in Multi-Shot Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08275)
*Tianyi Zeng,Junchao Liao,Yujie Wei,Ziying Zhang,Litao Li,Tianyi Wang,Zhichao Wei,Shuyao Xu,Wenwen Qiang,Siyu Zhu,Zhenghao Zhang,Long Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CutCraft：首个针对多镜头视听生成中“编辑技术执行”能力的基准。它用带编辑指令的结构化多镜头提示与分层混合评估框架检验模型是否能执行剪辑技巧，并提出了一个将生成拆分为规划、镜头合成与后合成的代理式编辑基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多依赖内容质量、同步性或物理合理性等代理指标，无法直接测量能否执行专业剪辑技巧（镜头结构、转场语法、视听剪接关系与蒙太奇）。因此需要专门的评测来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建带显式编辑规范的结构化多镜头提示集；设计分层混合评估体系，结合镜头结构对齐、专家模型度量、工具落地的多模态判断和基于量表的问答评价；实现一个代理式编辑基线，分为规划、镜头级合成与事后合成以显式实现J-cut、L-cut和过渡时机控制。

Result: 在13个闭源与开源最先进模型上的评估显示：普遍存在镜头结构不稳定、转场执行控制薄弱、复杂蒙太奇表现急剧下降等问题；审美质量与编辑合规性相关性弱。基准、指标与基线已开源（GitHub）。

Conclusion: 现有多镜头视听生成模型虽能产出连贯、影视感的内容，但在按专业编辑指令（如J-cut、L-cut、过渡时机、镜头结构）执行方面存在明显不足；连贯性并不等同于编辑技术的可控执行。

Abstract: Recent multi-shot audio-video generators can produce increasingly coherent and cinematic outputs, but coherence does not imply the ability to execute editing techniques. Professional editing depends on shot structure, transition grammar, audio-video cut relations, and montage, yet existing benchmarks largely rely on proxies such as content quality, synchronization, or physical plausibility, systematically missing whether such editing instructions are actually executed. We introduce CutCraft, the first benchmark for editing-technique execution in multi-shot audio-video generation. CutCraft extends structured multi-shot prompts with explicit editing specifications and is paired with a hierarchical hybrid evaluation framework that combines shot-structure alignment, expert-model metrics, tool-grounded multimodal judgment, and rubric-based question answering. Beyond evaluation, we design an agentic editing baseline that decomposes generation into planning, shot-level synthesis, and post-hoc composition, explicitly realizing editing semantics such as J-cuts, L-cuts, and transition timing. Across 13 state-of-the-art closed- and open-source models, CutCraft reveals a consistent gap between coherence and editing-technique execution: current systems often produce plausible multi-shot videos yet fail to execute editorial instructions reliably. We find unstable shot structures, weak control of transition execution, and sharp degradation on higher-order montage, while aesthetic quality is only weakly correlated with editing-technique compliance. The benchmark and metrics, and the editing agent baseline are available at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/cut-craft-bench.

</details>


### [217] [LEBGen: An LLM-Enhanced Bayesian Network Framework for Few-Shot Travel Survey Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08288)
*Zijian Shen,Bin Zhou,Jiguang Wang,Ya Zhao,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出把 LLM 行为知识嵌入贝叶斯网络结构学习（LEBGen），通过识别画像并修正结构，在极低采样率下生成高保真旅行调查合成数据，实验显示显著减少边缘分布与变量依赖误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模旅行调查昂贵且耗时，少样本难以覆盖异质人群并恢复复杂的人口属性与出行行为依赖；传统贝叶斯网络在少样本下易漏真依赖或保留伪依赖，LLM 的行为知识可作为补充证据。

Method: 提出 LEBGen：先用 LLM 根据人口属性与出行行为统计识别旅行者画像(personas)，将画像信息并入初始贝叶斯网络结构，然后由 LLM 恢复少样本中遗漏的依赖并剪除伪依赖，最终用观测数据估计网络参数并生成合成样本。

Result: 在 2022 年香港旅行特征调查上、2% 少样本设置下，LEBGen 将平均边缘 Jensen–Shannon 散度从 0.0671 降至 0.0091；相较最优基线，平均绝对 Cramér’s V 误差下降 14.3%，在分布性与依赖性保真度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: LEBGen 有效利用大语言模型补充少样本下的行为知识，改进贝叶斯网络结构学习，从而显著提升合成出旅行调查记录的分布和依赖关系保真度。

Abstract: Travel survey data are essential for transportation planning and travel behavior analysis, yet collecting large-scale representative samples is costly and time-consuming. A practical alternative is to generate synthetic survey records from a few-shot sample. However, such samples provide incomplete coverage of heterogeneous traveler groups and insufficient evidence for recovering the complex dependencies between demographic characteristics and travel behavior. Existing approaches have complementary limitations. Probabilistic generative models such as Bayesian networks (BNs) offer explicit distributional control, but structures learned from few-shot samples may omit meaningful dependencies or retain spurious ones. Large language models (LLMs) can help address these difficulties in BN structure learning by providing behavioral knowledge that complements the limited statistical evidence. We therefore propose LEBGen, an LLM-enhanced BN framework that uses this knowledge to refine network structure for few-shot travel survey data generation. Specifically, the LLM first identifies traveler personas from demographic attribute and travel behavior statistics, then recovers dependencies missed by the persona-augmented BN structure and prune spurious ones. The refined BN is parameterized exclusively from the observed data to generate synthetic records. Under a 2% few-shot setting on the 2022 Hong Kong Travel Characteristics Survey, LEBGen reduces the mean marginal Jensen-Shannon divergence from 0.0671 to 0.0091 and the mean absolute Cramer's V error by 14.3% over the best-performing baseline, substantially improving both distributional and dependency fidelity.

</details>


### [218] [FastE: Readout-Triggered Token Compression for LLM Embedding Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08407)
*Jinsong Shu,Jinyong Wen,Baokun Wang,Zhongle Xie,Lidan Shou,Weiqiang Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FastE 是一种无需训练的前缀状态压缩策略，基于批量均值的读出-前缀对齐阈值与读出注意力得分排序来逐层移除冗余前缀，能大幅降低 FLOPs，且保持接近原始嵌入质量，适用于可扩展的检索与多模态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到最终读出嵌入模型的 prefix 状态随深度变得可压缩（越深越冗余），希望在不重训练模型的情况下减少生成嵌入时的计算与内存开销，以便更高效地进行检索、索引、聚类和多模态表示计算。

Method: 训练-免疫、即插即用的方法。对每个批次计算读出-前缀对齐的均值并与一个固定阈值比较以判断压缩何时发生；按读出位置从各前缀状态接收到的注意力得分排序，保留得分最高的状态并在后续层中丢弃其余状态；通过允许设置最大移除比率来自定义质量-效率权衡。

Result: 在 NarrativeQA 上使用 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B，FastE 将 decoder-backbone 的 FLOPs 减少了 40.11%，同时保留了 99.53% 的 Full Forward nDCG@10。在五个文本嵌入基准、两个模型规模和三个跨模态检索任务上均展示了通过最大移除比率可调的良好质量-效率折中。

Conclusion: FastE 能识别并利用最终读出嵌入模型中随着层深增加而出现的 prefix 冗余，通过基于读出位置的注意力排序和批量均值对齐阈值的轻量在线启发式判定，在不进行训练的前提下剪除冗余 prefix 状态，从而显著降低计算成本同时几乎不损害嵌入质量。

Abstract: In this study, we identify depth-dependent prefix redundancy in final-readout LLM embedding models, notably across representative backbones including Qwen3-Embedding and Qwen3-VL-Embedding. We find that removing prefix states is substantially more damaging in shallow layers than at greater depth, showing that prefix states become increasingly compressible as the prefix and readout states propagate through the network. To this end, we introduce FastE, a training-free, plug-and-play method. FastE uses a shared fixed threshold on batch-mean readout-prefix alignment as a lightweight online heuristic for selecting when compression occurs, and ranks prefix states by the attention scores they receive from the readout position to determine which states are retained in subsequent layers. Our evaluations demonstrate FastE's ability to substantially reduce computational costs: on NarrativeQA with Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B, it reduces decoder-backbone FLOPs by 40.11% while retaining 99.53% of Full Forward nDCG@10. Across five text embedding benchmarks, two backbone scales, and three cross-modal retrieval tasks, the quality-efficiency trade-off is directly customizable through the maximum removal ratio without retraining. We believe FastE offers practical value for scalable embedding generation in retrieval, indexing, clustering, and multimodal representation systems.

</details>


### [219] [Feyospace-v1: How the Cyber Mercury Seven Trained Frontier Cyber Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08418)
*Zongjie Li,Alan Z. W,John Nicolas J,Walter H. F,Scott Donald L,Gordon Y. P,Deke X*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以数据为中心的训练引擎和五个系统，解决环境成本、教师采样与执行限制等瓶颈，生成经验证的164k轨迹并显著提升开放权重模型的攻防能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注点在于：模型规模并非约束性瓶颈，真正限制开放权重训练的是可执行环境成本、多轮可靠监督及强力教师的可用性；因此通过数据与环境工程来降低这些成本并产生高质量训练数据。

Method: 数据引擎构建多种可重置的可执行环境（编码、漏洞、CTF、内核历史、完整利用、固件、设备支撑），并通过五个系统：Choulea（隐藏推理签名分析）、SkyReal（减少教师采样成本）、Hongzwang（绕过教师执行的API限制）、PSBreakup（恢复模型合并后弱化的能力）、Kreator（把专家干预转化为可训练的推理）来生成候选轨迹；执行后进行验证与证据审计，筛选出164,269条用于长上下文监督微调并报告了基准提升。

Result: 论文展示了一个数据中心化框架，通过五个互补系统和可重置的多种执行环境，构建并筛选经验证的长上下文轨迹（164,269 条），用于对开源权重模型进行多轮监督微调。三版检查点在CyberGym全套测试上平均提升23.76%，在汇总的CTF套件上提升10.49%；Feyospace-s1 于2026-09-01 在官方CyberGym 排行榜取得63.24% 的验证成功率并列第10，且三版检查点在同参数量级模型中名列第1。

Conclusion: 作者证明了小规模团队可以通过构建可验证的执行环境与审计流程、结合一套用于保留高质量轨迹的数据管道，训练出在攻防任务上领先的开源模型；但可复现性、评估细节和安全合规性需进一步披露与审查。

Abstract: Training capable cyber agents is often treated primarily as a problem of model scale, yet open-weight post-training is constrained more directly by the cost of executable environments, reliable multi-turn supervision, and access to strong teachers. We present a data-centric framework that addresses these bottlenecks through five complementary systems: Choulea analyzes hidden reasoning signatures, SkyReal reduces teacher-sampling cost, Hongzwang bypasses API restrictions on teacher execution, PSBreakup restores capabilities weakened by model merging, and Kreator converts expert interventions into trainable reasoning. Our data engine constructs resettable coding, vulnerability, CTF, kernel-history, full-exploit, firmware, and device-backed environments. Candidate trajectories are retained only after execution verification and evidence auditing, yielding 164,269 trajectories for long-context supervised fine-tuning. The three checkpoints improve over their starting models by an average of 23.76% on the full CyberGym suite and 10.49% across the pooled CTF suites. As of September 1, 2026, Feyospace-s1 achieves a verified success rate of 63.24% and ranks 10th on the official CyberGym leaderboard, while all three checkpoints rank 1st among models at comparable parameter scales. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end demonstration that a seven-person independent team can train open-weight models with leading agentic cyber capability.

</details>


### [220] [SRPO: Setwise Relative Policy Optimization for Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08452)
*Shengtian Yang,Ziyu Xiong,Yu Li,Yewen Li,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SRPO 将“被消费的一组输出”作为单一动作，用基数归一的集合 log-ratio 与单次裁剪的相对优势来进行策略更新，能更好地对齐训练信号与多智能体执行，提升稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习通常独立优化每个响应或轨迹，即使多个输出共同导致一次状态转移，导致优化单元与实际执行动作不一致，产生归因与稳定性问题。

Method: 将活动集合（active set）作为多智能体动作；将成员的 log-ratio 按集合基数归一后合并为一个集合比（set ratio）；为该集合分配一个相对优势（relative advantage），并对集合级别的比值一次性进行 clipping（类似 PPO 的裁剪）；该方法对不同集合大小统一处理，涵盖独立分工与联合协同。

Result: 在数学推理与多轮搜索任务上，针对固定、混合与动态路由工作流和四种模型规模，SRPO 在宏观平均上取得最佳比较结果；优化诊断显示在不同事件降维与集合大小下具有更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: SRPO 通过将一次状态转移消费的最小输出集合视为单一多智能体动作，校准了优化单元与执行动作，从而改善了多智能体 LLM 的联合决策与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language models solve complex tasks by coordinating several policies in a shared environment. However, existing reinforcement learning methods usually optimize each response or trajectory separately, even when several outputs jointly cause one state transition. Consequently, the update unit differs from the action executed by the system. To address this problem, we propose SRPO (Setwise Relative Policy Optimization), which treats the active set the minimal set of outputs consumed by one transition, as one multi-agent action. Specifically, SRPO combines member log-ratios into one cardinality-normalized set ratio, assigns one relative advantage, and clips the set once. This formulation unifies division of labor and joint co-evolution as actions with different set sizes. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and multi-turn search demonstrate one training interface for fixed, mixed, and dynamically routed workflows across four model scales, with the strongest macro-average results among the reported comparisons. Optimization diagnostics further characterize its stability under different event reductions and set sizes.

</details>


### [221] [Personalizing LLM Agent Memory Using Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08558)
*Yanhong Qian,Qingguo Meng,Shihao Ding,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin,Hanrui Wang,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bio-Memory 通过在记忆条目中加入生物特征嵌入并在检索流程中先做生物匹配再做语义排序，显著降低跨用户记忆泄露，在多基准上取得明显提升，证明生物特征是共享环境下个性化记忆检索的实用控制信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户共享代理环境中，单纯基于语义相似度的记忆检索可能把他人数据误返回给当前请求者；需引入身份感知信号以保证个性化与隐私。

Method: 在 A-Mem 基础上，为每条原子记忆附加生物特征嵌入；检索时先根据生物特征匹配筛选候选池，再对候选集进行语义相似度排序与返回。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 的 10 用户共享-agent 评测中，基于人脸的个性化在 CALFW 上平均 F1/BLEU-1 差距最高达 27.29% / 21.15%；基于掌纹的个性化在 MS_Blue 上对应差距为 25.75% / 19.22%。Across 7 个人脸基准与 10 个掌纹协议，Bio-Memory 一致区分了所有者与非所有者查询。

Conclusion: Bio-Memory 在共享代理场景下通过结合生物特征匹配与语义检索，有效区分数据所有者与非所有者查询，从而显著提升个性化记忆检索的正确性与稳健性。

Abstract: Personalized memory helps LLM agents deliver stable, tailored assistance by storing and reusing user-specific data across interactions. In multi-user scenarios, however, retrieval must consider not only semantic similarity but also whether the current requester matches the identity associated with the stored memory. We propose Bio-Memory, a biometric-aware memory architecture that conditions memory retrieval on both semantic similarity and biometric matching. Built on top of A-Mem, Bio-Memory augments each atomic memory note with a biometric embedding and uses biometric matching to form the retrieval candidate pool before semantic ranking. We evaluate Bio-Memory on LoCoMo in a 10-user shared-agent setting over 7 face benchmarks and 10 palmprint protocols. Across datasets, Bio-Memory consistently separates owner and non-owner queries. Under face-based personalization, the largest average gap reaches 27.29% / 21.15% in F1 / BLEU-1 on CALFW; under palmprint-based personalization, the corresponding gap is 25.75% / 19.22% on MS_Blue. These results support biometrics as a practical control signal for personalized memory retrieval in shared environments.

</details>


### [222] [BIO-MEMART: Biometric-Aware KV Cache Memory for Multi-User LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08566)
*Yanhong Qian,Xuanying He,Qingguo Meng,Shihao Ding,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Bio-MemArt：用生物特征模板对共享 KV-cache 做访问门控，实验证明在保持检索与效率优势的同时能高效区分授权与非授权用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 KV-cache 从服务优化演化为长时记忆外部基质，多用户共享部署下仅靠语义相关性无法判断内存块是否对当前物理用户授权，因而需要物理用户级别的访问控制。

Method: 在每个 KV 内存块附加一个归一化的生物特征模板；检索时用当前用户的生物探针先过滤共享内存池得到授权候选集；仅在该候选集内运行原 MemArt 检索与 KV 重用流程，从而实现基于生物特征的门控。

Result: 在人机长对话 QA 任务上，面部基准的平均 owner / non-owner 生物识别成功率分别为 95.71% / 0.86%，掌纹基准分别为 97.60% / 2.00%；效率上，预填充 token 从完整上下文的平均 18781.96 降至 28.57，表明生物门控保留了低 token 操作区间。

Conclusion: Bio-MemArt 在共享多用户 LLM KV-cache 中成功加入了基于生物特征的访问控制，既保留了原有的潜在空间检索与 KV 重用优势，又能有效区分物理用户以防止非授权复用。

Abstract: KV cache is evolving from a serving optimization into an external memory substrate for long-term LLM agents. In a shared multi-user deployment, however, reusable KV blocks introduce a missing access-control question: semantic relevance alone cannot determine whether a memory block is authorized for the current physical user. We propose Bio-MemArt, a biometric-aware KV-cache memory framework for multi-user LLM agents. Bio-MemArt attaches a normalized biometric template to each stored KV memory block, filters the shared memory pool with the current user's biometric probe, and then runs the original MemArt retrieval and KV reuse pipeline only inside the authorized candidate pool. This design preserves latent-space retrieval, direct cache reuse, and decoupled position encoding while adding physical-user access control to shared KV memory. We evaluate Bio-MemArt under Owner and Non-owner query conditions on long-term dialogue QA with face and palmprint benchmarks. Across face benchmarks, the average owner and non-owner biometric success rates are 95.71% and 0.86%; across palmprint benchmarks, they are 97.60% and 2.00%. In the efficiency study, average prefill tokens drop from 18,781.96 under full-context prompting to 28.57 with Bio-MemArt, showing that biometric gating preserves the low-token operating regime of KV-cache memory.

</details>


### [223] [AgentGrad: Intervention-guided Prompt Optimization for Multi Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08572)
*Jaewon Chu,Jinwoo Seo,Jaewon Cho,Jeehye Na,Yunyang Xiong,Youngdae Kim,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentGrad通过逐个干预找出负责失败的智能体并对其输出进行细粒度监督，再通过语义聚类抽象文本梯度，解决了现有方法在梯度提取与聚合上的缺陷，从而在性能与效率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本梯度的MAS提示优化存在两方面问题：在梯度提取阶段，经常在未验证是否可行的情况下选择目标提示，且缺乏对单个智能体中间输出的层级监督；在梯度聚合阶段，随机拼接或混合各梯度，容易把不相关的故障模式混合，导致学习到的提示泛化性差。

Method: 提出两大核心机制：1) 顺序干预（sequential intervention）：对失败样例逐个修改各智能体行为，识别“目标智能体”（修改该智能体即可修复失败），并将其修改后输出作为该智能体层面的监督以提取细粒度文本梯度；2) 语义文本梯度抽象（semantic textual gradient abstraction）：对提取到的文本梯度按语义相似性进行聚类，并从每个簇抽象出通用的校正梯度，避免将不相关的失败模式混合在一起。

Result: 在五个多智能体系统基准上，AgentGrad取得了SOTA性能，并在优化效率上显著优于对比方法，平均墙钟时间减少约2.5倍；另外通过目标智能体定位与语义聚类提高了梯度质量与泛化性。

Conclusion: AgentGrad通过逐次干预与语义文本梯度抽象，针对多智能体系统（MAS）提示优化中的梯度提取与聚合两大瓶颈提出解决方案，从而提升了提示优化的有效性与通用性。实验证明在五个MAS基准上达到最先进性能，并比次优基线平均缩短2.5倍的墙钟时间。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) achieve strong performance by employing specialized multiple agents, yet their performance depends on the prompt design of each agent. For MAS prompt optimization, textual gradient methods that guide prompt updates using natural-language feedback have emerged as a leading paradigm. In this paper, we identify limitations in two stages of existing textual gradient approaches: gradient extraction and gradient aggregation.
