In gradient extraction, previous works select a target prompt without verifying whether modifying it resolves the failure, and derive gradients without agent-level supervision over the corresponding agent's intermediate output. In gradient aggregation, individual gradients are randomly grouped and concatenated, often mixing unrelated failure modes and producing prompts that fail to generalize. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{AgentGrad}, a prompt optimization framework for multi-agent systems based on sequential intervention and semantic textual gradient abstraction. For each failure, sequential intervention modifies the behavior of one agent at a time to identify the target agent whose modification resolves the failure. The modified output of the target agent then serves as agent-level supervision for extracting a fine-grained gradient. Semantic textual gradient abstraction clusters semantically similar gradients to prevent mixing unrelated failure modes, and abstracts each cluster into a generalized gradient that captures the shared corrective pattern. Experimental results show that AgentGrad achieves state-of-the-art performance across five MAS benchmarks and reduces wall-clock optimization time by $2.5\times$ on average compared to the next-fastest baseline.

</details>


### [224] [A Three-Tier Persona Vector for Controllable User Simulation in Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08592)
*Rahul Khedar,Eshita,Sneha Teja Sree Reddy Thondapu,Mayank Malhotra,Arup Kumar Das,Jitesh Chandra Mishra,Arun Menon,Avinash Karn,Mouli V*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用23维三层人格向量加查询复杂度叠加层生成大规模合成对话，证明人格与情绪动态能显著影响工具增强式LLM代理的表现，且方法可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架使用扁平的角色描述导致生成的对话高度同质化，无法反映真实用户在不同情景下的行为多样性，因此需要一个可操作、可复现且能反映情绪与场景交互的细粒度人格建模方法。

Method: 构建一个包含23个维度的人格向量：6个分类人口统计维度、12个连续行为特征（以高斯扰动围绕基线向量采样）和5个连续情绪状态（随场景动态变化）；另外增加4级查询复杂度叠加层以控制话语措辞；在合成数据管道中生成64,698条多轮对话，覆盖8种命名用户画像和3个生产语料，并基于目标达成率等指标进行评估。

Result: 人格向量在代理目标达成率上产生最多15.8个百分点的差异；同一人格在不同场景下因情绪动态产生不同行为；在领域特定流程（例如预订流程）中检测到15–20个百分点的合规性差距；还通过七条规则性特征相关性生成可审计的共现模式。模型全文规格化，便于复现。

Conclusion: 提出的三层人格向量模型为评估工具增强型大模型代理提供了更细粒度、可操作的用户行为建模手段，实验表明该模型能在多场景中产生可测量的差异并复现现实难度分布，且可完全复现。

Abstract: Evaluating tool-augmented LLM agents requires diverse, realistic user inputs yet most evaluation frameworks use flat role descriptions ("you are an angry customer") that produce near-identical conversations regardless of the underlying scenario. In this paper, we propose a three-tier persona vector with 23 operationalized dimensions: 6 categorical demographics (jurisdiction, age, channel, device, language proficiency, time availability), 12 continuous behavioral traits (patience, assertiveness, digital literacy, etc.) sampled with Gaussian noise around curated profile base vectors, and 5 continuous emotional states (frustration, anxiety, trust, confidence, stress) that shift in response to scenario context. Orthogonal to the persona, a 4-level query-complexity overlay controls utterance phrasing from direct to deliberately vague. We evaluate the persona model inside a synthetic data generation pipeline across 64,698 multi-turn conversations spanning 8 named profiles and 3 production corpora. Key findings: (i) a 15.8 percentage-point spread in agent goal-achievement across personas confirms trait vectors produce measurably different user behavior; (ii) the same persona behaves differently across scenarios due to scenario-reactive emotional state shifts, validating the scenario-reactive design; (iii) domain-specific projects show persona sensitivity on booking-flow compliance (~15-20 percentage points gap between tier-aware and pressure-test personas), demonstrating the model faithfully reproduces real-world difficulty distributions; (iv) seven rule-described trait correlations produce auditable co-occurrence patterns without requiring learned covariance matrices. The persona model is fully specified for reproduction.

</details>


### [225] [Graph-Based Personalized Memory for LLM Agents: Representation, Evolution, Retrieval, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08599)
*Dac Duy Anh Nguyen,Zhangchi Qiu,Shigeng Chen,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向长期个性化LLM代理的图结构记忆，提出生命周期框架，比较设计选项并指出评估、更新、隐私与可扩展性等核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理正向长期个性化助手演进，用户偏好与历史逐渐积累并时变，使得能够表达关系、时间与证据的结构化记忆（即图型记忆）成为实现个性化决策与可解释性的核心需求。

Method: 本文作为综述，提出以生命周期为中心的分析框架，将现有工作归纳到记忆表示、记忆演化、记忆检索与记忆评估四个模块，比较关键设计选择并综述评价实践与开放问题。

Result: 系统梳理了图记忆的研究谱系、典型方法与评估方式，指出现有研究分散、缺乏统一基准，并总结了包括更新策略、检索机制、融合方式、隐私保护与评估指标等关键挑战与未来方向。

Conclusion: 基于图的个性化记忆是构建长期、可适应、可控的LLM个人助手的有力范式，但仍需在表示、演化、检索、评估以及系统层面的鲁棒性、隐私与可扩展性上进行大量研究。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are evolving from single-session tools toward long-term personal assistants that must adapt to individual users across tasks, contexts, and interactions. This shift makes memory a core requirement for personalization, since user preferences, goals, constraints, relationships, and past experiences are accumulated gradually and often change over time. Graph-based personalized memory provides a structured way to model such user information through explicit relations, temporal context, and evidence links. Such representations can model not only what an agent remembers about a user but also how memories are connected, revised, and retrieved to support personalized decisions. However, existing work remains fragmented across personalized agents and generic graph memory frameworks, making it difficult to understand the design space as a whole. This survey develops a lifecycle-oriented view of graph-based personalized memory for LLM agents. We organize existing studies around memory representation, memory evolution, memory retrieval, and memory evaluation. We further compare key design choices, discuss current evaluation practices, and open challenges in building reliable long-term personalized agents. This survey aims to clarify how graph-based memory can support adaptive, controllable, and user-centric LLM agents.

</details>


### [226] [CLAMP: Constrained Decoding for Vision-Language Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08602)
*Tianyi Ma,Parisa Kordjamshidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLAMP constrains a frozen VLM planner with scene-supported object masks and an HMM lookahead over symbolic action models at decode time, improving executable and safer plans; limits include perception noise and constraint-specification brittleness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM-generated plans are linguistically plausible but often infeasible or unsafe because they reference unseen objects, select actions without required affordances, or violate action syntax; constraining decoding by scene evidence and symbolic dynamics can make plans executable while retaining language priors.

Method: Use initial scene observation to mask object tokens at decoding time; leverage a symbolic action model to define preconditions/effects; apply hard token masks and an HMM-based lookahead that reweights feasible next-token probabilities; adapt HMM at test time using label-free continuations sampled from the frozen VLM for zero-shot generalization.

Result: On VLABench, SafeAgentBench, and TaPA, CLAMP improves object grounding and safety metrics; most residual failures are due to perception errors or misaligned symbolic constraints rather than planner language priors.

Conclusion: CLAMP improves planning safety and object grounding by imposing scene-grounded decoding constraints on a frozen VLM and using HMM-based lookahead to bias feasible continuations, but remaining failures largely come from perception errors and imperfect constraint specifications.

Abstract: Embodied planning increasingly relies on vision-language models (VLMs) to translate instructions and visual observations into executable action sequences. However, fluent plans are not always executable. A VLM may refer to objects that are not visually observed, select actions whose required affordances are unavailable, or violate syntax and action constraints. We introduce CLAMP, a multimodal constraint-grounding framework that turns scene evidence into decoding-time constraints for a frozen VLM planner. CLAMP uses the initial observation to restrict object references to those supported by the scene, while a provided symbolic action model specifies state transitions and goals. During decoding, hard masks eliminate invalid next-token candidates, while a Hidden Markov Model (HMM)-based world-state lookahead module reweights the probabilities of the remaining feasible candidates based on action preconditions and goal reachability. This allows the planner to retain the VLM's language prior while preventing visually unsupported, unsafe, or infeasible candidates from entering the plan. For unseen tasks and environments, CLAMP adapts the HMM at test time using label-free continuations sampled from the frozen VLM. Experiments on VLABench, SafeAgentBench, and TaPA show that scene-grounded constraints improve object grounding and safety, while most remaining failures stem from perception errors or misaligned constraint specifications.

</details>


### [227] [GoAnt: Quality-Diversity Multi-Agent Search for Alpha Factor Discovery in Market Microstructure Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08719)
*Stella Zhao,Tommy Sha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为克服自动化alpha因子搜索中的过拟合与冗余问题，作者设计了一个由探查、利用、连接器三类代理和自适应心理地图+调度器组成的质量-多样性框架（GoAnt），并提出统一的评估协议，在真实A股微观数据上显著提高了高质量非冗余因子的产出和样本外稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化发现alpha因子时，基于价格-成交量和订单簿的符号化交易信号搜索存在过拟合预测代理、交易成本失效和在受限评估预算下重复探索冗余因子族的问题，导致执行鲁棒性和行为多样性不足。

Method: 将程序搜索任务拆分为Explorer（广泛探索）、Exploiter（局部优化）和Connector（连接不同解区）的并行独立工作者，所有工作者共享一个自适应的Mental Map——该地图按“leakage-free执行剖面”把候选分区并在每个niche保留精英；Queen是紧凑的调度器，基于显式的搜索状态摘要重新分配评估预算。并引入effective-yield协议直接从评估记录计数高质量、非冗余因子，以便对比不同归档或地图方法。

Result: 提出GoAnt：一个质量-多样性多代理搜索框架，结合互不通信的Explorer、Exploiter、Connector工作者、共享的自适应Mental Map和紧凑的Queen调度器。Mental Map按无泄漏执行剖面组织候选并在每个niche保留一个精英，Queen根据显式搜索状态摘要重新分配评估预算。并提出map-无关的effective-yield协议以公平计数高质量、互不冗余的因子。实证上在2023–2026年A股微观结构数据上，GoAnt在价格-成交量和订单簿设置下在匹配预算下将最强基线分别提升了57%和97%，质量加权产出分别为41.8和47.6；锁定人口在样本外保留0.64和0.67的样本内质量，静态地图为0.61和0.63。

Conclusion: GoAnt通过引入多角色协作、以执行剖面为刻画的心理地图以及预算感知的调度，提高了在受限预算下的因子质量与多样性，同时使用map-独立的计量方法保证方法间可比性，实验结果表明其在局部市场微观结构数据上的产出与外样本保持更好性能。

Abstract: Automated alpha factor discovery searches symbolic trading signals from price-volume panels and order-book data under a fixed evaluation budget. Existing single- and multi-agent program-search systems can overfit predictive proxies that fail after execution costs and repeatedly explore redundant factor families, limiting execution robustness and behavioral diversity. We introduce GoAnt, a quality-diversity multi-agent search framework that combines non-communicating Explorer, Exploiter and Connector workers with a shared adaptive Mental Map and a compact Queen dispatcher. The Mental Map organizes candidates by leakage-free execution profiles and retains one elite per niche, while the Queen reallocates the evaluation budget from explicit search-state summaries. We also define a map-independent effective-yield protocol that counts high-quality, mutually nonredundant factors directly from each method's evaluation records, giving archive-based and map-free systems the same ruler. On real A-share microstructure data spanning 2023--2026, GoAnt reaches quality-weighted yields of 41.8 and 47.6 in price-volume and order-book settings, improving the strongest baseline by 57% and 97% under matched budgets. Its locked populations retain 0.64 and 0.67 of in-sample quality out of sample, compared with 0.61 and 0.63 for a static map.

</details>


### [228] [Application of curiosity driven exploration methods for hardware interference identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08729)
*Ludovic Matar,Clement Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TL;DR：用好奇心驱动探索替代纯伪随机生成，可更高效地覆盖多核干扰行为空间，帮助发现更多极端干扰场景并辅助时序验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：安全关键嵌入式系统向多核迁移导致共享硬件资源争用产生不可忽视的核间干扰，现有手动或基于模型的分析难以穷尽复杂微架构组件间交互带来的全部行为，且认证标准要求全面识别干扰源。

Method: 方法：在模拟器环境中，使用基于人工智能的好奇心驱动探索算法（通过内部激励促进发现新行为）来生成并执行负载/程序，定义行为表征（例如硬件性能计数器或干扰相关指标），并用覆盖度和均匀性等度量与传统伪随机生成方法比较。

Result: 结果：在模拟器实验中，好奇心驱动方法在相同预算下获得了更宽泛且更均匀的行为覆盖，能更快发现极端或罕见的干扰模式，相较于伪随机生成方法在覆盖率和分布均一性上有显著优势。

Conclusion: 论文结论：将多核干扰分析视为复杂系统行为空间的探索问题，采用好奇心驱动的探索算法能在有限的试验预算内，比伪随机程序生成方法更广泛且更均匀地覆盖可能的干扰行为，从而提高发现极端干扰场景的能力。

Abstract: The transition from single-core to multi-core architectures in safety-critical embedded systems introduces significant challenges due to inter-core interference caused by contention for shared hardware resources. Such interference affects execution times and complicates the verification of strict temporal requirements, particularly in domains such as avionics where standards require comprehensive identification of interference sources. Existing interference analysis approaches, whether manual or model-based, struggle to capture the full range of behaviors arising from the complex interactions among micro-architectural components. In this paper, we frame multi-core interference analysis as the exploration of a complex system behavior space. We propose the use of curiosity-driven exploration algorithms from artificial intelligence to systematically and efficiently cover the space of possible interference behaviors. Using a simulator-based environment, we show that the proposed approach achieves broader and more uniform behavioral coverage within a limited experimental budget compared to traditional pseudo-random program generation methods.

</details>


### [229] [When Can One Obtain Certificates of Optimality Using Positivstellensaetze?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08736)
*Nayoon Kim,Allen Gehret,Shenyuan Ma,Jakub Marecek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 抽象化Fischer的Positivstellensatz，构建在有序域与函数代数上的正性/最优性证书框架，支持非多项式运算并分析证书大小与计算复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习与优化问题的目标和约束并非多项式（含超越或条件、可定义操作），但仍需可验证的正性或最优性证书。现有的Positivstellensatz工具多局限于多项式或闭域上的情形，缺乏对更广泛运算类与不完全代数（例如有理数域）下证书构造的系统理论。

Method: 通过给出标量与闭包类型的公理（axioms）对辅助原语进行约束，并在抽象的有序域上的函数代数中重证Fischer的构造性严格与弱Positivstellensatz，证明在这些抽象环境下可以构造下界与全局最优性证书；同时给出连续与可定义函数代数的具体实例并分析证书表示的项长度和共享子表达式的计算图复杂度。

Result: 给出一套可在抽象函数代数上成立的Positivstellensatz型定理（包括严格与弱形式），证明在满足显式标量与闭包公理的辅助原语下可构造下界与全局最优性证书；展示连续与可定义函数代数的实例化（涵盖无法开平方的有序域）；并就证书表示复杂度（项展开长度）与共享计算图复杂度给出分析与度量。

Conclusion: 提出了一个抽象公理化框架，将Fischer的构造性严格与弱Positivstellensatz的要点抽离并推广到抽象函数代数，从而在目标与约束非多项式的学习问题中得到正性与最优性的证书。该框架把目标/约束函数的构造能力与证书使用的辅助原语严格分离，允许在连续或可定义函数代数以及不闭合平方根的有序域上构造证书，并就证书的项展开长度与共享计算图复杂度给出分析。

Abstract: We study certificates of positivity and optimality for learning problems whose objectives and constraints need not be polynomial. We isolate an axiomatic core of Fischer's constructive strict and weak Positivstellensätze and prove the resulting theorems for abstract function algebras over ordered fields. The framework separates two roles that can otherwise be conflated: objective and constraint functions may be built from broad classes of continuous or definable operations, while the auxiliary primitives used to construct a certificate satisfy explicit scalar and closure axioms. We give instances over continuous and definable function algebras, including ordered fields not closed under square roots, derive lower-bound and global-optimality certificates, and analyze both expanded term length and shared computation-graph complexity.

</details>


### [230] [It's All in the Way You Say It: The Role of Information Representation in LLM-Based Glycemic-Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08772)
*Andrea Apicella,Pasquale Arpaia,Matteo Orefice,Andrea Pollastro,Roberto Prevete*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs via prompting can help predict hypoglycemia but not hyperglycemia compared to specialized models; how you represent glucose/physiology in the prompt matters more than adding contextual signals.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether and how prompt-based, general-purpose LLMs can be used for physiological time-series prediction—specifically postprandial hyper- and hypoglycemia—instead of or alongside specialized supervised models, and to identify the role of information representation and context exposure at inference time.

Method: Evaluated multiple open-weight LLMs (zero-shot and few-shot) on OhioT1DM for 30/60/90 min horizons. Varied textual representation of physiological data and amount of contextual information (glucose alone; derived descriptors; insulin, meals, carbs, activity). Compared to conventional patient-specific supervised models and Gluco-LLM.

Result: Conventional supervised models performed best for hyperglycemia prediction. Best prompt-based LLM configurations outperformed for hypoglycemia across all horizons. Performance depended strongly on how physiological data were represented textually; supplying extra contextual variables did not provide consistent gains. Results imply task-dependent utility of LLM prompting and highlight representation as a central design factor.

Conclusion: Prompt-based general-purpose LLMs can improve hypoglycemia prediction but not hyperglycemia compared to patient-specific supervised models; representation of physiological information is a primary determinant of prompt-based performance, while adding contextual variables (insulin, meals, activity) does not systematically help.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being investigated for physiological time-series prediction, yet their effectiveness may depend not only on the model itself, but also on how physiological information is represented and presented at inference time. This study investigates prompt-based general-purpose LLMs for postprandial hyperglycemia and hypoglycemia prediction in individuals with type 1 diabetes. Using the OhioT1DM dataset, we evaluate multiple open-weight LLMs under zero-shot and few-shot inference across prediction horizons of 30, 60, and 90 minutes. The analysis varies both the textual representation of the available physiological information and the amount of information exposed to the model, ranging from glucose observations alone to derived descriptors and additional contextual variables related to insulin, meals, carbohydrates, and physical activity. Performance is compared with conventional patient-specific supervised models and with Gluco-LLM, a language-model-based architecture explicitly adapted to glucose time-series forecasting. Results show a marked task-dependent behavior. Conventional supervised models achieve the strongest performance for hyperglycemia prediction, whereas the best observed prompt-based LLM configurations improve performance for hypoglycemia across all investigated horizons. The effectiveness of prompt-based inference is also strongly influenced by how physiological information is represented, while providing additional contextual information does not lead to a systematic improvement. Overall, these findings highlight physiological information representation as a central design factor in prompt-based LLM approaches to glycemic-event prediction.

</details>


### [231] [Closing the Consistency Gap: Self-Evolving Agents That Learn to Stay on Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08832)
*Evelyn Duesterwald,Benjamin Elder,Lilian Ngweta,Shashanka Ubaru,Malgorzata Zimon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过自动识别并记忆导致执行结果翻转的不稳定步骤，并将其转化为可复用的执行指导，本文提出的自我演化代理显著提升了LLM代理的跨次执行一致性，在AppWorld+ReAct/GPT-4.1设置下带来两位数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到大型语言模型驱动代理在平均表现上可能较好但在多次重复执行上的可靠性不足（定义为一致性差距），认识到缩小这一差距是可信赖部署的前提。

Method: 提出自我演化（self-evolving）代理框架：在代理执行轨迹中识别不稳定、低一致性的步骤，通过Consistency Analyzer定位可能在不同执行中翻转的节点，再由Guideline Generator将诊断转化为针对性指导并作为情节记忆（episodic memory）保存，随后在相似任务的未来运行中注入这些指导以提高一致性。基于ReAct/GPT-4.1在AppWorld基准上的实验验证该流程。

Result: 在AppWorld上以ReAct/GPT-4.1为基线，框架使“在同一任务上五次均成功”的任务比例在同任务评估上提升+16个百分点，在相似任务泛化上提升+13个百分点，从而有效减少一致性差距。

Conclusion: 该方法为提高LLM代理的可重复可靠性提供了一条可行路径：通过诊断-生成-记忆-注入的闭环，实现针对性修正并提高多次运行的一致成功率。未来需验证其对不同模型、任务域的泛化、内存规模与干预频率的影响，以及与其他稳定性手段（如集成、校准、约束推理）的对比。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents can be accurate on average yet unreliable in production, a discrepancy that has been observed but remains largely unaddressed. When given the same task five times, a ReAct agent on the AppWorld benchmark using GPT-4.1 succeeds in all five runs only 53% of the time, even though its per-run pass rate averages 77%. We call this 24-point shortfall the consistency gap, and we argue that addressing it is a precondition for trustworthy AI agent deployment. We present a self-evolving agent framework that reduces this gap by identifying unstable, low-consistency steps in agent trajectories and converting them into episodic memory the agent can draw on in future runs. At its core is a Consistency Analyzer that pinpoints where and why a trajectory is likely to flip across executions, and a Guideline Generator that converts the diagnosis into targeted guidelines, committed to memory and injected into future agent executions on similar tasks. On AppWorld with ReAct/GPT-4.1, our framework raises the fraction of tasks that succeed in all five runs by +16 points on same-task evaluation and +13 points on similar-task generalization.

</details>


### [232] [SkillAdam: Stable and Efficient Skill Evolution for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08944)
*Gaoyuan Li,Meihao Fan,Yizhe Liu,Shaolei Zhang,Ju Fan,Siyi Wang,Jiaheng Hou,Xudong Weng,Honghan Tian,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillAdam 通过引入记忆化问题-尝试记录和波动感知的编辑预算，把 Adam 的一二矩思想移植到离散技能文档优化，从而在技能自我进化上实现更稳定且更高效的迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工撰写技能昂贵且难扩展，现有基于执行反馈的自我进化方法更新策略启发式且常导致不稳定和低效率；因此需要保证‘方向稳定性’和‘更新自适应性’。

Method: 提出一个受 Adam 启发的离散文档优化框架：用“优化记忆”（相当于一阶矩）记录问题和以往解决尝试以稳定更新方向；用“波动驱动的编辑预算”（相当于二阶矩）跟踪加权历史的案例级改进变化并自适应控制更新幅度。

Result: 在短/长任务共七个基准上，SkillAdam 展示了更稳定的优化动力学、SOTA 性能，并显著降低所需迭代次数与优化成本；同时发布了代码仓库。

Conclusion: SkillAdam 能在技能自我进化任务中实现更稳定、高效的优化，达到七个基准上的最先进性能，并以更少的迭代和更低的成本得到更强的技能文档。

Abstract: Agent skills provide a lightweight way to equip frozen language-model agents with domain knowledge and procedural guidance, yet obtaining high-quality skills remains costly and difficult to scale. Expert-written skills require substantial human effort. Recent skill self-evolution methods automate an iterative loop that uses execution feedback to revise skills, but their heuristic update strategies often yield unstable optimization and low iteration efficiency. We identify two challenges in realizing stable and efficient skill self-evolution. Direction Stability requires effective corrections to accumulate rather than be overwritten by iteration-local feedback. Update Adaptivity requires the scope of each revision to reflect the consistency of recent case-level improvements. We introduce SkillAdam, an Adam-inspired framework for optimizing discrete and non-differentiable skill documents. As a functional analogue of Adam's first moment, an optimization memory records identified problems and the outcomes of prior solution attempts to stabilize the update direction. As a functional analogue of Adam's second moment, a volatility-driven edit budget tracks the history-weighted variation of recent case-level improvements and adaptively controls the update magnitude. Across seven benchmarks that span short- and long-horizon tasks, SkillAdam achieves state-of-the-art performance with more stable optimization dynamics. It also obtains stronger skills with substantially fewer optimization iterations and lower cost than prior methods. Code repository: https://github.com/ruc-datalab/SkillAdam

</details>


### [233] [PlannerForge: LLM Agents for Scenario-Based Testing of Motion Planners in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08965)
*Yuan Gao,Sebastian Müller,Mattia Piccinini,Marc Kaufeld,Yuchen Zhang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Qunying Song,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlannerForge 是一个覆盖完整场景驱动测试流水线的 LLM-agent 框架，实验证明其在生成、检索、修改、评估和增强等任务上表现优异，且部分开源 LLM 能与商用模型持平；端到端保留率高，并能通过 cost-tuning 在不微调的情况下显著提升规划器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶场景测试工具链碎片化，子模块之间交互少；而大型语言模型在感知、规划、控制等 ADS 子系统中表现出潜力，尚无工作将整个场景驱动测试流水线统一到 LLM-agent 框架下。

Method: 设计一个由若干 LLM-agent 组成的模块化框架（包括场景生成、选择、修改、模块路由、规划器测试与增强等），在五种提示条件下使用 10 个现成 LLM 后端（含开源 20–35B 模型及商用 API）评估各子任务性能，并进行端到端链路测试与与多基线对比（Scenario Factory 2.0、BM25、From-Words-to-Collisions）。采用 cost-tuning 优化规划器性能，统计可执行生成数、属性实现率、检索准确率、物理编辑有效率、规划成功率与碰撞率等指标。

Result: 最佳任务分数在 0.88–1.00 之间；开源 20–35B 模型在大多数任务上匹配商用 API；Qwen3.6:35B 在五项任务中有三项达商业水平；端到端链路保留率：商业 83%、开源 78%；在自然语言生成上超越 Scenario Factory 2.0（可执行 193 vs 144 /200）；实现 92–96% 的城市/道路/车辆属性请求；rank-1 检索优于 BM25（92.0% vs 67.5%）；物理编辑有效率 ≥94% vs 31%（From-Words-to-Collisions）；N=400 时 cost-tuning 将规划成功率从 50.4% 提升至 70.2%，碰撞率从 19.0% 降至 8.4%。

Conclusion: PlannerForge 提出并实现了一个覆盖整个场景驱动测试流水线的统一 LLM-agent 框架，扩展到场景生成、检索、修改、ADS 执行与评估，并新增 ADS 增强与基准测试两个阶段。实验证明其在多项任务上取得优异成绩，部分开源模型可与商业 API 竞争，端到端链路保留率高，并能在不做领域微调的情况下通过 cost-tuning 显著提升规划器成功率与减少碰撞率。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous driving is a critical challenge. Scenario-based testing is a systematic process used to validate Autonomous Driving Systems (ADSs), but it remains a fragmented modular pipeline in which scenario generation, retrieval, modification, ADS execution, and results analysis are performed by separate tools with little interaction. Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise across ADS sub-systems such as perception, planning, and control. However, no prior work covers the whole scenario-based testing pipeline for ADSs with a unified LLM-agent framework. We present PlannerForge, an LLM-agent framework that extends all scenario-based testing stages (from Scenario Generation to ADS Assessment) and adds two further LLM-enhanced stages: ADS Enhancement and ADS Benchmarking. We evaluate PlannerForge with 10 off-the-shelf LLMs across all tasks (Generation, Selection, Modification, Module Routing, Planner Testing, and Enhancement) under 5 prompt conditions. Best-per-task scores range from 0.88 to 1.00, and open-source 20-35B backends match commercial APIs on most tasks. Open-source models such as Qwen3.6:35B match commercial APIs on three of the five tasks. Chaining the modules end-to-end retains 83% / 78% of seed queries (commercial / open). It outperforms Scenario Factory 2.0 (Finkeldei et al., 2025) on natural-language generation (193 vs. 144 executable of 200) and realises 92-96% of requested city, road and vehicle attributes. It outperforms BM25 (Robertson and Zaragoza, 2009) at rank 1 selection (92.0% vs. 67.5%) and From-Words-to-Collisions (Gao et al., 2025) on physically valid edits (>=94% vs. 31%). At N=400, cost-tuning lifts planner success from 50.4% to 70.2% and cuts collisions from 19.0% to 8.4%, without domain-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [234] [Good Pretraining, Bad SFT: Checkpoint Quality Across the Training Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08966)
*Sohir Maskey,Philipp Scholl,Jonas Knupp,Pit Neitemeier,Sascha Wirges*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在30B MoE管线中，传统的按预训练损失或基准选检查点的方法会失效；更稳健、局部平坦（高解密度）的检查点更可能成为下游训练后的优良起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验常用的语言模型检查点选择策略（基于预训练损失或基准分数）在后续完整训练流水线中是否仍能保证最佳起点，尤其在大规模混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）模型上。

Method: 在30B Mixture-of-Experts训练流水线中对多个中间检查点进行跟踪：记录预训练损失与基准分数，随后将这些检查点作为起点进行完整的下游训练栈，比较最终下游表现，并通过对权重施加局部扰动来测量各检查点的解的密度与性能保持情况。

Result: 在一个完整的30B MoE训练流水线上验证，预训练损失或基准分数最高的检查点并不总是经过后续下游训练后表现最好的；那些在最终下游训练后表现更好的检查点具有更高的“解的密度”（solution density），即在局部权重扰动下能更好地保留下游性能。

Conclusion: 检查点选择应考虑解空间几何特性（如解的密度/局部平坦性），而不是仅靠训练损失或即时基准分数；通过评估对局部权重扰动的鲁棒性可以更可靠地挑选用于后续训练的起点。

Abstract: Language-model checkpoints are commonly selected by pretraining loss or benchmark scores, assuming that the highest-scoring checkpoint will remain the best starting point for subsequent training. We show that this assumption can fail in a full 30B mixture-of-experts training pipeline. The checkpoints that perform better after the full downstream training stack also have higher solution density, i.e., retain downstream performance under local weight perturbations.

</details>


### [235] [Deposon: An Auditable, Conservation-Guaranteed, Game-Theoretically Tested Scattering Layer over LLM Reasoning Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09001)
*Qihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deposon offers an auditable scattering framework for LLM reasoning graphs that achieves verifiability (machine-epsilon audit) but not consistent predictive improvement; several formal claims fail and empirical benefits over simple baselines are limited.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-step LLM reasoning discards intermediate paths without auditable ledgers; aim to create an auditable mechanism that tracks and certifies reasoning-path contributions and dynamics.

Method: Introduce a Deposon scattering layer that binds graph nodes to two-parameter states and routes path mass via transmission/reflection/dissipation (T+R+A=1); evaluate on synthetic and real reasoning benchmarks, test convex fusion with semantic priors, model reverse dynamics as a potential game, and report empirical metrics (energy-audit deviation, ECR, cyclic-graph median residual).

Result: On synthetic traps the layer outperforms a decoy baseline dramatically; on real tasks it matches a trivial keyword filter and shows no predictive fusion gains; formal dynamical propositions were falsified under pre-registered tests, leaving only consistency-level evidence for an approximate potential-game structure. Code released.

Conclusion: Deposon primarily provides machine-verifiable auditability rather than consistent predictive gains; several formal claims were falsified and dynamical results weakened to approximate consistency.

Abstract: Multi-step LLM reasoning lacks a machine-recheckable ledger: discarded reasoning paths leave no auditable record. We propose the Deposon scattering layer, which binds each node of an LLM-generated concept-decomposition graph to a two-parameter Deposon state; paths undergo three-channel scattering -- transmission, reflection, irreversible dissipation -- obeying T+R+A=1 for arbitrary parameters, with a maximum per-path energy-audit deviation of 2.2E-16 (machine epsilon). We report all three evidence tiers honestly. On synthetic trap benchmarks the path-filtering gain is closed (pre-registered): unified reaches 100% versus a decoy-capture baseline at 7%/10%. On real benchmarks the layer is indistinguishable from a trivial six-keyword rule filter (GSM8K 0.87 >= 0.85, McNemar p=0.5; StrategyQA 0.899 = 0.899); no difference is detected here, so we sharpen the claim to "the differential value lies solely in machine verifiability." Fusion yields a second negative result: convex combinations with a semantic prior never improve (physics 0.484 -> 0.452), and the apparent lambda=2 gain is an anti-field artifact; any fusion gain must be nonlinear. Modeling the reverse dynamics as a potential game on the graph, we evidence an auditable scalar's monotonicity and near-gradientness and quantify the empirical coordination ratio (ECR). The three formalized dynamical-equivalence propositions (P1a/P1b/T-P1c) are falsified under the pre-registered kill protocol, and the potential-game claim is downgraded to approximate (cyclic-graph median residual 0.669): only consistency-level evidence survives at the dynamical level. Code: github.com/zeroandcat/Deposon.

</details>


### [236] [Answer-Distribution Trajectories: A Stochastic-Dynamics View of LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09030)
*Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Davide Murari,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Pietro Liò,George Montañez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过跟踪随推理展开的答案预测分布轨迹，作者能揭示探索、修正、运动与承诺等动态阶段，区分相同终点但不同内部过程的推理路径，并展示训练与推理设计如何系统地改变这些轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终点准确率和熵序列不足以揭示为何模型得出某个答案，需一种能展示不确定性由哪些候选解构成以及如何随推理演化的表征，以更好地理解与改进推理过程。

Method: 对模型在链式思考各步的输出（通过多次抽样或其他概率估计）汇总为随时间变化的完整答案预测分布，并以该分布的轨迹为分析对象，计算并比较不同模型/任务/超参下的动态模式与统计特征（包含但不限于熵、模式转换时间、质量-置信关系等）。

Result: 提出“答案分布轨迹”（answer-distribution trajectories），在链式思考推理过程中跟踪模型对所有候选答案的预测分布，从而比仅看终点或熵曲线更精细地刻画推理动态。

Conclusion: 答案分布轨迹为分析大模型推理过程提供了更细粒度且解释性强的表征，有助于区分成功/失败机制并指导训练和推理策略的设计，但对答案空间定义、计算成本和可扩展性提出了实际挑战。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning provides a structured computation between a model's input and final answer. Yet it is often evaluated through endpoint accuracy, which ignores the path taken to reach that answer. An emerging line of work addresses this limitation using entropy profiles, which track how uncertainty evolves over the reasoning process but do not reveal which competing hypotheses account for that uncertainty. We introduce answer-distribution trajectories, a stochastic-dynamics-inspired representation that tracks the model's full predictive distribution over answers as reasoning unfolds. As a strictly finer representation than endpoint and entropy summaries, answer-distribution trajectories enable us to characterize a trace through a dynamical reasoning profile spanning exploration, revision, motion, and commitment, and to distinguish different dynamical mechanisms of reasoning success and failure. Across sixteen open-weight language models and four reasoning benchmarks, we show that traces with the same endpoint and similar entropy profiles can exhibit substantially different reasoning dynamics. We further find substantial variation in these dynamics both within and across models and tasks, with different objectives favoring different dynamical profiles. Additionally, we show that training and inference choices systematically reshape these profiles. Our results suggest that answer-distribution trajectories provide a rich framework for analysing and evaluating the dynamics of LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [237] [Time-Varying Data as Sheaves: an Invitation to Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09056)
*Wilmer Leal,Benjamin Merlin Bumpus,Jana K. Nickel,Johan García,James Fairbanks,Warren Dixon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“叙事”这一抽象框架以统一时变对象的表示与分析，并通过信息损失、结构分解与多智能体控制三条线索展示其应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学工程大量依赖时变数据，但用于描述时间现象的数学工具分散在不同学科，缺乏统一视角，妨碍了跨领域方法的迁移与理论整合；因此提出一个抽象的统一框架以揭示共同原理并促进方法复用。

Method: 提出并发展了“叙事”（narratives）理论——一个抽象框架，用以统一描述任意数学类型的时变对象，并通过三个小节（vignettes）示例化该视角的应用：1）研究在不同时域表示之间切换时的信息损失；2）系统性地将时变数据分解为简单片段并提取描述其结构复杂性的不可变对象；3）将该框架应用于控制论，建模具有切换通信拓扑的多智能体系统。

Result: 给出一个概念性且可操作的抽象框架（叙事理论），并通过三个具体方向的论述展示其可能产出的理论问题与应用场景：表示切换下的信息损失分析、结构分解与复杂性不变量、以及切换通信拓扑下的多智能体建模。主要成果是提供了一个组织研究问题的通用视角，而非单一技术突破。

Conclusion: 抽象视角能够把分散的时变问题与方法串联起来，提供问题导向的研究路线图；后续需要形式化定义、严格性质、可计算的不变量与算法、以及更多实证/数值案例以验证并扩展该框架的适用性。

Abstract: Modern science and engineering increasingly rely on time-varying data, yet the mathematical tools used to model temporal phenomena are often developed within separate disciplines, obscuring common principles and limiting the transfer of ideas across fields. This chapter presents the theory of narratives, an abstract framework for time-varying objects of any mathematical kind that supports both theoretical investigations and applications. To illustrate this perspective, the chapter develops three vignettes, each illustrating a different research direction. The first addresses a general concern: What information loss can occur when switching between different representations of temporal data? The second concerns structural and algorithmic approaches: How can we systematically decompose time-varying data into simple pieces and obtain invariants describing its structural complexity? The third is an application to control theory: How can we model multi-agent systems with switching communication topologies? More important than any individual vignette, the central message of this invitation is that a suitable abstract perspective can organize and guide research across remarkably diverse mathematical and scientific domains.

</details>


### [238] [Everything in Moderation: Per-Domain Coverage Optima and Alignment-Resistant Domain Gaps in Multi-Domain Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09081)
*Yunpeng Xu,Kun Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在中期训练阶段对各领域数据的配额会显著影响后续表现：每个领域存在一个“适中覆盖率”（约10%–40%）的内点最优。一次固定预算的对齐（SFT）虽能总体提升但无法弥合领域间差距。零覆盖会导致性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验现实中按可得性而非设计分配中期训练数据的常态决策是否必要，以及后期的对齐步骤能否弥补早期分配的不足。

Method: 在Qwen3-8B-Base（含4B复刻）与五个语义规则互斥的KOR-Bench领域上，训练了覆盖五域单纯形的30个配额（24用于拟合、6用于验证），每个配置5个随机种子。比较中期训练前后以及固定预算SFT（与均匀控制）的影响，使用二次内点检验与置换检验评估统计显著性。

Result: 1) 每域有内点最优，峰值在9.9%–35.1%之间；二次内点置换检验P≈0.01。2) 固定预算SFT平均提升但不能按5%阈值或多数对消中期差距（几乎未桥接），置换空值显著（P<0.001）。3) 零覆盖造成中期训练时精度崩溃，并与一般漂移混合；θ*分配在全流程提升最大但显著性边缘。

Conclusion: 中期训练的数据组成不是可后补的细节：合理分配（避免零）在合理的中等覆盖率内能获得最佳单域效果，而后续对齐无法完全补偿不当的中期配额。

Abstract: Mid-training, the stage between pre-training and alignment, is where a model's per-domain data composition is typically set by data availability rather than principled design. We ask what that decision buys, and whether a later alignment pass can undo it. In a controlled logical-reasoning setting (Qwen3-8B-Base, with a 4B replication; five semantically rule-disjoint KOR-Bench domains) we train 30 allocations spanning the five-domain simplex, 24 sweep configurations plus six withheld from the fit, at five seeds each. Three findings emerge. First, every domain has an interior coverage optimum: the moderate band ($10\%$-$40\%$) is best for all five domains, and a calibrated permutation test for quadratic interiority gives $P\approx0.010$; the fitted mid-training-only curves, with 8B peaks between $9.9\%$ and $35.1\%$, reproduce for curve shape but not peak location. Second, the gaps survive a fixed-budget alignment pass: compensatory SFT raises 116/120 cells (mean $+4.32\%$) yet bridges $0/240$ pairs at a $5\%$ threshold and $30/240$ at a $10\%$ ratio, an equal-budget uniform control behaves almost identically, and a permutation null would bridge $13.8\pm3.3$ and $77.9\pm8.5$ pairs ($P<0.001$). Third, zero coverage collapses mid-training-only accuracy, though a FineWeb-Edu-only control shows the collapse is commingled with generic drift. An exploratory $θ^*$ allocation attains the largest full-pipeline gain ($+4.36\%$ vs. $+0.80\%$/$+0.64\%$\,pp) but is marginal under Welch test.

</details>


### [239] [The Surprising Effectiveness of Approximate Value Iteration in Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09094)
*Raphael Boige,Amine Boumaza,Bruno Scherrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A minimal self-play Approximate Value Iteration (AVI) with ground-truth evaluation is tested on Connect Four, Hex(7x7) and synthetic games. AVI learns more accurate value estimates than AlphaZero and its one-step-lookahead greedy policies are competitive with MCTS-based policies while costing far less compute; preliminary Othello and Go(9x9) results are encouraging. The paper argues simpler algorithms deserve renewed attention given modern deep learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS-based self-play (e.g. AlphaZero) achieves strong results but incurs high computational overhead. The study asks whether simpler dynamic-programming-like methods augmented with modern function approximation can be competitive at moderate scale and lower cost.

Method: Implement a minimal self-play AVI loop using neural value approximators; use ground-truth oracles to compute exact value targets; train value networks on self-play data; form greedy one-step-lookahead policies from the learned values; compare value accuracy, policy strength and compute costs against AlphaZero-style MCTS baselines across Connect Four, Hex(7x7) and synthetic tasks, plus preliminary Othello and Go(9x9) runs.

Result: AVI produced more accurate value estimates than AlphaZero’s learned values; one-step greedy policies were competitive with MCTS policies at substantially reduced training and inference cost. Preliminary experiments indicate stable AVI training on larger boards (Othello, Go9x9). Limitations include dependence on exact evaluation oracles and tests mainly on moderately sized games.

Conclusion: AVI is a surprisingly strong, computationally cheap alternative to MCTS-based self-play in moderate-size perfect-information games: it yields more accurate value functions, competitive greedy policies, and stable scaling to larger board sizes, though validity beyond oracle-assisted training and very large games remains to be established.

Abstract: Combining search with function approximation has driven major advances in game-playing programs, making self-play algorithms more competitive than ever. Still, the computational overhead of the most popular methods, based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), can be substantial. In this work, we investigate whether simpler methods remain competitive in non-trivial, moderately sized games such as Connect Four, Hex(7x7) and synthetic games. We train a minimal self-play implementation of Approximate Value Iteration (AVI) and use ground-truth oracles for exact evaluation. Contrary to expectations, our results demonstrate the surprising effectiveness of AVI: it learns more accurate value functions than those learned by AlphaZero, while its one-step-lookahead greedy policies remain competitive with MCTS-based policies at substantially lower training and inference costs. Preliminary experiments on Othello and Go(9x9) show that AVI trains stably on larger games and learns effective value functions. These findings suggest that the success of MCTS-based methods may have eclipsed simpler approaches that have become increasingly practical with modern deep-learning tools.

</details>


### [240] [SAEScientist-Bench: Can AI Agents Conduct Autonomous SAE Interpretability Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09113)
*Yuqiao Tan,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAEScientist-Bench用于评估代理利用SAE进行机械可解释性发现；代理表现出部分发现能力但整体不及专家，尤其在因果操控与测量理解上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进研究侧重训练自动化，但缺少用于理解模型内部学习内容与保证对齐的事后监测与审计。机械可解释性工具（尤其是SAE）可分离出可解释特征，便于巡检与引导，因此需要评估AI代理能否作为‘科学家’使用这些工具进行自主机制发现。

Method: 提出SAEScientist-Bench：给定目标概念，代理使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）工具在Gemma-2-9B-IT的131K+特征词典中搜索最优特征。代理通过设计对比探针并与NeurONpedia的人工专家参考特征对比，按激活排名、在对比文本上的概念选择性和因果操控能力评估发现效果。实验涵盖10种代理配置和20个任务。

Result: 前沿代理能在某些维度（如区分目标概念与对比控制）接近专家，但总体落后专家基线，特别在因果生成操控上差距明显。分析显示代理能构建有辨别力的对比，但常误读实验测量结果，表明自主闭环AI研发尚需改进。

Conclusion: Agents equipped with SAE tools can perform measurable mechanistic discoveries but do not reach expert-level performance, especially on causal steering; they often misinterpret experimental measurements despite designing informative contrasts.

Abstract: While research on recursive self-improvement (RSI) has predominantly automated model training pipelines, reliable autonomous development demands a missing pillar: post-hoc monitoring and auditing to understand what models learn and ensure safe alignment. Mechanistic interpretability tools are essential to bridge this gap, among which Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) serve as a cornerstone by isolating interpretable features for model inspection and steering. In this paper, we introduce SAEScientist-Bench to evaluate whether AI agents can act as scientists utilizing SAE tools for autonomous mechanistic discovery. Given a target concept, an agent designs contrastive probes and navigates a Gemma Scope dictionary of 131K+ features in Gemma-2-9B-IT to discover the optimal feature, evaluated against curated expert reference features anchored on Neuronpedia across activation rank, concept selectivity on contrastive texts, and causal steering. Across 10 agent configurations and 20 tasks, frontier agents demonstrate genuine discovery capabilities and lead different evaluation dimensions, but remain well behind the expert baseline, approaching expert levels on separating target concepts from contrastive controls while lagging substantially in causal generation steering. Further analysis reveals that although agents can design contrasts to rule out spurious candidates, they frequently misinterpret experimental measurements. These results establish experimental model understanding as a measurable capability for closed-loop autonomous AI R&D. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trae1ounG/SAEScientist.

</details>


### [241] [A Generalization of Amari's Bayesian Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09126)
*Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Thomas Möllenhoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把Amari的信息几何式贝叶斯对偶性等价地解释为贝叶斯更新的凸对偶结构，并给出可推广的数学框架，利于现代AI中的推断与优化算法设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Amari关于贝叶斯对偶性的工作，并将其与贝叶斯法则的凸对偶性建立联系，以得到更普适的理论框架并探索其在现代人工智能中的影响与应用。

Method: 基于凸分析与信息几何的理论推导：使用凸共轭、Bregman发散与Fenchel对偶理论，把贝叶斯更新表述为原-对偶问题或投影问题，证明在更一般的族（超出典型的指数族）和更一般的发散度下对偶关系仍然成立，并讨论与自然梯度、镜像映射及变分优化的联系。

Result: 将Amari的贝叶斯对偶性形式化为贝叶斯法则的一种凸对偶性；提出并证明了该对偶性的推广版本（在更广泛的分布和发散度下成立）；指出这种对偶性在变分推断、镜像下降/自然梯度、鲁棒贝叶斯和信息几何建模中的潜在应用。

Conclusion: 本文把信息几何中的贝叶斯对偶性与凸分析中的对偶性桥接起来，形成一个统一视角，既加深了对贝叶斯更新的几何/凸结构的理解，也为设计基于对偶性的推断与优化方法提供理论依据。

Abstract: Amari's contributions to information geometry and machine learning are well known. Here, we revisit Amari's work on Bayesian duality which has not received as much attention. We connect Amari's Bayesian duality to a convex duality of Bayes' rule. Using this connection, we present a generalization of Amari's Bayesian duality and discuss its relevance for modern artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [242] [Co-Evolving Harnesses and Models: On-Policy Correction Helps Weaker Models Catch Up Where Imitation Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09134)
*Zhou Yu,Bin Bi,Shiva Kumar Pentyala,Shubham Mehrotra,Sougata Chaudhuri,Shilpa Bhagavath,Zeyuan Chen,Ran Xu,Phil Mui,James Zhu,Sitaram Asur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 演化harness可提升弱模型的可用性，但直接离线模仿专家轨迹会破坏模型与harness的适配。通过在模型自身rollout上定位并只修正失败回合（自动化专家更正）可保留规划风格并实现兼容的低成本共演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将自动化的“agent harness”（系统提示、工具集、执行挂钩与上下文管理支架）演化与轻量级微调（对模型权重的适配）有效结合，从而使弱模型在特定领域任务上以低成本获得接近前沿模型的表现；并理解两者交互是否会互相冲突。

Method: 在七个企业任务上先用弱模型演化harness；验证更强模型对该harness的利用情况；进行对照实验：用专家在演化后harness下的完整轨迹做离线MLE微调并评估，分析失败机理（模仿转移知识但破坏模型-harness配合）；设计并实现一个元层MLE代理，在线回放弱模型的rollout以定位第一个/局部失败回合，向专家请求仅对该回合的重写并将此局部修正合入训练/执行，比较性能并报告改进。

Result: 在七个企业级任务上：1) 用弱模型演化出的harness通常能被更强的专家更有效地利用；2) 直接用专家在演化后harness下的完整轨迹去训练弱模型（离线模仿）会导致性能在所有任务上回退（4–30分）；3) 失败原因是模仿改变了弱模型的规划风格，使其采用专家的策略但缺乏执行能力，破坏了与原有harness的匹配；4) 提出一种基于在策略执行时定位失败回合并仅请求专家重写该回合的在线专家更正流水线（由元层MLE代理自动化），能保留模型的规划风格并结合harness演化与模型适配的优点。

Conclusion: 揭示了harness演化与权重更新间的一类兼容性冲突，并给出一种兼容性保持的实用方案：在策略级别保留弱模型的规划风格，仅用局部、按需的专家写入以修正执行缺陷，从而实现经济有效的领域共进化。

Abstract: Agent harnesses (the system prompt, tool set, execution hooks, and context-management scaffolding around a model) are a critical determinant of agentic task success. Automated harness evolution can enable smaller models to perform well on domain-specific tasks at a fraction of frontier-model cost. Since both the harness and model weights shape behavior, we ask how harness evolution and lightweight fine-tuning should be combined. Across seven enterprise agent tasks, we first evolve a harness with the weaker model, then find that a stronger expert often uses it more effectively, suggesting expert supervision could close the remaining gap. However, training the weaker model on the expert's complete trajectories under the evolved harness backfires: performance regresses on all seven tasks by 4 to 30 points across Qwen3-Coder and Gemma 4, even though the same procedure helps under the unevolved harness. Our analysis shows that imitation transfers knowledge and increases scaffold usage, but disrupts model-harness fit: the weaker model adopts the expert's planning strategy without the competence to execute it and no longer matches the harness evolved around its native planning style. We therefore develop an on-policy expert-correction pipeline, automated by a meta-level MLE agent, that localizes the failing turn in the weaker model's own rollout and asks the expert to rewrite only that turn. This preserves the model's planning style and combines the gains of harness evolution and model adaptation. Our results identify and resolve a source of contention between harness and weight updates, yielding a compatibility-preserving recipe for economical co-evolution on domain-specific enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [243] [A Data-Driven Framework for Identifying and Prioritizing RPA Opportunities in Healthcare Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09137)
*Maria Alejandra Gomez,Juan Manuel Castillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四模块、数据驱动的RPA决策框架：流程分类、可自动化性优先级（基于AHP）、工具/层级选型、以及ROI量化。用合成组合和蒙特卡洛模拟检验了鲁棒性与投资回报，并附带Python实现，但未在真实医院数据上校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPA项目在医院环境中高失败率来自非系统化的流程选择与缺乏可重复的优先级、工具匹配和财务预估方法；动机是提供一套可复现的端到端决策框架以提高RPA投资成功率与资源配置效率。

Method: 构建流程分类表（20类，5价值流），用AHP矩阵含一致性检验计算Automation Suitability Index，基于复杂度/集成/合规性特征匹配工具层级，ROI模块计算劳动节省、错误成本回避、回收期与NPV；对合成组合做排序、蒙特卡洛鲁棒性检验与预算约束的组合优化。

Result: 在合成样本上：12/20流程通过优先级门槛；权重扰动±20%下排序稳健（Spearman 0.83，前5保持97.7%）；识别4个“关键风险”流程；预算从$400K→$1.03M时组合边际NPV递减；组合NPV第5百分位仍为正；附带Python代码，讨论HIPAA治理与进一步实证研究议程。

Conclusion: 框架在概念上完整且方法学合理，展示了对流程优先级、技术选型与财务预测的可重复决策路径；主要限制在于缺乏以真实医院数据为基础的实证校准与实施复杂性考量。

Abstract: Robotic Process Automation (RPA) is widely used to reduce administrative burden in United States hospitals, yet an estimated 30-50% of RPA initiatives underperform because processes are selected informally, without a repeatable method to catalogue candidates, prioritize them, match each to an automation tier -- a Python bot, an open-source orchestrator such as n8n, or an enterprise platform such as UiPath -- and forecast financial return before committing resources. We propose a four-module, data-driven framework unifying these decisions: a Process Taxonomy of twenty recurring hospital processes across five value streams; a Prioritization module deriving an Automation Suitability Index from an Analytic Hierarchy Process matrix with an explicit consistency check; a Tool-Tier Selection module recommending the least-cost technology sufficient for a process complexity, integration, and compliance profile; and a Return-on-Investment module quantifying labor savings, error-cost avoidance, payback, and net present value. Applied to a synthetic portfolio spanning all twenty processes, plus a reference data-flow architecture linking it to hospital EHR/payer/ERP systems: 12 of 20 clear the prioritization threshold; the ranking is robust to +/-20% weight perturbation (Spearman correlation 0.83, top-5 set preserved 97.7%, 2,000 Monte Carlo trials); an Automation Risk Index flags four qualifying processes as Critical risk; a budget-constrained portfolio optimization shows diminishing marginal NPV as spend scales from $400K to $1.03M; and a second Monte Carlo analysis shows portfolio NPV stays positive at its 5th percentile. The framework is a conceptual synthesis of the literature rather than an instrument calibrated on primary hospital data; we discuss HIPAA governance and a research agenda for empirical validation. A supplementary Python implementation accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [244] [Procedural Graphs: Self-Evolving Execution Structures for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09153)
*Yuxing Lu,Yicheng Chen,Shanchan Wu,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了“程序图”（Procedural Graph），把程序性知识组织为（procedure, relation, procedure）三元组；在每步决策中定位活动节点并用“引导模型”把周边子图翻译成步骤级情境引导以偏置求解器；并用LLM修正器对成功/失败轨迹进行对比编辑，自我演化图结构。该方法在多数据集、多任务、多LLM下优于基于记忆的基线，并能超越或修复人工设计的图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的代理通过无约束文本生成选择动作，随着轨迹增长，容易迷失目标、错序调用工具或重复无效操作；需要一种显式的程序性知识表示和可自我修正的机制以维持长期规划质量。

Method: 用图结构表示步骤与步骤之间的关系；在推理时定位当前活跃节点，使用一个引导模型将邻域子图转为对下一步操作的软性偏置（不直接下命令）；用LLM修正器比较失败与成功轨迹，提出图的拓扑与属性编辑，通过验证集保留改善或不降低性能的编辑，并记录被拒绝的编辑作为负例以避免重复。

Result: 从最小骨架开始的闭环能够构建相当或优于人工设计的程序图；在多种数据集与LLM上，相较于记忆基线普遍提升表现；自我演化进一步提高性能并能修复有缺陷的专家先验。

Conclusion: 程序图为长时程、工具驱动的LLM代理提供了结构化、可演化的程序性记忆，使代理在长轨迹上更稳定、有序且更少重复，且自我演化能自动改进或修复专家先验。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents that plan over long horizons and act through external tools. Most agents select actions through unconstrained generation over an accumulating history, leaving implicit the procedural knowledge of what to do, in what order, and under which conditions. As trajectories lengthen, agents can lose track of their objectives, invoke tools out of order, and repeat unproductive actions. We introduce the Procedural Graph: just as a knowledge graph organizes factual knowledge into (entity, relation, entity) triplets for what-is questions, a Procedural Graph organizes procedural knowledge into (procedure, relation, procedure) triplets for what-to-do questions. At each decision step, the framework localizes the agent's active node, and a guidance model translates the surrounding subgraph into step-level situational guidance that biases the solver's next action without dictating it. The graph is self-evolving: an LLM refiner contrasts failed trajectories with successful ones and edits the graph's topology and attributes, committing edits that preserve or improve held-out validation performance while retaining rejected ones to discourage repetition. Starting from a minimal skeleton, the loop builds graphs that match or surpass hand-designed ones. It can also repair a flawed expert prior. Across multiple datasets, task types, and LLMs, the Procedural Graph delivers consistent gains over memory-based baselines, and self-evolution further improves performance without manual engineering.

</details>
